TL;DR: AdaptivePath是一个主动感知框架，将全切片病理图像（WSI）的证据获取建模为序列决策问题。Navigator通过学习病理医生审查的标签来驱动异常检测导航，以分层方式从低到高放大倍数获取稀疏观察；Morphology Interpreter将观察转换为问题条件证据，Deliberator跨放大倍数评估证据并修订中间答案，Arbiter整合生成最终答案。在WSI和区域病理VQA基准上达到最先进零样本性能，癌症亚型分类准确率达80.14%，盲法诊断实用性研究中病理医生使用其观察序列达到82.9%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WSI方法存在两大不足：一是将密集采样补丁压缩为全局表示，削弱了预测与形态学之间的关联；二是使用启发式区域选择的预训练视觉-语言模型，缺乏病理学训练的观察策略。这些方法无法实现跨越空间尺度的稀疏诊断证据识别的精准决策。

Method: AdaptivePath由三个核心组件构成：（1）Navigator——学习与问题无关的异常驱动导航策略，通过交替表示学习和近端策略优化（PPO）进行训练，再通过几何和外观一致性目标微调以稳定聚焦轨迹；（2）Morphology Interpreter——将获取的观察转换为问题条件的证据表示；（3）Deliberator——跨放大倍数评估证据并修订中间答案；（4）Arbiter——整合整个推理历史生成最终答案。推理时在有限的ROI预算下从低到高放大倍数层级化获取稀疏观察。

Result: 在WSI和区域病理VQA基准上取得最先进的零样本性能；在六个TCGA队列的癌症亚型分类中达到80.14%准确率；在盲法诊断实用性研究中，使用AdaptivePath选择观察序列的病理医生达到82.9%准确率。

Conclusion: 学习到的主动感知机制能够实现对千兆像素病理切片的有效且可追踪的视觉推理，在性能上超越了现有方法，同时通过可解释的观察序列为临床诊断提供了实用支持。

Abstract: Whole-slide visual reasoning requires identifying sparse diagnostic evidence in gigapixel pathology slides and integrating observations across spatial scales. Existing WSI methods either compress densely sampled patches into global representations or use pretrained vision-language models with heuristic region selection, weakening links between predictions and morphology or lacking pathology-trained observation policies. We present AdaptivePath, an active-perception framework that formulates WSI evidence acquisition as sequential decision making. The Navigator learns question-agnostic abnormality-driven navigation from pathologist-reviewed labels to select observation locations and spatial extents, avoiding costly question-specific trajectory annotations. We train this policy through alternating representation learning and proximal policy optimization, followed by fine-tuning with geometric and appearance consistency objectives to stabilize focus trajectories. During inference, the Navigator hierarchically acquires sparse observations from low to high magnification under a limited ROI budget. A Morphology Interpreter converts observations into question-conditioned evidence, while the Deliberator evaluates evidence and revises intermediate answers across magnifications. The Arbiter integrates deliberation history to produce final answers. AdaptivePath achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on WSI and region pathology VQA benchmarks and reaches 80.14% accuracy for cancer subtype classification across six TCGA cohorts. In a blinded diagnostic-utility study, pathologists using AdaptivePath-selected observation sequences achieve 82.9% accuracy. These results demonstrate that learned active perception enables effective and traceable visual reasoning over gigapixel pathology slides.

</details>


### [125] [JSGS: JPEG State-Guided Supervision for 3D Gaussian Splatting from Mixed-Quality Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08659)
*Jinhua Cui,Anhong Wang,Kai Hu,Donghan Bu,Peihao Li,Tammam Tillo,Hao Jing,Shiao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JSGS提出了一种针对混合质量JPEG图像下的3D高斯泼溅优化方法，利用JPEG文件中的量化表构建视图特定的观测算子，在不同频带分配监督权重，并引导高斯控制器正则化不一致区域中的小图元，在七个场景和三种混合质量调度下均取得最优LPIPS和SSIM指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准3DGS假设输入图像能忠实采样场景辐射度，但混合质量的JPEG图像存在压缩伪影（如块效应和振铃效应），这些伪影会污染跨视图共享高斯的更新，导致重建质量下降。

Method: JSGS利用每个JPEG文件中存储的亮度和色度量化表构建视图特定JPEG观测算子，对渲染视图进行编解码以实现与解码输入图像的域匹配比较。亮度量化表在中频带提供连续权重，低频带损失锚定粗结构，加权中频损失重新分配DCT坐标间的监督；块不一致性引导高斯控制器正则化不一致区域中高不透明度的小图元。

Result: 在七个场景和三种混合质量调度下，JSGS在所有调度下均获得最低平均LPIPS和最高平均SSIM，同时渲染速度约为150 FPS。

Conclusion: 通过JPEG量化表驱动的频带感知监督策略，JSGS有效缓解了混合质量JPEG图像对3DGS重建的影响，在质量指标和渲染效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Standard 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) assumes that every input image faithfully samples scene radiance. However, mixed-quality JPEG images violate this assumption because compression-induced blocking and ringing artifacts can corrupt updates to Gaussians shared across views. To address this problem, we propose JPEG State-Guided Supervision for 3D Gaussian Splatting from Mixed-Quality Views (JSGS). JSGS uses luminance and chrominance quantization tables stored in each JPEG file to construct a view-specific JPEG observation operator. This operator encodes and decodes each rendered view for domain-matched comparison with the corresponding decoded input image. The luminance quantization table supplies continuous weights within a fixed middle frequency band. A loss in the low frequency band anchors coarse structure, while the weighted middle frequency loss redistributes supervision among the selected DCT coordinates. The resulting block disagreement also guides the Gaussian Controller to regularize small primitives with high opacity in disagreement regions. Across seven scenes and three mixed-quality schedules, JSGS achieves the lowest mean LPIPS and the highest mean SSIM under every schedule while rendering at approximately 150 FPS. Code: https://github.com/Jayden-Cui/JSGS.

</details>


### [126] [Degradation-Guided Underwater Image Restoration with Task-Oriented Latent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08661)
*Xu Zhang,Xuhui Cao,Kangzhe Yuan,Laibin Chang,Yichu Xu,Shi Chen,Huan Zhang,Yong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PROTEUS是一种水下图像复原方法，从特征级和表征级两个互补角度处理退化信息：特征级通过引导动态特征调制模块利用空间变化的退化线索自适应处理特征；表征级通过任务导向的潜在控制器学习结构化控制码，对跳跃连接特征进行通道级调制，在五个配对和四个非参考水下基准上取得了竞争性的复原性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水下图像复原方法忽视了退化信息的双重角色：一方面其空间和光谱线索可引导自适应复原，另一方面退化纠缠特征在解码阶段可能未经明确调控而被传播。现有方法要么未充分利用退化线索，要么直接通过跳跃连接前向传递编码器特征，缺乏对退化信息的系统利用。

Method: 提出PROTEUS框架，包含两个互补模块：(1) 特征层面：引导动态特征调制模块（GDFMB）利用空间变化的退化线索在网络的各个阶段自适应调整特征处理；(2) 表征层面：任务导向的潜在控制器（TOLC）在判别性正则化下学习结构化控制码，用于对跳跃连接特征进行通道级调制，且不要求控制码构成度量意义上的更干净嵌入。

Result: 在五个配对基准和四个非参考水下图像基准上的广泛实验表明，PROTEUS取得了具有高度竞争力的复原性能，并在复原质量与计算成本之间实现了良好平衡。

Conclusion: 通过耦合退化引导的特征适应与任务导向的潜在控制，PROTEUS有效解决了水下图像复原中退化信息双重角色的问题，实现了性能与效率的兼顾。

Abstract: Degradation information in underwater images plays a dual role: its spatial and spectral cues can guide adaptive restoration, while degradation-entangled features may be propagated without explicit regulation during decoding. Existing methods largely overlook this dual role, either underexploiting degradation cues or directly forwarding encoder features through skip connections. To address this issue, we propose PROTEUS, which couples degradation-guided feature adaptation with task?oriented latent control. PROTEUS tackles this problem from two complementary perspectives. At the feature level, the Guided Dynamic Feature Modulation Block exploits spatially varying degradation cues to adapt feature processing across network stages. At the representation level, the task-oriented latent controller learns a structured control code under discriminative regularisation and uses it for channel-wise modulation of skip features, without requiring the code to form a metrically cleaner embedding. Extensive experiments on five paired and four non-reference underwater benchmarks demonstrate that PROTEUS achieves highly competitive restoration performance, with a favourable balance between restoration quality and computational cost.

</details>


### [127] [FiRe: Fixed-Noise Refinement for Visual Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08664)
*Yan Zeng,Changlu Guo,Oskar Kristoffersen,Anders Nymark Christensen,Morten Rieger Hannemose,Anders Bjorholm Dahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FiRe（Fixed-noise Refinement）框架，将输入映射到固定噪声水平并迭代精炼该噪声状态，替代传统DDPM方法冗长的反向去噪轨迹，实现更高效、更高质量的视觉反事实解释生成。相比最强基线，推理速度提升约3倍，FLOPs减少8倍，同时保持或超越现有反事实质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于DDPM的视觉反事实解释方法沿长反向去噪轨迹进行分类器引导编辑，噪声水平不断变化导致语义可编辑性与空间可控性难以平衡；同时可编辑状态是有噪声的，而目标分类器在干净图像上训练，导致需要昂贵的递归去噪或低质量的一步估计来获得分类器可用的干净图像。

Method: FiRe将输入映射到固定噪声水平，在该水平上迭代精炼噪声状态。具体包含三个关键组件：(1) 将Pixel Mean Flow适配到视觉反事实解释场景，从噪声状态直接预测干净图像供分类器引导；(2) 引入三个FiRe特有控制机制——动态双掩码策略（决定编辑累积位置）、自适应引导（决定哪些变化可见）以及早停机制（决定精炼何时终止）。

Result: 在三个数据集的五个任务上，FiRe相比最强近期基线，在线推理速度提升约3倍，FLOPs减少8倍，同时获得可比或最先进的反事实质量。

Conclusion: FiRe通过固定噪声精炼策略有效解决了DDPM方法中噪声水平变化带来的可编辑性与空间控制矛盾，以及噪声状态与干净分类器之间的不匹配问题，在保证反事实质量的同时显著提升了计算效率。

Abstract: Visual counterfactual explanations aim to change classifier decisions through realistic and localized edits while preserving decision-irrelevant content. Existing DDPM-based methods typically perform classifier-guided editing along a long reverse denoising trajectory. The changing noise levels make semantic editability and spatial control difficult to balance, and the editable state is noisy, whereas the target classifier is trained on clean images. As a result, these methods require either costly recursive denoising or low-quality one-step estimates to obtain classifier-facing clean images. We propose FiRe, a Fixed-noise Refinement framework for visual counterfactual explanations. Rather than following a reverse denoising trajectory, FiRe maps the input to a fixed noise level and iteratively refines the noisy state at that level. To provide clean images for classifier guidance, FiRe first adapts Pixel Mean Flow to visual counterfactual explanation, enabling direct clean-image prediction from noisy states. To make fixed-noise refinement produce minimal and localized counterfactual edits, FiRe introduces three FiRe-specific controls: a dynamic dual-mask strategy, adaptive guidance, and early stopping, which determine where edits accumulate, which changes become visible, and when refinement stops. Experiments on five tasks across three datasets show that, compared with the strongest recent baseline, FiRe achieves about 3$\times$ faster online inference and 8$\times$ fewer FLOPs while obtaining comparable or state-of-the-art counterfactual quality.

</details>


### [128] [UniSpace: Unified Visual Representation and Scalable Multimodal Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08676)
*Jinbo Yan,Limeng Qiao,Jie Qin,Junyan He,Feize Wu,Guanglu Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Patch Reparameterization方法，对预训练语义ViT的patch嵌入进行重构感知的重新参数化，在不改变语义通路的前提下保留细粒度视觉信息，从而构建统一的视觉表示空间（UniSpace），使理解、生成和编辑三种任务可以在同一个8B MoTE模型中完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义视觉编码器在多模态理解和图像生成中扮演核心角色，但其最终token会丢失细粒度视觉细节，导致像素重建效果差，限制了在图像生成和编辑等重建敏感任务中的应用。作者希望探索是否可以在一个统一的视觉表示空间中同时实现理解、生成和编辑。

Method: 首先发现语义ViT的冻结Transformer块并非本质无法保留视觉细节，问题出在原始patch参数化将表示推向语义抽象。基于此提出Patch Reparameterization，保留原有语义通路的同时，增加一个重构感知的patch嵌入，为相同的冻结ViT块提供细粒度视觉信息，进而扩展为8B参数的MoTE（Mixture-of-Transformer-Experts）模型UniSpace。

Result: 统一表示在保留多模态理解能力的同时，实现了高保真图像重建，并取得了良好的重建-生成权衡。系统级评估表明该方法支持实际的文生图和指令式图像编辑任务，证明重新参数化的预训练ViT可以作为可扩展多模态建模的统一视觉接口。

Conclusion: 通过patch重新参数化，预训练语义ViT可以被赋予像素级重建能力，从而在不牺牲理解能力的前提下统一理解、生成和编辑任务，为构建统一的视觉表示空间提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Semantic vision encoders have become a central visual interface for multimodal understanding and semantic conditioning in image generation. However, their final tokens discard fine-grained visual details, leading to poor pixel reconstruction and limiting their use in reconstruction-sensitive tasks such as image generation and editing. In this work, we ask whether understanding, generation, and editing can be modeled in a single visual representation space built from a pretrained semantic ViT. We show that the frozen Transformer blocks of a semantic ViT are not intrinsically unable to preserve visual details. Instead, the original patch parameterization drives the representation toward semantic abstraction, making fine-grained information difficult to recover from the final tokens. Based on this observation, we introduce \emph{Patch Reparameterization}, which preserves the original semantic pathway while adding a reconstruction-aware patch embedding that provides fine-grained visual information to the same frozen ViT blocks. The resulting unified representation preserves multimodal understanding while enabling high-fidelity image reconstruction and a favorable reconstruction--generation trade-off. We further scale this representation into \emph{UniSpace}, an 8B Mixture-of-Transformer-Experts model that performs understanding, generation, and editing in the same visual space without a separate VAE pathway. System-level evaluations demonstrate practical text-to-image generation and instruction-based image editing, showing that a reparameterized pretrained ViT can serve as a unified visual interface for scalable multimodal modeling.

</details>


### [129] [Semi-Dense Matching Uncertainty Is Not Just Local Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08685)
*Khoa Hoang,Hoang-Tuan Nguyen,Huong Ninh,Hai Tran,Long Q. Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个轻量级的事后整体不确定性估计框架（仅9个可学习参数），通过两分量校准拉普拉斯混合模型显式捕获局部细化噪声和粗分配失败的长尾分布，并引入CoRe几何重拟合模块利用粗分配成功的后验概率作为软对应权重，在多种预训练匹配器和鲁棒估计器上持续提升下游几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半稠密匹配方法在粗到细范式下虽能平衡性能与计算成本，但缺乏良好量化的不确定性估计，粗分配灾难性失败被忽略，导致误差分布截断、几何估计严重误判。

Method: 提出两分量校准拉普拉斯混合模型（仅9个可学习参数）来同时捕获局部细化噪声和粗分配失败的长尾；引入Coarse-success posterior Refit (CoRe)方法，利用粗分配成功的后验概率作为软对应权重进行几何重拟合。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在多种预训练匹配器和鲁棒估计器上一致提升下游几何精度，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 该轻量级事后不确定性估计框架能有效量化粗分配失败带来的不确定性，以极低开销提升几何估计精度，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reliable semi-dense matching is essential for modern geometric vision systems. Designed under a coarse-to-fine paradigm, it achieves an optimal balance between performance and computational cost. However, existing methods often struggle to provide well-quantified uncertainties, where catastrophic coarse-assignment failures are ignored, leading to truncated error distributions and severely misjudged geometric estimations. In this paper, we propose a lightweight, post-hoc overall uncertainty estimation framework that introduces a two-component calibrated Laplace mixture model with only 9 learnable parameters. The objective is to explicitly capture both the sharp local refinement noise and the broader tail of coarse-assignment failures. We introduce the Coarse-success posterior Refit (CoRe) method, a geometric refitting module that utilizes the posterior probability of coarse-assignment success as soft correspondence weights. Extensive experiments show that our method consistently improves downstream geometric accuracy across various pretrained-only matchers and robust estimators with minimal computational overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/khoavpt/Probabilistic-matching.

</details>


### [130] [CUPA-T2*: Covariance-Aware Uncertainty Propagation and Alignment for T2* Mapping in Accelerated MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08693)
*Gideon N. L. Rouwendaal,Natascha Niessen,Hannah Eichhorn,Dirk H. J. Poot,Christine Preibisch,Julia A. Schnabel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CUPA-T2*框架，通过将随机蒙特卡洛dropout重建的逐体素回波间不确定性显式传播到T2*拟合中，实现高加速磁共振T2*定量成像。该方法使用异方差MLP和基于相关性的正则化器，在加速脑部MRI数据上展现出竞争性的拟合性能，并改善了高加速下的白质性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量T2*图谱在生物标志物发现方面潜力巨大，但扫描时间过长限制了其临床应用。现有加速方法通过k空间欠采样结合学习重建，但重建伪影和噪声会传播到后续T2*拟合中，降低准确性。因此需要一个显式处理不确定性传播的框架。

Method: 提出CUPA-T2*框架：(1) 从随机蒙特卡洛dropout重建中获取逐体素回波间不确定性；(2) 通过协方差感知采样将这些不确定性传播到T2*拟合；(3) 使用异方差MLP进行T2*拟合；(4) 引入基于相关性的正则化器，鼓励预测方差与重建不确定性之间的对齐。

Result: 在加速脑部MRI数据上的实验显示：CUPA-T2*在整体T2*拟合性能上具有竞争力，在高加速下改善了白质性能。与异方差基线相比，重建不确定性与预测T2*方差之间的对齐显著提高，但同时也揭示出与校准（ECE）和选择性预测性能（AURC）之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: CUPA-T2*实现了重建不确定性感知的T2*拟合，提供逐体素不确定性图谱以支持定量T2*估计的解释，为临床实践中的高加速定量成像提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Quantitative T2* maps have strong potential for biomarker discovery but are limited by long scan times, rendering them impractical in clinical settings. Significant acceleration can be achieved through undersampling in k-space combined with learning-based reconstruction. However, reconstruction artifacts and noise can propagate into downstream T2* fitting, degrading its accuracy. We introduce CUPA-T2*, a framework that explicitly propagates voxel-wise inter-echo uncertainty from stochastic Monte Carlo dropout reconstructions to downstream T2* fitting via covariance-aware sampling. T2* fitting is performed with a heteroscedastic MLP and a correlation-based regularizer that encourages alignment between predicted variance and reconstruction uncertainty. Experiments on accelerated brain MRI data show tissue-dependent behavior: CUPA-T2* achieves competitive overall T2* fitting performance and improves white-matter performance at higher accelerations. Compared with a heteroscedastic baseline, the proposed framework substantially increases alignment between reconstruction uncertainty and predicted T2* variance, while also revealing a trade-off with calibration (ECE) and selective prediction performance (AURC). CUPA-T2* enables reconstruction uncertainty-aware T2* fitting and delivers voxel-wise uncertainty maps to support the interpretation of quantitative T2* estimates.

</details>


### [131] [OccAnyScene: Towards Unified Indoor-Outdoor 3D Occupancy Predictio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08696)
*Junjie Liu,Wanshui Gan,Zitong Dai,Guiping Cao,Yan Li,Ke Chen,Dongmei Jiang,Xiangyuan Lan,Jianguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Cross-Scene 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction新任务，要求单一模型同时处理异构室内外场景（不同相机、空间范围、体素规格和语义体系）。为此提出OccAnyScene框架，基于预训练深度基础模型，采用像素对齐视锥特征聚合和视锥参数化高斯构建，在室内Occ-ScanNet达到59.92% mIoU、室外SurroundOcc-nuScenes达到23.06% mIoU，均创下SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D语义占用预测方法通常针对固定场景类型和占用协议设计，缺乏跨场景（室内外）的通用能力。真实世界中场景类型多样，相机配置、空间范围、体素规格和语义分类体系各异，需要单一模型能够统一处理这些异构场景。核心挑战在于如何在变化的相机配置和场景尺度下，实现度量一致且场景自适应的图像到3D提升映射。

Method: 提出OccAnyScene框架，核心创新包括：(1) 基于预训练深度基础模型构建像素-视锥中心的高斯框架；(2) Pixel-Aligned Frustum Feature Aggregation（像素对齐视锥特征聚合），为每个特征像素构建相机感知的视锥查询；(3) Frustum-Parameterized Gaussian Construction（视锥参数化高斯构建），将每个查询解码为多个高斯，其位置和大小由预测的像素深度及对应视锥几何约束。

Result: OccAnyScene在室内Occ-ScanNet上达到59.92% mIoU，在室外SurroundOcc-nuScenes上达到23.06% mIoU，均刷新了跨场景3D语义占用预测的新SOTA记录。

Conclusion: 通过深度基础模型和视锥几何约束的高斯表征，OccAnyScene实现了单一模型对异构室内外场景的统一3D语义占用预测，验证了跨场景占用预测的可行性，为该任务设立了新的基准。

Abstract: 3D occupancy prediction is fundamental to scene understanding, yet existing 3D semantic occupancy methods are typically specialized to fixed scene types and occupancy protocols. We introduce Cross-Scene 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction, a new task setting which requires a single model to handle heterogeneous indoor and outdoor scenes with varying cameras, spatial ranges, voxel specifications, and semantic taxonomies. This setting poses a fundamental challenge: achieving metric-consistent yet scene-adaptive image-to-3D lifting across varying camera configurations and scene scales. To address this challenge, we propose OccAnyScene, a pixel-frustum-centered Gaussian framework built upon a pretrained depth foundation model. Specifically, the framework employs Pixel-Aligned Frustum Feature Aggregation to construct a camera-aware frustum query for each feature pixel, and Frustum-Parameterized Gaussian Construction to decode each query into multiple Gaussians whose positions and sizes are constrained by the predicted pixel depth and corresponding frustum geometry. OccAnyScene sets new state-of-the-art results, achieving 59.92% mIoU on the indoor Occ-ScanNet and 23.06% mIoU on the outdoor SurroundOcc-nuScenes.

</details>


### [132] [SRE-FER: Regional residual evidence learning for mitigating local evidence dilution in fine-grained facial expression recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08702)
*Jiaye Song,Ruochen Zhang,Yuliang Wang,Jiaqi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SRE-FER框架，通过区域残差证据学习（RERA模块）解决细粒度面部表情识别中局部肌肉线索被全局特征稀释的问题，在三个基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度面部表情识别依赖捕捉区分相邻情绪的细微肌肉线索，但现有方法存在困境：基于检测器的方法依赖脆弱的landmark管线，而直接迁移基础模型（如DINOv3）在传统全局读取下会导致局部证据稀释——早期全局聚合会冲淡稀疏的肌肉信号，造成恐惧/惊讶、悲伤/中性等类别间的持续混淆。

Method: 提出SRE-FER框架，核心模块RERA通过添加零初始化的残差logits来细化类别边界，同时保留骨干网络的全局预测。训练时利用FACS解剖先验提供动作单元（AU）引导，将区域特征导向表情相关区域，推理时无需外部面部管线。可选Full设置进一步路由样本特定的非冗余token。

Result: 在三个基准上取得优异性能：RAF-DB达92.76%，FERPlus达91.32%，AffectNet-7达67.78%，与现有FER方法相比具有高度竞争力。

Conclusion: SRE-FER通过读取级别的区域残差证据学习有效恢复了被全局聚合稀释的局部肌肉证据，在不依赖推理时外部面部管线的情况下实现了细粒度表情识别性能的提升。

Abstract: Fine-grained facial expression recognition (FER) hinges on capturing subtle muscular cues that distinguish adjacent emotions. Yet capturing these cues presents a dilemma. Detector-based methods depend on fragile landmark pipelines, whereas we find that directly transferring foundation models such as DINOv3 under conventional global readouts can cause local evidence dilution: early global aggregation washes out sparse muscular signals and leaves persistent confusion between categories such as fear/surprise and sad/neutral. To recover this evidence, we propose SRE-FER, a readout-level regional residual evidence learning framework. Its core module, RERA, adds zero-initialized residual logits that refine class boundaries while preserving the backbone's global prediction. Training-time action unit (AU) guidance steers regional features toward expression-relevant areas using Facial Action Coding System (FACS)-based anatomical priors, without requiring an external facial pipeline at inference. An optional Full setting further routes sample-specific non-redundant tokens. On three benchmarks, SRE-FER attains 92.76% on RAF-DB, 91.32% on FERPlus, and 67.78% on AffectNet-7, demonstrating highly competitive performance compared to existing FER methods.

</details>


### [133] [Resolution Meets Reduction: Efficient Visual Context for 3D Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08713)
*Jonathan Suprijadi,Raphael Stock,Moritz Langenberg,David Zimmerer,Kim-Celine Kahl,Stefan Denner,Yannick Kirchhoff,Karol Gotkowski,Maximilian Rokuss,Jeremias Traub,Tassilo Wald,Constantin Ulrich,Klaus Maier-Hein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了视觉-语言模型在3D CT全身扫描自动生成放射学报告中的应用，重点研究了视觉token预算在视野、空间分辨率和视觉到语言投影之间的分配策略。作者评估了4种视觉编码器、5种token压缩投影器（最高64倍压缩）和5种指令微调LLM，在CT-RATE和Merlin两个大型CT报告数据集上验证。结果表明，解剖引导的感兴趣区域裁剪是最一致的策略（平均提升+3.7分），而分辨率提升效果高度依赖投影器选择，最佳配置在CT-RATE上达到49.5的临床macro F1，在Merlin上达到49.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在3D CT体积上的应用面临巨大的计算挑战：现代视觉编码器每次扫描可产生数万个视觉token，成为输入给LLM的主要计算瓶颈。虽然视觉到语言投影器可以压缩序列，但可能丢失临床相关信息。因此，如何分配视觉token预算（在视野、空间分辨率和投影方式之间）是一个尚未解决的关键设计问题。

Method: 作者进行了大规模系统评估，包括：4种异构视觉编码器（CNN和ViT）、5种token减少投影器（最高64倍压缩）加上一个非压缩MLP投影器基线、5种指令微调LLM（1.7B-4B参数），在CT-RATE和Merlin两个大规模CT报告数据集上进行实验。在匹配LLM token预算的条件下，比较不同策略组合的临床性能。

Result: 解剖引导的感兴趣区域裁剪是最一致的策略，在20个设置中的19个提升了临床macro F1，对3D ViT Primus编码器平均提升+3.7分，对基于切片的2D ViT Curia编码器平均提升+1.1分。增加输入分辨率的效果高度依赖投影器选择：PerceiverResampler配合更高分辨率的Curia特征是分辨率研究中最强的配置。最佳配置在CT-RATE上达到49.5的临床macro F1，在Merlin上达到49.0。

Conclusion: 视觉token预算的分配是3D CT放射学报告生成中的关键设计维度，解剖引导的区域裁剪是一种稳健且高效的策略，未来应重视如何通过智能区域选择和投影器设计来优化有限的视觉token预算。

Abstract: Vision-language models offer a promising path toward automating radiology report generation, but applying them to full 3D CT volumes poses substantial computational challenges. Modern foundation vision encoders (VEs) can produce tens of thousands of vision tokens per scan, making the visual sequence passed to the large language model (LLM) a primary computational bottleneck. Vision-to-language projectors can compress this sequence to reduce computation, but may discard clinically relevant detail; conversely, effective compression can accommodate higher-resolution inputs while keeping the downstream token count fixed. How this vision-token budget should be allocated across input field of view, spatial resolution, and vision-to-language projection therefore remains an open design question. We systematically evaluate four heterogeneous VEs (CNN- and ViT-based), five token-reducing projectors at up to 64x compression alongside a non-reducing MLP projector baseline, and five instruction-tuned LLMs (1.7B--4B) on two large-scale CT report datasets (CT-RATE and Merlin). At matched LLM token budgets, anatomy-guided region of interest cropping is the most consistent strategy, improving clinical macro F1 in 19 of 20 settings by +3.7 points on average for the 3D ViT Primus encoder and +1.1 for the slice-based 2D ViT Curia encoder. Increasing input resolution further is strongly projector-dependent: the PerceiverResampler, paired with higher-resolution Curia features, yields the strongest configuration in the resolution study on both datasets. Our best configurations achieve state-of-the-art clinical macro F1 on the test sets, reaching 49.5 on CT-RATE and 49.0 on Merlin. Code and models will be published upon publication.

</details>


### [134] [High-Quality Exposure Correction with Diffusion-Based Image Generation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08720)
*Ziwen Li,Meng Cao,Jinpu Zhang,Chunyang Li,Long Bao,Heng Sun,Yuehuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DPEC框架，利用预训练大規模扩散模型中的图像生成先验进行曝光校正，通过高效微调策略实现单步去噪生成增强图像，并结合联合交叉注意力模块整合多尺度扩散先验特征，在多个数据集上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有曝光校正方法过度关注像素级精度，难以有效建模极端曝光区域，导致感知质量不佳；扩散模型在图像生成中表现优异，但如何成功应用于曝光校正仍是一个开放且具挑战性的问题，关键在于扩散随机过程中保持图像结构准确性和高保真度。

Method: (1) 提出高效微调策略，从预训练扩散模型派生曝光校正器，实现单步去噪生成； (2) 设计联合交叉注意力模块，融合扩散模型与回归模型优势，整合多尺度扩散先验特征以保留高频细节并减少随机伪影； (3) 让扩散模型专注处理低频内容而非全部复杂纹理细节。

Result: 实验结果表明，DPEC方法在多个曝光校正数据集上，在保真度、感知质量和视觉效果方面均一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: DPEC成功将扩散模型先验应用于曝光校正任务，通过扩散与回归模型相结合的策略有效解决了极端曝光区域建模问题，实现了保真度与感知质量的双重提升。

Abstract: Although most existing exposure correction methods achieve high fidelity, they often place excessive focus on overall pixel-wise accuracy, making it challenging to effectively model extreme exposure regions, which results in suboptimal perceptual quality. Recently, diffusion models have received significant attention due to their remarkable performance in the realm of image generation. However, their successful application to exposure correction remains a challenging and open question. The key challenge lies in generating accurate image structures and maintaining high image fidelity during stochastic diffusion processes. In this paper, we propose DPEC (Diffusion Prior-based Exposure Correction), a novel framework for image exposure correction that utilizes diffusion-based image generation priors encapsulated in pre-trained large-scale diffusion models. Specifically, we first propose an efficient fine-tuning strategy to derive an exposure corrector from pre-trained models, enabling the generation of enhanced images in a single-step denoising process. Moreover, we seamlessly combine the strengths of diffusion models and regression models, and design a joint cross-attention module to integrate multi-scale diffusion prior features, thereby effectively preserving high-frequency details and minimizing random artifacts. The diffusion model focuses on dealing with low-frequency content rather than all the intricate texture details. The experimental results demonstrate that the proposed DPEC method consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods on multiple exposure correction datasets, whether in terms of fidelity, perceptual quality, or visual effects.

</details>


### [135] [TomaMMU: A Comprehensive Multimodal Understanding Benchmark for Tomato Leaf Diseases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08727)
*Gia-Han Truong,Khang Nguyen Quoc,Luyl-Da Quach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TomaMMU，一个大规模番茄叶病多模态理解数据集（含28,808张图像、15个类别、213,119个人工标注的视觉问答对），以及TomaBench基准框架，用于评估视觉语言模型（VLMs）在番茄疾病理解上的表现。实验表明14个SOTA VLMs在细粒度识别和事实推理上存在明显不足，而简单微调即可将挑战性MCQ准确率提升至96.09%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在农业植物病理学领域缺乏系统性的评估数据集和基准，尤其是在细粒度疾病识别和诊断推理方面存在明显空白，因此需要构建大规模、高质量的多模态数据集来推动该领域的发展。

Method: 采用三阶段流程构建数据集：数据收集（Data Collection）、人工标注（Human Annotation）和问答生成（Question-Answer Generation）。在此基础上，设计了TomaBench基准，将七项农业任务组织为三层级分类体系：基础感知（Basic Perception）、病理理解（Pathology Understanding）和专家诊断（Expert Diagnosis），实现从低级视觉识别到高级诊断推理的系统评估。

Result: 对14个SOTA视觉语言模型进行测试，发现它们在细粒度识别和基于事实的推理上表现不佳，尤其在挑战性多项选择题和开放式问题上持续落后。通过TomaMMU进行简单微调后，挑战性MCQ准确率提升至96.09%，优于近期VLMs。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型难以将视觉感知转化为可靠的诊断知识，亟需领域特定的适应训练。TomaMMU/TomaBench为植物病理学领域的VLM评估和改进提供了重要资源，简单微调即可大幅缩小性能差距，指明了未来研究方向。

Abstract: To address this gap, we introduce TomaMMU, a large-scale Tomato leaf disease MultiModal Understanding dataset, alongside TomaBench, a benchmark for evaluating VLMs on tomato disease understanding. TomaMMU comprises 28,808 high-quality images spanning 15 categories and 213,119 human-annotated visual question-answer pairs, generated through a three-stage pipeline comprising Data Collection, Human Annotation, and Question-Answer Generation. Building on this foundation, TomaBench organizes seven agricultural tasks into a hierarchical three-level taxonomy spanning Basic Perception, Pathology Understanding, and Expert Diagnosis, which together enable systematic evaluation from low-level visual recognition to high-level diagnostic reasoning. The tasks assess visual symptom recognition, taxonomic relationships, and diagnostic reasoning, offering a comprehensive view of how well models grasp plant pathology. Our results pronounced gaps in fine-grained recognition and factually grounded reasoning with 14 state-of-the-art VLMs, consistently underperforming on both challenging MCQs and open-ended questions. These results suggest that current VLMs struggle to translate visual perception into reliable diagnostic knowledge, motivating the need for targeted domain adaptation. Simple fine-tuning on TomaMMU substantially narrows this gap, boosting accuracy on challenging MCQs to 96.09%, outperforming recent VLMs, and pointing toward promising directions for future work. All data and code is available in https://huggingface.co/datasets/enalis/TomaMMU.

</details>


### [136] [AnchorFold: A Focus-Then-Fold Framework via Recursive Attention Propagation for Efficient Multi-Vector Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08732)
*Haoyu Zuo,Yibo Yan,Xin Zou,Shuliang Liu,Yi Cao,Mingdong Ou,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnchorFold是一个无需训练的文档侧索引压缩框架，通过"聚焦-折叠"两阶段策略，在保持检索精度的同时大幅压缩视觉文档检索索引，5倍压缩下平均保留98.3%的NDCG@5性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量视觉语言检索器通过晚交互实现细粒度视觉文档检索，但每页存储和计算数百个视觉patch嵌入带来巨大开销。现有无训练方法要么在激进压缩下性能急剧下降（剪枝），要么无法显式优先考虑重要区域（合并）。

Method: AnchorFold采用"聚焦-折叠"框架：聚焦阶段通过递归注意力传播在视觉自注意力图上进行多步传播，跨注意力头和层集成分数，选择最高中心性token作为锚点；折叠阶段将剩余token分配给最相似的锚点，并通过中心性加权聚合来总结每个锚点组，保留非锚点的贡献同时集中容量于结构重要token。

Result: 在ViDoRe v1/v2和REAL-MM-RAG上使用三种检索骨干网络，AnchorFold在γ≤0.20压缩率下一致优于所有无训练基线。在ViDoRe v1/v2上，5倍压缩时平均保留98.3%的完整索引NDCG@5，实现近乎无损压缩；20倍压缩时仍保留92.4%。

Conclusion: AnchorFold通过显式识别和利用文档中结构重要的token，在无需训练的情况下实现了高效且近乎无损的视觉文档检索索引压缩，显著优于现有的剪枝和合并类无训练方法。

Abstract: Multi-vector vision-language retrievers enable fine-grained Visual Document Retrieval (VDR) through late interaction, but storing and scoring hundreds of visual patch embeddings per page incurs substantial overhead. Existing training-free methods rely on pruning or merging: pruning degrades sharply under aggressive compression, whereas merging does not explicitly prioritize important regions when forming representatives. We introduce AnchorFold, a training-free focus-then-fold framework for document-side index compression. AnchorFold applies Recursive Attention Propagation over visual self-attention graphs, performing multi-step propagation within each attention head and integrating scores across heads and layers. The focus stage selects the highest-centrality tokens as anchors. The fold stage assigns remaining tokens to their most similar anchors in the normalized retrieval space and summarizes each anchor-centered group through centrality-weighted aggregation. This preserves non-anchor contributions while concentrating capacity on structurally important tokens. Across ViDoRe v1/v2 and REAL-MM-RAG with three diverse retrieval backbones, AnchorFold consistently outperforms all evaluated training-free baselines at $γ\leq 0.20$. On ViDoRe v1/v2, it retains 98.3% of full-index NDCG@5 on average at $5\times$ compression, achieving near-lossless compression, and 92.4% at $20\times$ compression.

</details>


### [137] [IDATA: Scalable Invertible Diffusion for Unrestricted Adversarial Transfer Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08734)
*Yi Pan,Jun-Jie Huang,Tianrui Liu,Zihan Chen,Lin Liu,Zhao Wentao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IDATA，一个内存高效的扩散框架用于无限制对抗迁移攻击。它通过可逆扩散模块(IDM)实现恒定内存的反向传播，并利用低频约束模块(LFCM)基于离散小波变换将扰动限制在语义稳定的低频子空间，从而在攻击成功率、内存效率和视觉隐蔽性上均超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的对抗攻击方法面临两大挑战：多步反向传播导致的高内存开销，以及中间潜变量上频率无关的扰动影响迁移性和视觉隐蔽性。

Method: 提出两个关键组件：1) 可逆扩散模块(IDM)，将扩散轨迹上的对抗优化重构为可逆过程，通过按需重建中间状态实现恒定内存反向传播；2) 低频约束模块(LFCM)，利用离散小波变换(DWT)将潜变量分解为低/高频分量，将扰动限制在语义稳定的低频子空间以提升迁移性并保持视觉隐蔽性。

Result: 在多个基准测试和多种模型架构上的大量实验表明，IDATA在攻击成功率、内存效率和视觉隐蔽性三个方面均持续优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: IDATA为深度视觉模型的黑盒鲁棒性评估提供了一个有前景的工具，代码已公开。

Abstract: Unrestricted adversarial transfer attacks are important for evaluating the black-box robustness of deep visual models. Diffusion-based attacks have shown promising transferability and visual imperceptibility by optimizing adversarial perturbations along denoising trajectories in latent space. However, existing methods are limited by two challenges: memory-intensive multistep backpropagation and frequency-agnostic perturbation over intermediate latents. To address these issues, we propose IDATA, a memory-efficient diffusion framework for unrestricted adversarial transfer attack. IDATA consists of two key components: an Invertible Diffusion Module (IDM) and a Low-Frequency Constraint Module (LFCM). Specifically, IDM reformulates adversarial optimization over diffusion trajectories as an invertible process, enabling constant-memory backpropagation through on-demand reconstruction of intermediate states instead of storing the full denoising chain. Moreover, LFCM leverages Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) to decompose latent variables into low- and high-frequency components, restricting perturbations to semantically stable low-frequency subspaces, thereby improving transferability while preserving visual imperceptibility. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks and diverse model architectures demonstrate that IDATA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in attack success rate, memory efficiency, and visual imperceptibility. These results suggest that IDATA is a promising tool for black-box robustness evaluation of deep visual models. Code is available at https://github.com/colourful-pan/IDATA.

</details>


### [138] [Parcel2Progression: An Anatomy-aware Longitudinal Framework for Alzheimer's Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08753)
*Madhumitha Venkatesh,Shanawaj S Madarkar,Konda Reddy Mopuri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Parcel2Progression（P2P），一个基于纵向Transformer的框架，利用图谱引导的分区编码器（Atlas-guided Parcel Encoder）将3D脑部扫描token化，再通过纵向Transformer整合不规则、任意长度的纵向访视与患者年龄信息，实现高效的阿尔茨海默病进展预测。该方法在MCI转AD预测和AD vs CN分类任务上优于现有方法，计算复杂度从二次方降至线性，并具备良好的可解释性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病（AD）进展是一个纵向过程，早期阶段存在细微的病理线索。然而，计算限制导致大多数神经影像模型要么牺牲空间信息，要么限制纵向扫描数量。作者旨在克服这一瓶颈，充分利用高分辨率、可变长度的4D结构MRI扫描序列，同时解决计算效率和临床可解释性问题。

Method: 提出P2P框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 图谱引导的分区编码器（Atlas-guided Parcel Encoder），将3D扫描切分为一组更丰富的解剖学基础表示（tokenization）；(2) 纵向Transformer，整合不规则、任意长度的纵向访视与患者年龄信息。该设计使得计算复杂度与扫描次数呈线性关系（相比朴素4D ViT的二次方复杂度），同时实现了分区级别的可解释性。

Result: P2P在ADNI、AIBL和MIRIAD三个数据集上的MCI转AD转换预测和AD vs CN分类任务中均优于先前方法。利用纵向扫描相比单次扫描基线在AD分类和MCI转换预测的平衡准确率上分别提升最高达5%和7%。通过分区显著性和注意力展开的可解释性分析揭示了与临床一致的AD和MCI萎缩模式。此外，在合成数据集上验证了异常检测的可靠性，并在额颞叶痴呆等其他神经退行性疾病上验证了模型的泛化性。

Conclusion: P2P通过将图谱先验知识与纵向Transformer框架结合，成功实现了对高分辨率、可变长度4D sMRI的高效利用，在性能、计算效率、可解释性和跨疾病泛化性方面均展现出显著优势，为神经影像纵向分析提供了一种实用且临床可用的解决方案。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progression is a longitudinal process with subtle pathological cues in the early stages. Yet, computational constraints have limited most neuroimaging models to either compromise spatial information or limit the number of longitudinal scans. We aim to overcome this bottleneck and fully leverage high-resolution, variable-length T1w structural MRI (4D sMRI) scan sequences. We introduce Parcel2Progression (P2P), a Longitudinal Transformer Framework which tackles this challenge using an Atlas-guided Parcel Encoder that tokenizes 3D scans into a set of richer anatomically grounded representations. A Longitudinal Transformer then integrates irregular, arbitrary-length longitudinal visits with patient age. This synergy delivers two key advantages: (1) parcel-specific interpretability, and (2) computational tractability for long-term analysis, which scales linearly with the number of scans compared to a naive quadratic 4D ViT cost. P2P outperforms prior works and baselines in both MCI (Mild Cognitive Impairment) to AD conversion prediction and AD vs. CN (Cognitively Normal) classification tasks across ADNI, AIBL, and MIRIAD datasets. Leveraging longitudinal scans boosts performance over single-scan baselines by up to 5% and 7% in balanced accuracy for AD classification and MCI conversion prediction tasks, respectively. Interpretability analysis using parcel saliencies and attention rollouts reveals clinically consistent atrophy patterns in AD and MCI subjects. We also demonstrate the frameworks' reliability in anomaly detection using a synthetic dataset, and test the model's generalizability for other neurodegenerative diseases like Frontotemporal Dementia.

</details>


### [139] [UPolarSQ: Polar Representation Learning for Optic Disc and Peripapillary Atrophy Segmentation and Quantification in Fundus Photographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08771)
*Mengxian He,Yunyun sun,Ziyue Gao,Wengkei Lam,Shunyi Zhang,Wu Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UPolarSQ，一个统一极坐标域框架，用于近视眼底图像中视盘（OD）和视盘周围萎缩（PPA）的分割及生物标志物量化。通过将OD中心区域的图像映射至极坐标，利用增强的U-Net网络（UPolarSeg）进行分割，并直接从极坐标分割掩膜中提取临床生物标志物，在内部和外部数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近视引起的后极部重构常伴随视盘变形和视盘周围萎缩（PPA），二者是重要的临床结构生物标志物。然而，在笛卡尔坐标系眼底图像中，PPA常呈现不规则且部分可见的新月形区域，导致分割碎片化和量化结果依赖后处理，亟需一种更鲁棒且统一的分割与量化框架。

Method: UPolarSQ首先将OD中心区域映射至极坐标系，使OD和PPA边界可表示为径向剖面；随后采用UPolarSeg——一种结合径向-角度解耦模块和边界感知辅助监督的U-Net分割网络，以建模各向异性极坐标特征和径向边界过渡；最后从预测的极坐标掩膜中确定性地提取视盘形状和PPA宽度等临床生物标志物，在共享几何表示下统一分割与量化。

Result: 在内部和外部队列上的实验表明，UPolarSQ改善了OD/PPA分割性能，并支持可靠的极坐标原生生物标志物估计，适用于近视眼底分析。

Conclusion: UPolarSQ通过极坐标域的统一框架，有效解决了近视眼底图像中PPA分割碎片化的问题，实现了可靠的分割与生物标志物量化，为近视临床分析提供了结构化的自动化工具。

Abstract: Myopia-induced posterior-pole remodeling is frequently accompanied by Optic Disc (OD) deformation and Peripapillary Atrophy (PPA), both of which provide clinically relevant structural biomarkers. In Cartesian fundus images, however, PPA often appears as an irregular and partially visible crescent adjacent to the OD, leading to fragmented segmentation and post-processing-dependent quantification. We propose UPolarSQ, a unified polar-domain framework for OD/PPA segmentation and biomarker quantification in myopic fundus images. UPolarSQ first maps an OD-centered region of interest into polar coordinates, where OD and PPA boundaries can be represented as radial profiles. It then employs UPolarSeg, a U-Net-based segmentation network enhanced with a Radial-Angular-Decoupled Module and boundary-aware auxiliary supervision to model anisotropic polar features and radial boundary transitions. Clinical biomarkers, including disc shape and PPA-width-related measurements, are deterministically extracted from the predicted polar masks, aligning segmentation and quantification within a shared geometric representation. Experiments on internal and external cohorts demonstrate that UPolarSQ improves OD/PPA segmentation and supports reliable polar-native biomarker estimation for myopic analysis.

</details>


### [140] [LASA: Language-and-Source-Anchored Alignment for Domain Generalized Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08805)
*Jinhong Zhu,Weiqi Yan,Shengchuan Zhang,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LASA框架解决域泛化语义分割中的特征完整性问题，通过文本与源域锚定对齐的三个协同组件（TSGST、DAQA、DADO），在多个基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有域泛化语义分割方法（风格随机化和特征归一化）在缓解域偏移时损害了特征完整性：风格随机化因粗粒度特性扭曲特征流形，特征归一化因刚性设计抑制了具有判别性的域敏感语义细节。

Method: 提出LASA框架，包含三个协同组件：TSGST模块利用源域特征作为结构锚点并结合VLM先验进行细粒度指导，解决流形扭曲问题；DAQA模块通过类别指导和域感知签名重新校准对象查询；DADO模块将查询分布与共享分类器对齐，确保跨域类别响应一致性。

Result: 在多个挑战性基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: LASA框架通过引入语言和源域锚定机制，有效解决了现有方法在域泛化语义分割中特征完整性问题，为跨域语义分割提供了一种新的解决方案。

Abstract: Domain Generalization Semantic Segmentation (DGSS) focuses on generalizing knowledge from labeled source domains to unseen target domains where data is unavailable during the training phase. While conventional methods utilize style randomization or feature normalization to mitigate domain shifts, they often impair feature integrity. Specifically, style randomization distorts the underlying feature manifold due to its coarse-grained nature, while feature normalization suppresses discriminative, domain-sensitive semantic details owing to its rigid design. To address these limitations, we propose the Language-and-Source-Anchored Alignment (LASA) framework, which comprises three synergistic components: Text-and-Source-Guided Style Transfer (TSGST), Domain-Aware Query Adapter (DAQA), and Domain-Aware Decoder Optimizer (DADO). Concretely, the TSGST module addresses manifold distortion by utilizing source features as structural anchors and vision-language model (VLM) priors as fine-grained guidance. To restore suppressed discriminative and domain-sensitive details, the DAQA module recalibrates object queries via categorical guidance and domain-aware signatures, while the DADO module aligns the resulting query distributions with a shared classifier to ensure consistent categorical responses across domains. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [141] [AdapterMoE: A Two-Stage Hard-Routing Mixture-of-Experts Architecture for Multi-Crop Disease Recognition with Calibrated Rejection and Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08808)
*Pin-Hsun Huang,Shaou-Gang Miaou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdapterMoE，一种基于确定性硬路由的Mixture-of-Experts框架，用于多作物病害识别。它将路由目标从纯准确率转向部署成本、扩展灵活性和拒绝稳定性之间的权衡，通过RouterHead分类作物并用温度缩放校准各作物的Adapters，在PlantVillage数据集上达到与最优基线统计上无显著差异的准确率，同时大幅降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的软路由MoE在作物病害识别中存在专家崩溃问题（少数专家主导、无语义对应），且面临高重训成本、对非目标输入拒绝不稳定以及准确率饱和等挑战。作者希望从准确率单一目标转向综合考虑部署成本、扩展灵活性和拒绝稳定性的多目标权衡。

Method: 提出AdapterMoE框架：(1) RouterHead负责作物分类并通过最大Softmax概率阈值拒绝非目标作物；(2) 双重门控（Energy+KNN）的OOD模块捕获分布偏移输入；(3) 五个作物专属Adapter叠加在冻结的EfficientNet-B0骨干上，每个通过温度缩放校准；(4) 采用确定性硬路由，在数据层面硬隔离专家，避免专家崩溃并提供逐作物增量更新的add_crop接口。

Result: 在PlantVillage数据集（5种作物、26个类别）上，通过五个系统的公平对比，AdapterMoE达到与最优基线统计上无显著差异的准确率（Macro-F1差异在0.24个百分点内），同时将训练成本降至全网络基线的约9%，并能高效扩展新作物。

Conclusion: 硬路由的AdapterMoE通过牺牲极小精度损失（统计上不可区分），在部署成本、扩展灵活性和拒绝稳定性方面取得了显著优势，为多作物病害识别提供了一种更实用、更可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Timely crop-disease identification is critical to food security. Multi-crop recognition suits Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), but conventional soft-routing MoE learns crop assignment freely end-to-end, letting a few experts dominate (expert collapse) with no semantic correspondence to crops, and facing high retraining costs, unstable rejection of non-target inputs, and a saturated accuracy ceiling. We shift the objective from accuracy toward a trade-off among deployment cost, scaling flexibility, and rejection stability, using deterministic hard routing. We propose AdapterMoE: a RouterHead classifies the crop and rejects non-target crops via a Maximum Softmax Probability threshold, with a dual-gate Energy+KNN out-of-distribution module catching distribution-shifted inputs; five per-crop Adapters atop a frozen EfficientNet-B0 backbone discriminate diseases, each calibrated via Temperature Scaling. Because experts are hard-isolated at the data level, the design avoids expert collapse and exposes an add_crop interface for local, per-crop updates instead of full retraining. On PlantVillage (5 crops, 26 classes), across a fair five-system comparison, AdapterMoE attains accuracy statistically indistinguishable from the best baselines (Macro-F1 within a 0.24-point band) while cutting training cost to about 9% of full-network baselines, expanding to a new crop in

</details>


### [142] [360CityArena: A Realistic Virtual Urban Navigation Benchmark for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08814)
*Kenta Watanabe,Atsuyuki Miyai,Mizuki Takenawa,Kiyoharu Aizawa,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 360CityArena是一个基于360度视频构建的写实城市环境基准测试，用于评估具身智能体在城市探索中的能力。该基准覆盖东京秋叶原地区85条街道，包含175个人工设计的任务，涵盖环境理解、路径推理和空间推理三大类。实验表明，最强模型Gemini 2.5 Flash仅达到17.1%的准确率，远低于人类77.3%的水平，揭示了城市规模具身导航与推理仍面临巨大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室外基准测试要么缺乏足够的逼真度，要么复杂度不足，与真实城市环境存在较大差距，无法有效评估具身智能体在城市探索中的真实能力。

Method: 基于东京秋叶原地区的602段360度视频片段（覆盖85条街道）构建写实重建环境，设计175个人工精制任务，分为环境理解、路径推理和空间推理三大类，并使用最先进的LMM智能体进行评测。

Result: 最强模型Gemini 2.5 Flash仅达到17.1%的准确率，而人类水平为77.3%，两者之间存在巨大差距，表明当前LMM智能体在城市规模具身导航和推理方面仍远未达到人类水平。

Conclusion: 360CityArena为照片级真实城市区域导航和空间推理提供了一个必要且具有挑战性的测试平台，揭示了城市尺度具身导航与空间推理中仍存在的重大挑战。

Abstract: We present 360CityArena, a benchmark for evaluating the urban exploration capabilities of embodied agents within a photorealistic environment constructed from 360-degree videos. Existing outdoor benchmarks either lack sufficient photorealism or complexity, resulting in a considerable gap from real-world urban environments. 360CityArena is built on a realistic reconstruction of the Akihabara district in Tokyo, Japan, using 602 360-degree video segments covering 85 streets, and consists of 175 meticulously human-crafted tasks. It encompasses three task categories: Environment Understanding, Path Reasoning, and Spatial Reasoning, covering fundamental abilities required for urban exploration, such as localization, landmark search, path planning, and relational spatial reasoning, thereby enabling comprehensive evaluation in realistic urban scenes. Our evaluation using state-of-the-art LMM-based agents shows that even the strongest model, Gemini 2.5 Flash, performs far below human level (human: 77.3% vs. Gemini 2.5 Flash: 17.1%), revealing substantial challenges that remain in city-scale embodied navigation and reasoning. 360CityArena provides a necessary and challenging testbed for photorealistic urban-district navigation and spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [143] [MRI super-resolution in ten sampling steps using a diffusion bridge model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08819)
*Mojtaba Safari,Hang Yu,Zach Eidex,Mingzhe Hu,Ryan J. Sanford,Alexandru Florea,Shansong Wang,Chih-Wei Chang,Erik H Middlebrooks,Aditya Juloori,Stanley L. Liauw,Ralph Weichselbaum,Xiaofeng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SR-DBM（超分辨率扩散桥模型），将MRI超分辨率重建建模为低分辨率与高分辨率图像分布之间的随机传输。通过Doob's h-变换的均值回归随机微分方程，SR-DBM将过程锚定在成对的HR和LR图像端点，从测量解剖结构而非高斯噪声初始化重建，仅需10个采样步骤即可恢复HR图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI获取时间长导致患者不适和运动伪影，传统扩散模型做超分辨率需要大量采样步数，且从高斯先验初始化不适合图像恢复任务。

Method: 提出SR-DBM（super-resolution diffusion bridge model），将超分辨率建模为LR到HR分布之间的随机传输。利用Doob's h-变换实现均值回归SDE，锚定过程端点为成对的HR和LR图像，通过确定性反向轨迹由网络在每个采样步预测干净图像，仅需10步采样。

Result: 在7T脑部T1 MP2RAGE和盆腔T2加权前列腺图像上，SR-DBM在两个数据集上均取得最高PSNR和SSIM及最低GMSD（脑部：27.66±1.52 dB、0.96±0.02、7.96±1.86；前列腺：27.87±2.29 dB、0.80±0.05、8.38±1.44），对每个对比方法均有统计学显著提升（Wilcoxon符号秩检验，Holm校正，p<0.05）。定性结果上产生最小残差误差，最好地保留了精细结构和病变。

Conclusion: SR-DBM通过扩散桥模型有效解决了传统扩散SR的采样效率问题和先验不匹配问题，在超高频7T MRI和盆腔MRI超分辨率重建中达到最优性能，且仅需10个采样步，具有较高的临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Objective. MRI provides excellent soft-tissue contrast, but long acquisition times can cause patient discomfort and lead to motion artifacts, forcing a trade-off between spatial resolution and scan time. Diffusion-based super-resolution (SR) reconstructs high-resolution (HR) images from low-resolution (LR) inputs, but typically needs many sampling steps and initializes from a Gaussian prior ill-suited to image restoration. We developed an efficient diffusion framework that reconstructs HR MRI directly from LR data. Approach. We propose super-resolution diffusion bridge model (SR-DBM), a super-resolution diffusion bridge model that casts SR as a stochastic transport between the LR and HR image distributions. Through a Doob's h-transform of a mean-reverting stochastic differential equation, SR-DBM pins the process to the paired HR and LR images at its endpoints, initializing reconstruction from the measured anatomy rather than from Gaussian noise. The HR image is recovered by a deterministic reverse trajectory in which a network predicts the clean image at each of only ten sampling steps. We evaluated SR-DBM on ultra-high-field 7T brain T1 MP2RAGE maps and pelvic T2-weighted prostate images against nine comparison methods using PSNR, SSIM, GMSD, and LPIPS. Main results. SR-DBM attained the highest PSNR and SSIM and the lowest GMSD on both datasets (brain: 27.66+-1.52 dB, 0.96+-0.02, 7.96+-1.86$; prostate: 27.87+-2.29 dB, 0.80+-0.05, 8.38+- 1.44), with statistically significant gains over every comparison method (two-sided Wilcoxon signed-rank test with Holm correction, p<0.05). The strongest baseline, SR-EMamba, ranked second. Qualitatively, SR-DBM produced the smallest residual errors and best preserved fine structures and lesions.

</details>


### [144] [LogiShot: Logically Coherent Cross-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08820)
*Shuai Guo,Yuhang Yang,Zeyu Zhang,Pengfei Yu,Wei Zhai,Yang Cao,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LogiShot框架，通过联合编码上下文视频与多模态条件信号，并在生成过程中维护视觉记忆，实现跨镜头视频生成的逻辑连贯性与视觉一致性。同时构建了11万样本的数据集和专用基准来评估跨镜头逻辑连贯性，实验证明其优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数跨镜头视频生成流程依赖孤立的文本脚本或显式参考图像，当用户指令不够明确或存在歧义时，生成的片段可能单独看起来合理但无法与整体叙事对齐，导致内容脱节。作者认为实现跨镜头逻辑连贯需要建立镜头间的逻辑连接并保持视觉一致性。

Method: 提出LogiShot框架，包含两条互补路径：(1) 联合编码上下文视频与其他条件信号，生成密集多模态线索，为跨镜头生成提供视觉语义证据；(2) 在生成过程中维护上下文视频的视觉记忆，以保持跨镜头的视觉一致性。此外构建了11万样本的数据集和专门的评估基准。

Result: 实验表明LogiShot在多个镜头的逻辑连贯性方面持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: LogiShot通过多模态联合编码与视觉记忆机制有效解决了跨镜头视频生成的逻辑连贯性问题，且模型和数据将公开发布，为该领域提供了新的资源和基准。

Abstract: Generating cross-shot videos that are logically connected is essential for content creation. Currently, most cross-shot video-generation workflows, such as short-drama production, still rely on isolated textual scripts or explicit reference images to specify the generated content. Consequently, when user instructions are underspecified or ambiguous, a generated clip may appear visually plausible on its own but fail to align with the overall narrative, leading to disjointed content. We argue that achieving cross-shot logical coherence in video generation requires establishing logical connections across shots and maintaining visual consistency. To this end, we propose LogiShot, which incorporates information through two complementary paths: 1) LogiShot jointly encodes the context video and other conditioning signals, yielding dense multimodal cues that provide visual-semantic evidence for cross-shot generation; 2) the model maintains a visual memory of the context video throughout generation to preserve visual consistency across shots. Additionally, we construct a dataset with 110K samples and a dedicated benchmark for evaluating cross-shot logical coherence. Experiments demonstrate that LogiShot consistently outperforms existing baselines in terms of logical coherence across multiple shots. Model and data will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [145] [Visual Token Codec: Unleashing Spatial Redundancy for ViT Feature Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08832)
*Donghui Feng,Fengxi Zhang,Changsheng Gao,Wenhan Yang,Qi Wang,Qunshan Gu,Hongwei Hu,Zhengxue Cheng,Li Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VTC是一种双路径学习的视觉token编解码器，将全局token和局部patch token分离到各自的编码路径中，利用patch token在原始二维网格上的空间相关性进行高效特征压缩，在分类、分割和检测任务上大幅超越现有ViT特征编码基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式部署大型视觉基础模型时，ViT主干被分区并需在计算节点间交换中间token特征。现有ViT特征编解码器将异构的全局和patch token展平为L×C伪图像，导致熵模型主要捕捉序列轴依赖而忽视patch token固有的二维网格结构，压缩效率受限。

Method: VTC采用双路径结构：全局token使用轻量级因子化先验压缩；patch token在patch-token网格上使用空间-通道上下文熵模型编码。此外引入后续ViT块的特征匹配监督以支持中间层压缩，并集成变速率模块实现单编解码器内的速率自适应。

Result: 在DINOv2和SAM3上的实验表明，VTC在分类、分割和检测任务上持续优于代表性ViT特征编码基线。在达到未压缩特征性能90%的水平时，VTC在各任务上将比特率降低15.7倍至37.4倍。

Conclusion: VTC证明了利用patch token二维网格结构先验能显著提升ViT中间特征压缩效率，并提供中间层率-效用分析以支持实际传输和存储场景的部署选择。

Abstract: Distributed deployment of large vision foundation models often partitions a ViT backbone and exchanges intermediate token features between computing nodes, making efficient feature compression critical under bandwidth and computation constraints. Existing ViT feature codecs typically flatten heterogeneous global and patch tokens into an L x C pseudo image, causing entropy models to mainly capture sequence-axis dependencies while overlooking the native two-dimensional patch-grid structure. In this paper, we show that ViT patch tokens retain strong local spatial correlations on the original grid. To exploit this structural prior, we propose the Visual Token Codec (VTC), a dual-path learned codec that separates global and patch tokens into dedicated coding paths. Global tokens are compressed with a lightweight factorized prior, whereas patch tokens are encoded on the patch-token grid using a spatial-channel context entropy model. To support intermediate-layer compression and practical rate adaptation, VTC further incorporates feature-matching supervision after subsequent ViT blocks and variable-rate modules within a single codec. Experiments on DINOv2 and SAM3 show that VTC consistently outperforms representative ViT feature coding baselines on classification, segmentation, and detection tasks. At 90% of uncompressed-feature performance, VTC reduces bitrate by 15.7x-37.4x across these tasks. We further provide intermediate-layer rate-utility analyses for practical transmission- and storage-oriented deployment scenarios.

</details>


### [146] [SLAP: Selective Local Vision-Language Alignment for Fish Re-Identification via Partial Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08840)
*Cigdem Beyan,Tonje Knutsen Sordalen,Kim Tallaksen Halvorsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于选择性局部视觉-语言对齐的鱼类个体再识别框架，通过局部最优传输实现视觉补丁嵌入与身份感知提示嵌入之间的选择性匹配，避免背景和弱判别区域的干扰，在多个海洋数据集上优于现有CLIP-based方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CLIP-based鱼类再识别方法过度依赖全局图像-文本对齐，导致背景和弱判别区域也参与跨模态监督，降低了模型的判别能力。而鱼类个体识别的关键判别线索往往集中在特定身体区域，需要更精细的局部对齐策略。

Method: 提出选择性局部视觉-语言对齐框架，利用局部最优传输（POT）在视觉补丁嵌入和多个身份感知提示嵌入之间建立局部对应关系。POT允许选择性匹配，强调最强的跨模态对应关系，避免对弱匹配区域的强制对齐。框架采用端到端训练，推理时仅保留适配后的视觉编码器。

Result: 在纵向的Symphodus melops数据集上，该方法在闭集和开集评估协议下均一致优于近期的CLIP-based再识别方法。在其他数据集的额外评估也证明了该方法在多样化海洋再识别基准上的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 选择性局部视觉-语言对齐通过局部最优传输有效地解决了鱼类再识别中全局对齐的局限性，能够提取更具判别性的视觉表征，并为细粒度跨模态识别提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Individual fish re-identification (ReID) is a fine-grained recognition problem in which identity-discriminative cues are often localized to specific body regions rather than distributed uniformly across the animal. Nevertheless, recent CLIP-based ReID methods rely predominantly on global image-text alignment, allowing background and weakly discriminative regions to contribute to cross-modal supervision. We propose a selective local vision-language alignment framework that establishes localized correspondences between visual patch embeddings and multiple identity-aware prompt embeddings through Partial Optimal Transport (POT). Rather than enforcing exhaustive correspondence, POT enables selective matching between visual patches and prompt embeddings, allowing the model to emphasize the strongest cross-modal correspondences while avoiding forced alignment of weakly matching regions, thereby yielding more discriminative visual representations for retrieval. The framework is trained end-to-end, while only the adapted visual encoder is retained during inference. Experiments on the longitudinal Symphodus melops dataset demonstrate consistent improvements over recent CLIP-based ReID methods under both closed-set and open-set evaluation protocols. Additional evaluations on other datasets further demonstrate the generalization capability of the proposed method across diverse marine ReID benchmarks.

</details>


### [147] [Toward Mask Annotation-Free Surgical Instrument Segmentation from Endoscopic Images Using Text-Prompted Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM3)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08844)
*Nakul Poudel,Richard Simon,Cristian A. Linte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架，利用SAM3的零样本能力结合微调的视觉语言模型Qwen，实现了无需像素级标注和手动提示的腹腔镜手术器械实例级分割，在EndoVis 2017和2018数据集上显著优于直接使用SAM3文本提示的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术器械分割方法依赖像素级标注或手动空间提示，限制了可扩展性和自动化程度。SAM3支持文本提示的自动分割，但器械名称作为文本提示因领域差异无法直接使用，需要探索替代方案。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段使用自然语言对齐的通用提示词"tool"，利用SAM3的零样本能力生成二值掩码；第二阶段将掩码扩展到实例级别，通过微调视觉语言模型Qwen对SAM3生成的掩码区域进行器械分类。

Result: 在EndoVis 2017和2018数据集上评估，该方法虽未达到全监督方法性能，但显著优于直接使用SAM3文本提示进行实例级器械分割。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了SAM3在手术器械分割中的局限性和潜力，为无标注手术器械分割提供了一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Surgical instrument segmentation is a fundamental task for computer-assisted interventions, yet most existing methods rely on pixel-level annotations or manual spatial prompts, which limit scalability and automation. The recently introduced Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM3) offers a pathway to annotation-free, automatic segmentation via text-based prompting; however, the instrument name as a text prompt could not be directly used due to a large domain gap. To overcome these limitations, we propose a two-stage framework that achieves instance-level segmentation without requiring ground truth masks or manual interaction. In the first stage, we leverage a natural-language-aligned generic prompt - "tool" - to produce binary masks using SAM3's zero-shot capability. In the second stage, these masks are extended to instance-level by integrating a vision-language model (Qwen) that is fine-tuned on SAM3-generated masked regions for instrument classification. We evaluate our approach on the EndoVis 2017 and 2018 datasets. Results show that, while our two-stage approach does not reach the performance of current fully supervised methods, it significantly outperforms the direct use of SAM3 for instance-level instrument segmentation with text prompts. Overall, our findings highlight both the limitations and potential of SAM3, suggesting a promising direction toward annotation-free surgical instrument segmentation.

</details>


### [148] [Zero-Shot Traffic Accident Detection via a Coarse-to-Fine VLM-Tracking Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08867)
*Dipit Saha,Shah Mohammad Abdul Mannan,Mohammad Raihan Rashid,Ruwad Naswan,Ahnaf Tahmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的、两阶段由粗到细的交通事故检测管线，结合固定的Qwen3-VL-32B视觉语言模型与YOLO11x目标检测和BoT-SORT跟踪，在CVPR ACCIDENT基准上以0.504的三路调和平均分数超越了所有基线，相对最优集成方法提升22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通监控摄像头持续捕捉事故，但将原始CCTV视频转换为结构化事件记录（精确定位事故发生时间、地点和碰撞类型）在大规模场景下仍未解决，且ACCIDENT基准严格限制不能使用标注的真实世界训练数据。

Method: 提出一个无训练的两阶段由粗到细管线：第一阶段稀疏采样完整视频片段以确定碰撞时刻；第二阶段对紧密时间窗口内的帧进行重检，使用带有稳定车辆身份和归一化边界框坐标的帧，为模型同时提供视觉叠加层和显式的数值场景描述。系统采用冻结的Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct视觉语言模型配合YOLO11x目标检测和BoT-SORT跟踪。

Result: 在官方2,027段真实CCTV测试集上，系统取得0.504的三路调和平均分数，超过所有组织者发布的基线，包括最佳多模型集成方法（0.412），相对提升22%。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过精心设计的提示工程和模态融合策略，无需任何训练数据的纯推理管线即可在交通事故检测基准上超越有监督的集成方法，展示了大型视觉语言模型在零样本视频事件理解任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Traffic surveillance cameras capture accidents continuously, yet converting raw CCTV footage into structured event records that pinpoint when, where, and what type of collision occurred remains unsolved at scale. The ACCIDENT @ CVPR benchmark evaluates exactly this joint prediction under a strict constraint: no labeled real-world training data is available. We introduce a training-free, two-pass coarse-to-fine pipeline that pairs a frozen Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct vision-language model with YOLO11x object detection and BoT-SORT tracking. A first pass sparsely samples the full clip to anchor the collision moment in time; a second pass re-examines a tight window around that estimate using frames annotated with stable vehicle identities and normalized bounding-box coordinates, which gives the model both a visual overlay and an explicit numeric description of the same scene. On the official 2,027-clip real-CCTV test set, our system achieves a three-way harmonic mean score of 0.504, surpassing all organizer-published baselines including the best multi-model ensemble (0.412) by a 22% relative margin.

</details>


### [149] [Sparse Attention to Emotion: Efficient Facial Emotion Recognition via Token Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08873)
*Aya Manel Zitouni,Aicha Zenakhri,Karim Haroun,Larbi Boubchir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAE（Sparse Attention to Emotion）模型，通过丢弃对情绪识别无贡献的图像token，在保留竞争性精度的同时将计算复杂度降低高达90%，并在RAF-DB数据集上取得新的最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Vision Transformer的面部情绪识别方法存在O(N²)的二次计算复杂度，难以在边缘设备部署；同时FER任务并不需要全部面部信息，特定区域（眼睛、嘴巴、脸颊）已携带足够的判别性信息。

Method: 提出SAE模型，通过稀疏注意力机制丢弃对情绪上下文无附加价值的图像token，保留具有判别性信息的关键面部区域，从而在保持精度的同时大幅降低计算成本。

Result: 即使抑制90%的图像token，SAE仍能达到与最先进方法竞争的精度；在RAF-DB数据集上取得新的最佳结果，同时将计算复杂度降低高达90%。

Conclusion: SAE证明了稀疏化注意力策略在面部情绪识别中的有效性——并非所有面部信息都是必要的，通过选择性丢弃冗余token可实现轻量级且高精度的FER模型，适合边缘部署。

Abstract: Facial Emotion Recognition (FER) is an important task that has significant implications across various fields such as biometrics, health, and human-computer interaction. Current Vision Transformer-based approaches display quadratic complexity $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$, with N being the input sequence length, making them cumbersome to deploy at the edge. In this paper, we hypothesize that the FER task does not necessarily require all facial information to correctly interpret emotional states, as specific regions such as the eyes, the mouth, and parts of the cheeks carry discriminative information that can be sufficient to recognize emotions. Based on this, we propose Sparse Attention to Emotion (SAE), a model that discards image tokens that have no added value to the emotional context, while preserving good accuracy and achieving a significant gain in computational cost. Surprisingly, even after suppressing 90\% of the image tokens, our model achieves competitive accuracy to state of the art methods at much lower cost, providing a lightweight Facial Emotion Recognition approach. Experimental results demonstrate that SAE achieves new state of the art results on the RAF-DB dataset while reducing the computational complexity by up to 90\%.

</details>


### [150] [AeroReformer2: Spoken-Query Referring Segmentation for Aerial Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08874)
*Rui Li,Chenxi Duan,Haoyang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向遥感图像的语音查询指代分割基准数据集，并设计了高效的双边网络模型，在清洁和干扰测试集上均取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感图像指代分割基准仅支持书面文字输入，缺乏语音查询的支持，而口语提供了一种自然、免手的交互方式来指定遥感图像中的任意目标，这一差距亟需填补。

Method: 构建了基于RISBench的语音查询基准数据集，加入多种口音和声音并附带三种信噪比下的转子、风和混合噪声干扰。提出了高效双边网络，包含边界保持的视觉路径、令牌保持的语音编码、核线性跨模态注意力以及分辨率精化头，在两个尺度上条件化视觉特征而无需求解稠密跨模态亲和矩阵。

Result: 基于Swin-Base的模型在清洁测试集上达到62.09% mIoU和68.22% oIoU，超过最强音频适配的遥感基线5.38和2.08个百分点；在硬干扰集上mIoU达到54.09%，同样保持最优。

Conclusion: 这是首个关于遥感图像全句语音查询指代分割的基准与模型研究，证明了语音查询在遥感指代分割领域的可行性，代码将公开。

Abstract: Spoken language offers a natural, hands-free interface for specifying an arbitrary target in dense remote-sensing imagery, yet existing referring remote-sensing image segmentation benchmarks accept only written expressions. To bridge this gap, we introduce \dataset, a spoken-query benchmark derived from RISBench that adds accent- and voice-diverse speech while preserving the original image, mask, and data splits. Its hard evaluation sets combine rotor, wind, and mixed interference with three signal-to-noise levels. We also propose \model, an efficient bilateral network that combines a boundary-preserving visual path with token-preserving speech encoding, kernel linear cross-modal attention, and a resolution refinement head. The design conditions visual features at two scales without materializing a dense speech--visual affinity matrix, then restores fine boundaries using high-resolution visual features. On the clean test split, \model with Swin-Base achieves 62.09\% mean intersection over union (mIoU) and 68.22\% overall intersection over union (oIoU), outperforming the strongest audio-adapted remote-sensing baseline by 5.38 and 2.08 percentage points, respectively. It retains the best hard-set mIoU at 54.09\%. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first benchmark and model study of full-sentence spoken-query referring segmentation for remote-sensing imagery. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [151] [City Sentinel: A Unified AI-Based Smart Surveillance Framework for Real-Time Multi-Threat Detection Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08887)
*Hanan Syed Shabir,Noor Fatima,Safia Baloch,Masroor Hussain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了City Sentinel，一个统一的AI监控框架，将六种检测能力（人脸识别、车牌识别、火灾烟雾检测、武器刀具检测、暴力检测和交通事故检测）集成到一个可扩展平台中。系统采用Next.js仪表盘、FastAPI后端、云PostgreSQL存储以及InsightFace、YOLOv8和EasyOCR等模型，在RTX 3060 GPU上实现了743 ms的中位延迟，支持四条并发RTSP流，并展示了各检测模块的良好准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速城市化增加了对同时监控多种公共安全风险的需求，而传统系统通常采用分离的解决方案（分别处理人脸识别、车辆识别、火灾检测和行为分析），导致基础设施碎片化、操作员需要管理多个界面，效率低下。

Method: 系统采用模块化多模型架构：以Next.js构建操作员仪表盘，FastAPI作为后端，云PostgreSQL用于事件存储，集成InsightFace（人脸识别）、YOLOv8（火灾/刀具/武器检测等）和EasyOCR（车牌识别）视觉模型，通过RTSP协议处理摄像头流，使用专用推理worker处理并发视频流。

Result: 在RTX 3060工作站上，系统达到743 ms中位端到端延迟，在2秒延迟限制内支持4条并发RTSP流；人脸匹配率91.2%，车牌读取准确率85.7%，火灾、刀具和武器检测模块的mAP@0.5分数在0.846至0.889之间；用户验收测试中，操作员在1分钟内完成新身份注册，平均12秒可从实时画面中识别标记人员。

Conclusion: 模块化、开源的多样模型架构可以提供广泛的监控覆盖、云审计能力和灵活的扩展性，同时保持实用的实时性能，证明了统一AI监控平台在智慧城市公共安全领域的可行性和价值。

Abstract: Rapid urbanization has increased the need for surveillance systems that can monitor multiple public safety risks at the same time. Traditional systems often use separate solutions for facial recognition, vehicle identification, fire detection, and behavioral analysis, resulting in fragmented infrastructure and multiple interfaces for operators to manage. This paper presents City Sentinel, a unified AI-based surveillance framework that integrates six detection capabilities into one scalable platform: facial recognition, automatic number plate recognition (ANPR), fire and smoke detection, weapon and knife detection, violence detection, and road accident detection. The system combines a Next.js operator dashboard, FastAPI backend, cloud-based PostgreSQL event storage, InsightFace and YOLOv8 vision models, and EasyOCR for plate recognition. Camera streams are processed through dedicated inference workers using RTSP. On a workstation equipped with an NVIDIA RTX 3060 GPU, the system achieves a median end-to-end latency of 743 ms and supports four concurrent RTSP streams within a two-second latency limit. It achieves a 91.2% face-match rate, 85.7% plate-reading accuracy, and mAP@0.5 scores of 0.846 to 0.889 across the fire, knife, and weapon detection modules. In user-acceptance testing, operators could enroll a new identity in under one minute and identify a flagged person from live footage in an average of 12 seconds. The results demonstrate that a modular, open-source, multi-model architecture can provide broad surveillance coverage, cloud-based auditability, and flexibility for adding new detection capabilities while maintaining practical real-time performance.

</details>


### [152] [From Recovery to Drop-off: How Action Post-training Reduces a VLM's Late-Layer Depth Decodability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08904)
*Alexander Hackett,Arnaud Denis-Remillard,Axel Cassou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）在动作后训练过程中，其空间理解能力（特别是深度感知）相比基础视觉-语言模型（VLM）如何退化。发现存在两阶段退化："地板"（每层深度解码能力普遍下降）和"悬崖"（最后层深度解码能力急剧崩塌），并将"悬崖"因果定位到晚期MLP写入的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解VLM在构建VLA时动作后训练对空间理解能力的保持程度，这关系到机器人操作等下游应用中模型的几何理解能力是否被牺牲。

Method: 使用权重匹配的开源基础VLM/VLA对（Molmo2-ER和MolmoAct2-LIBERO），从每个解码器层探针深度感知；通过消融实验（晚期MLP写入消融 vs 注意力消融）因果定位退化来源；进行模块级分解分析MLP写入中的深度可解码性。

Result: VLA在每层深度解码能力都更差（"地板"）；且VLA的晚期层深度可解码性崩塌（"悬崖"）。消融晚期MLP写入可恢复大部分"悬崖"，而注意力消融和基础VLM中相同干预无此效果。分解表明基础VLM在累积MLP写入中携带深度信息，而动作后训练破坏了晚期累积写入中的深度可解码性。

Conclusion: 动作后训练以非均匀方式损害VLM的空间理解：普遍性退化（地板）加上晚期层特异性崩塌（悬崖），后者因果定位于晚期MLP写入的干扰，为VLA训练中保护几何能力提供了可操作的干预方向。

Abstract: How much of a vision-language model's (VLM) spatial understanding remains after the action post-training process of building a vision-language-action model (VLA)? We probe depth perception, a primitive of spatiogeometric understanding, from every decoder layer of a weight-matched open-source base VLM/VLA pair: Molmo2-ER and MolmoAct2-LIBERO. First, the VLA decodes depth worse at every layer, a persistent gap we call the floor. Second, the degradation is not uniform: while the base VLM's depth decodability improves through its final layers, the VLA's collapses, an additional late-layer drop we call the cliff. We causally localize the cliff to late-layer MLP interference: ablating the late-layer MLP writes recovers the majority of the terminal decodability cliff, while matched attention ablations and the same intervention in the weight-matched base VLM produce no comparable recovery. A module-level decomposition explains this dissociation: the base VLM carries depth most accessibly in accumulated MLP writes, whereas action post-training collapses depth decodability in the late accumulated writes.

</details>


### [153] [ToolVision: Learning When and How to Use Visual Tools with Capability-Aligned Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08907)
*Delin Mao,Chenghao Sun,Jingwei Song,Chishui Chen,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ToolVision提出了一种两阶段训练方法，分别解决SFT和RL阶段中工具使用监督信号错配的问题，使多模态模型更有效地通过代码调用视觉工具来补偿感知能力的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SFT-then-RL训练范式在工具使用上存在两个阶段性的监督错配：SFT阶段，学生模仿强教师模型的工具调用模式，但可能无法复现教师依赖的感知能力，导致学会调用工具却不会有效利用；RL阶段，仅基于结果的奖励会使工具执行失败成为负担而抑制工具使用，而无差别奖励又鼓励了无效但正确的工具操作。

Method: ToolVision包含两个关键设计：在SFT阶段，采用多智能体pipeline探索候选轨迹，由包含学生尺度模型的委员会对逐步证据增益进行打分，用于排序和剪枝搜索分支，仅保留成功执行且最终答案正确的轨迹用于SFT；在RL之前，ToolVision比较学习者有工具和无工具的表现，只在工具能带来明确收益的问题上对成功的工具使用给予奖励。两个信号均从公开任务数据中自动构建，无需额外的人工标注。

Result: ToolVision-8B在全部七个主要基准上相对其基础模型均有提升，在三个高分辨率基准上超越了Thyme-7B、CodeVision-8B和CodeDance-7B，在V*和HRBench 8K上超过了Qwen3-VL-32B-Thinking。

Conclusion: ToolVision通过分别针对SFT和RL阶段的监督错配设计自动化的信号构建机制，有效提升了多模态模型调用视觉工具进行推理的能力，且无需额外人工标注，将公开数据集和源代码。

Abstract: Thinking with images allows a multimodal model to compensate for limited perception by invoking visual tools through code. Yet the prevailing SFT-then-RL recipe creates a different supervision misalignment at each stage. SFT is expected to teach how to use tools, but trajectories from stronger teachers may succeed through perceptual capabilities that a smaller student cannot reliably reproduce or exploit, causing the student to imitate tool-call patterns without learning how to make them useful. RL is expected to teach when to use tools, but outcome-only rewards make fallible tool execution a liability and suppress tool use, whereas a blanket bonus for every correct tool-using trajectory encourages valid but ineffective operations. To address these two misalignments, we introduce ToolVision. During SFT, a multi-agent pipeline explores candidate trajectories, and a committee including student-scale models scores stepwise evidence gain to rank and prune the search branches. Only successfully executed trajectories with correct final answers are retained for SFT. Before RL, ToolVision compares the learner's performance with and without tools, then rewards successful tool use only on questions where tools provide a clear benefit. Both signals are constructed automatically from public task data without additional human annotations of tool use or necessity. ToolVision-8B improves over its base on all seven main benchmarks, surpasses Thyme-7B, CodeVision-8B, and CodeDance-7B on all three high-resolution benchmarks, and outperforms Qwen3-VL-32B-Thinking on V* and HRBench 8K. We will publicly release the datasets and source code.

</details>


### [154] [From Noise to Meaning: Meaningful Secret Sharing with Tamper Detection for Facial Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08924)
*Ajnas Muhammed,Iurii Medvedev,Nuno Gonçalves*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的面部数据保护方法，将视觉秘密共享产生的噪声状秘密份额通过自适应最低有效位隐写嵌入到有吸引力的封面图像中，并结合数字水印和加密哈希实现双重认证，以保护面部生物特征数据的隐私、安全和完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于AI的人脸识别系统需要保护训练用敏感生物特征数据。现有的视觉秘密共享虽可将面部图像分割为秘密份额，但这些份额像噪声一样容易引起怀疑并被识别为加密内容，容易遭受针对性收集和"先收割后解密"攻击，且无法检测篡改行为。

Method: 该方法与视觉秘密共享配合，引入封面图像，使用自适应最低有效位隐写技术将秘密份额嵌入封面图像中，实现感知透明性和完全隐私保障。同时通过强数字水印和加密哈希构建两层认证机制，保护秘密份额的完整性，抵御比特翻转、裁剪和替换攻击。

Result: 在多个公开人脸数据集上进行的大量实验表明，该技术在人脸识别准确率上表现更好，同时消除了秘密份额的显眼性并保证了完整性，展现出对翻转、裁剪和替换攻击的高弹性。

Conclusion: 该框架为保护面部数据建立了新标准，同时保障了隐私、安全和完整性三个维度。

Abstract: Popularity of AI-based face recognition system directly demands protection of sensitive biometric data used for training. Visual secret sharing is an interesting idea, as it splits facial images into secret shares that look random and spread across many institutions. However, these shares look like noise and can easily spark suspicion and recognized as encrypted content. This makes them open to targeted collection and harvest-now-decrypt-later attacks. Additionally, visual secret sharing does not detect tampering, allowing attackers to modify shares and threaten the integrity of reconstruction. In this paper, we introduce a new method that turns distracting noise-like secret shares into visually appealing cover images with additional cryptographic tamper detection. The proposed technique works with visual secret sharing and introduces cover images to embed the shares using adaptive least significant bit steganography. Here, cover images with perceptual transparency are used to store secret shares while guaranteeing complete privacy. A two layer authentication using strong digital watermarking and cryptographic hashing is used to protect the integrity of shares. The proposed technique shows high resilience in stopping bit-flipping, cropping, and substitution attacks. Extensive experiments on multiple public face datasets show that the technique shows better FR accuracy, while eliminating share conspicuousness and guaranteeing integrity. The proposed framework sets a new standard for protecting facial data in such a way that privacy, security, and integrity are protected.

</details>


### [155] [Zero-shot 2D Grounding with Novel Affordance Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08929)
*Haomeng Zhang,Raymond A. Yeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了零样本2D affordance接地任务，针对训练中未见过的affordance类型进行泛化。作者构建了NAT基准测试集，并提出AffordAnything（无需训练的基于分割线索的方法）及其可训练变体AffordAnything+，在AGD20K-NAT基准上较SOTA方法OOAL在IoU@0.4上提升12.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D affordance接地研究仅关注训练中见过的affordance类型，缺乏对未见affordance类型泛化能力的研究，而这在实际应用中至关重要。作者旨在填补这一空白，提出零样本新affordance类型接地任务。

Method: 提出NAT基准测试集（AGD20K-NAT）；提出AffordAnything——一种无需训练的方法，利用affordance区域与物体子部分之间的强相关性，通过分割线索进行接地；在此基础上开发AffordAnything+可训练变体，学习如何组合这些分割线索以进一步提升性能。

Result: 在AGD20K-NAT基准上，最佳模型AffordAnything+在IoU@0.4指标上较现有SOTA方法OOAL取得了12.3%的绝对提升。

Conclusion: 利用分割线索可以有效地将affordance接地扩展到未见过的affordance类型，实现零样本泛化，且可训练变体能够进一步提升性能。

Abstract: 2D affordance grounding aims to locate the region of an object that a human can interact with. Existing research focuses on recognizing affordance types seen during training and does not study models' ability to generalize to novel affordances, which is crucial for real-world applications. We propose the task of zero-shot 2D grounding with novel affordance types (NAT) and introduce the NAT benchmarks. We then propose AffordAnything, a training-free method that leverages segmentation cues, motivated by the strong correlation between affordance regions and object subparts. To further improve performance, we develop AffordAnything+, a trainable variant that learns to combine these cues. On the proposed AGD20K-NAT benchmark, our best model AffordAnything+ achieves a substantial improvement of 12.3% (absolute) in IoU@0.4 over the SOTA affordance grounding method, OOAL.

</details>


### [156] [Can Webcam Gaze Constrain Mesa-Objectives in Driving Models? An Instrument Precision Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08947)
*Lennox Anderson,Ahmed Boutar,Jonah Mulcrone,Tal Erez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动驾驶危险检测系统中是否可以通过人类注视模式（网络摄像头眼动追踪）来约束模型学习过程中的"伪目标"（mesa-objective）形成。通过收集137,663帧注视数据，在两种校准协议、两种模型架构和五个随机种子下进行实验，结果显示注视信息没有带来统计显著的性能提升。几何分析表明，WebGazer的误差范围（130-257像素）超过了93%的危险目标物体尺寸（中位数36像素），在仪器精度层面使得目标级别的注视归因无法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的危险检测系统可能发展出"mesa-objectives"——即通过学习虚假相关性而非真正的危险识别来达到高训练性能的内部目标。研究者希望探索人类注视模式能否作为特权信息（privileged information）来约束这种伪目标的形成，从而提高系统的真实危险识别能力。

Method: 收集了388个真实行车记录仪片段中的137,663帧级注视样本，并将其与危险标注同步。实验设计包含两个校准协议（9点/45点击和11点/440点击）、两种模型架构（随机森林和因果Transformer）和五个随机种子，使用配对t检验评估统计显著性。同时进行了几何分析来验证注视归因的物理可行性。

Result: 所有实验均未产生统计显著的性能提升（p值分别为0.919、0.578和0.667）。几何分析揭示了根本原因：WebGazer的报告误差（约130-257像素，取决于配置）超过了93%的危险目标物体尺寸（中位数仅36像素），导致在仪器精度层面无法实现目标级别的注视归因。

Conclusion: WebGazer.js基于网络摄像头的眼动追踪精度不足以支撑自动驾驶场景中目标级别的注视归因，因此无法有效约束mesa-objective的形成。这为未来研究提供了重要启示：需要更高精度的眼动追踪设备（如专用眼动仪），或在模型设计中考虑注视信息的固有噪声。

Abstract: Current hazard detection systems in autonomous driving may develop mesa objectives, learned internal goals that achieve high training performance through spurious correlations rather than genuine hazard recognition. We investigate whether human gaze patterns, captured via webcam-based eye tracking (WebGazer.js), can serve as privileged information to constrain mesa-objective formation. We collected 137,663 frame-level gaze samples synchronized with hazard annotations across 388 real dashcam clips, then test this hypothesis across two calibration protocols (9-point/45-click and 11-point/440-click), two model architectures (Random Forest and causal Transformer), and five random seeds per experiment with paired t-tests. No experiment yields a statistically significant improvement from gaze (p = 0.919, 0.578, and 0.667 respectively). A geometric analysis reveals the root cause: WebGazer's reported error (~130-257 px depending on configuration) exceeds 93% of detected hazard object sizes (median 36 px), rendering object-level gaze attribution physically impossible at this instrument precision.

</details>


### [157] [EndoMD-SLAM: Endoscopic Gaussian Splatting SLAM under Optical Degradation with Memory and Static-Transient Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08949)
*Nuo Chen,Kangqi Ni,Lulin Liu,Joga Ivatury,Ying Ding,Farshid Alambeigi,Tianlong Chen,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EndoMD-SLAM是一个专为内窥镜光学退化场景设计的稠密3D重建SLAM框架，通过记忆驱动的门控机制和自监督静态-瞬态分解，分别解决跟踪漂移和地图污染问题，在严重光学退化的结肠镜视频中降低91%轨迹误差并提升9.9dB渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯泼溅SLAM系统依赖严格的多视角光度一致性假设，但在常规内镜手术中，移动碎屑和水冲洗等间歇性光学退化会严重破坏这一假设，导致系统将相机附着伪影错误融合进持久3D几何中，造成跟踪漂移和不可逆的地图损坏。

Method: 提出EndoMD-SLAM框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 跟踪端采用记忆驱动的门控机制，检测不可靠观测并暂停地图更新，利用历史关键帧实现漂移感知的重定位；(2) 建图端采用自监督的静态-瞬态分解，将视觉污染物隔离到专门的瞬态场中，防止伪影与持久解剖地图结构纠缠。

Result: 在从结肠镜视频构建的退化聚焦基准上进行广泛实验表明，标准基线在严重光学退化下失败，而EndoMD-SLAM保持了几何完整性，绝对轨迹误差降低91%，渲染保真度提升9.9dB PSNR。

Conclusion: EndoMD-SLAM通过显式区分静态解剖结构与瞬态光学伪影，有效解决了内窥镜SLAM在光学退化条件下的稳定性问题，为临床内镜导航和文档记录的稠密3D重建提供了鲁棒方案。

Abstract: Dense 3D reconstruction is critical for clinical endoscopic navigation and documentation. While Gaussian Splatting SLAM systems show promise in this domain, they fundamentally rely on strict multi-view photometric consistency. In routine procedures, this assumption is severely violated by intermittent optical degradations like moving debris and water flushing. Standard systems erroneously fuse these cameraattached artifacts into the persistent 3D geometry, causing severe tracking drift and irreversible map corruption. To address this limitation, we propose EndoMD-SLAM, a framework designed to maintain stability under optical degradation through specialized tracking and mapping mechanisms. On the tracking side, a memory-driven gating mechanism detects unreliable observations to suspend map updates and utilizes historical keyframes for drift-aware relocalization. On the mapping side, a self-supervised static-transient decomposition isolates visual contaminants into a dedicated transient field. This explicit separation prevents artifacts from structurally entangling with the persistent anatomical map. We curate a degradationfocused benchmark from colonoscopy videos to systematically evaluate these failure modes. Extensive experiments show that while standard baselines fail under severe optical degradation, EndoMD-SLAM preserves geometric integrity, reducing absolute trajectory error by 91% and improving rendering fidelity by 9.9 dB PSNR.

</details>
