Subject-driven generation aims to synthesize customized images to mimic the appearance of subjects in given reference images within different visual contexts specified by the text prompts. The central challenge here is that, when the reference image changes, the diffusion model cannot efficiently adapt to different visual contexts while consistently maintaining the subject identity. Existing methods either train the model with a large domain-specific dataset or fine-tune the model using the reference image for hundreds of iterations before actual image generation. In this work, we explore a new approach, called \textit{In-Loop Model Adaptation} (IMA), which adapts the core diffusion model at each generation step during the actual process of image generation, without being trained on the reference image before the generation process. To this end, we establish a DDIM inversion chain that maps the reference image to a sequence of latent, as well as a text-to-image generation chain which generates the image from the text prompt only. We then introduce a masked latent consistency loss and a noise regularization loss to characterize the latent-noise difference between the diffusion model and these two chains at each generation step. This coupled latent-noise loss is used to guide the in-loop model adaptation to preserve the subject identity specified by the reference image while maintaining accurate alignment with the text prompt, resulting in high-fidelity text-to-image generation. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed IMA method significantly improves the performance of subject-driven text-to-image generation.

</details>


### [192] [Task-Adaptive 3D Cross-Field MRI Translation via Field-Conditioned Content-Style Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09264)
*Haowen Pang,Yingqi Hao,Pengli Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于场条件内容-风格预训练的3D无配对跨场MRI翻译框架，用于解决不同磁场强度（0.1T至7T）下MRI图像之间的域偏移问题，并通过解剖内容与场依赖对比特征的解耦来实现可控的场到场翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场强度是MRI中域偏移的主要来源，影响信噪比、组织对比度、空间细节和解剖边界可见性。由于同一受试者跨多个场强的配对采集数据很少，无配对跨场翻译极具挑战性。

Method: 提出3D无配对跨场MRI翻译框架，包含3D内容编码器、3D风格编码器、场条件风格生成器、AdaIN调制解码器和多场判别器。通过对抗学习、循环一致性、身份、内容、风格和多样性约束实现解剖保真与可控翻译。先在所有场强上进行预训练，再适配到具体任务目标域。

Result: 在MRIxFields数据集上跨越五个场强和三种MRI模态进行实验，配对测试数据表明该框架能够适应三个挑战设置（Any-to-7T、0.1T-to-High、Any-to-Any），同时保持合成体数据中的三维解剖结构。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于场条件内容-风格预训练的框架能有效解决跨场MRI翻译问题，在无配对训练条件下实现高保真的多场强图像合成，代码已开源。

Abstract: Magnetic field strength is a major source of domain shift in magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), affecting signal-to-noise ratio, tissue contrast, spatial detail, and the visibility of anatomical boundaries. The MRIxFields 2026 challenge investigates this problem through cross-field MRI translation across acquisitions at 0.1T, 1.5T, 3T, 5T, and 7T. Its three tasks, Any-to-7T, 0.1T-to-High, and Any-to-Any synthesis, require the generation of target-field image characteristics while preserving subject-specific anatomy. This problem is particularly challenging because paired acquisitions of the same subject across multiple field strengths are rarely available for training. We propose a 3D unpaired cross-field MRI translation framework based on field-conditioned content-style pretraining. The proposed framework first learns controllable field-to-field translation across all available field strengths by disentangling anatomical content from field-dependent contrast characteristics. The pretrained backbone is then adapted to task-specific target domains. Our model comprises a 3D content encoder, a 3D style encoder, a field-conditioned style generator, an AdaIN-modulated decoder, and a multi-field discriminator. Adversarial learning encourages realistic target-field appearance, while cycle-consistency, identity, content, style, and diversity constraints promote anatomical fidelity and controllable translation. We evaluate the proposed method on MRIxFields data spanning five field strengths and three MRI modalities. Experiments on paired test data demonstrate that the framework can adapt to the three challenge settings while preserving three-dimensional anatomical structure in the synthesized volumes. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/Idea89560041/3D-MRI-Field-Translation.

</details>


### [193] [Did the Grid Erase the Event? EndoClock for Auditing Medical World-Model Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09266)
*Yarin Udi,Tom Sharon-Shahak,Roee Masad,Dan Pri-Tal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出医学世界模型在将多模态录音同步到固定时间网格时，可能因内源采样时钟而抹除任务关键证据。作者提出四机制分类法，并开发EndoClock作为预训练审计工具，用于识别证据存留的最低位表示，并通过超声心动图案例展示了这一失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态医学数据的预处理通常将各流同步到固定速率网格上，但当观测时钟依赖于潜在状态或采集状态（即内源时钟）时，同步可能并非中性操作，会抹除模型需要的任务相关证据，这可能导致下游诊断任务失败。

Method: 提出一个四机制分类法来刻画区分目标事件或状态所需证据的存留位置：采样值、网格单元更新模式、原生时间轴，或仅存在于外部采集通道。基于该分类法，实现EndoClock作为保守的预训练审计工具，报告由可用证据支持的最低位见证表示，或在无法判定时标记为未解决。

Result: 在超声心动图场景中，B模式视频写入在脉冲波多普勒采集期间停止，而对应的测量事件仅记录在外部采集日志中，该案例清晰展示了同步化可能造成的证据丢失。

Conclusion: 这是一项初步的失败预警和可执行审计工具，其实践建议是保留原生观测过程足够长的时间，以确定同步化是否已抹除目标任务所需的信息。

Abstract: Medical world models commonly learn from multimodal recordings synchronized onto a fixed-rate grid. This preprocessing resamples each native stream onto a shared time axis. Each stream has an observation clock that governs when observations are emitted or updated. When this clock depends on the latent or acquisition state, it is endogenous. In such settings, synchronization may not be neutral and can erase task-relevant evidence before the model sees the data. We introduce a four-regime taxonomy that characterizes where the evidence needed to distinguish a target event or state survives. The relevant witness may remain in the sampled values, in grid-cell update patterns, in native timing, or only in an external acquisition channel. EndoClock operationalizes this taxonomy as a conservative pretraining audit. It reports the lowest witness-bearing representation supported by the available evidence, or unresolved when no regime can be established. We illustrate this failure in echocardiography, where B-mode video write-outs cease during pulsed-wave Doppler acquisition while the corresponding measurement events remain recorded only in an external acquisition log. This work is a preliminary failure alert and executable audit. Its practical message is to preserve the native observation process long enough to determine whether synchronization has erased information required by the intended task.

</details>


### [194] [GRASP: Granularity-Aware Region Alignment and Semantic Prototype Learning for Fine-Grained Cross-Modal Understanding in Drone Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09270)
*Jiahui Cui,Yan Zhao,Kan Wei,Enze Zhu,Peirong Zhang,Lei Wang,Yiru Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GRASP学习框架，通过区域聚焦对齐（RFA）和语义扰动增强匹配（SPEM）两种协同策略，解决无人机视角下跨模态理解中背景干扰导致的焦点错位和视觉同构带来的细粒度歧义问题，在GeoText-1652基准和未见ERA数据集上取得竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机场景的宽视野和俯视角特性给视觉-语言理解带来双重挑战：宏观层面背景杂波导致跨模态焦点错位，模型过度关注全局环境相似性而忽视具体物体细节；微观层面视觉同构使候选物体结构相似但细微属性不同，产生歧义。

Method: 提出GRASP（粒度感知区域对齐与语义原型）学习框架，包含两个协同策略：(1) Region-Focused Alignment (RFA)促进以对象为中心的跨模态对齐并抑制背景干扰；(2) Semantic Perturbation Enhanced Matching (SPEM)利用前景纯化语义原型码本（SPC）构建语义扰动负样本，实现细粒度语义判别。

Result: 在GeoText-1652基准和未见ERA数据集上的广泛实验表明，GRASP在无人机视角细粒度图文检索中取得了竞争性性能，验证了其在航空场景跨模态理解中的有效性。

Conclusion: GRASP框架有效解决了无人机视角下跨模态理解的两大核心挑战（焦点错位和视觉同构），代码已公开，为航空场景细粒度视觉-语言导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Fine-grained cross-modal understanding in drone views is essential for aerial vision-language navigation. However, the inherent wide field of view and overhead perspective of drone scenarios impose dual challenges on vision-language understanding. At the macro level, overwhelming background clutter in visual representations leads to Cross-Modal Focus Misalignment, where the model prioritizes global environmental similarities over specific object details. At the micro level, Visual Isomorphism creates ambiguity, where candidates share similar geometric structures yet differ only in subtle attributes. To address these challenges, we propose the Granularity-Aware Region Alignment and Semantic Prototype (GRASP) learning framework, enhancing discriminative capability through two synergistic strategies. Specifically, we introduce Region-Focused Alignment (RFA) to promote object-centric cross-modal alignment while suppressing background interference. Concurrently, to tackle visual isomorphism, we propose Semantic Perturbation Enhanced Matching (SPEM), which leverages a foreground-purified Semantic Prototype Codebook (SPC) to construct semantically perturbed negatives for fine-grained semantic discrimination. Extensive experiments on the GeoText-1652 benchmark and the unseen ERA dataset demonstrate that GRASP achieves competitive performance in drone-view fine-grained image-text retrieval, validating its effectiveness for cross-modal understanding in aerial scenarios. Our code implementation is available at https://github.com/UCAS-JC/GRASP.

</details>


### [195] [UniDFKD: A Unified Semantic Prior Framework for Architecture-Agnostic Data-Free Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09287)
*Xuewan He,Tong Chu,Zihan Cheng,Yuchen Su,Qianxin Xia,Guoming Lu,Jielei Wang,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniDFKD，一种统一的数据无关知识蒸馏框架，用显式、架构无关的语义先验替代架构特定的统计先验（如Batch Normalization统计量），通过三个维度（类别语义条件、空间语义锚定、空间语义蒸馏）控制合成-蒸馏全过程，在CNN和ViT的同类及异类蒸馏中均超越现有方法20%以上的绝对精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DFKD方法依赖架构特定的统计先验（如BN统计量）指导数据合成，但在现代架构（如ViT）中这些先验往往缺失，导致合成数据的语义质量下降，进而造成灾难性的性能退化。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：(1) 类别语义条件（CSC）——用语言派生嵌入持续调制生成器，定义"合成什么"；(2) 空间语义锚定（SSA）——将教师的空间归因锚定到高斯先验，决定"证据在哪里"；(3) 空间语义蒸馏（SSD）——显式对齐师生空间证据和预测，控制"知识如何传递"。

Result: 在CNN和ViT上的大量实验表明，UniDFKD在同类和异类蒸馏设置下均取得新的最优结果，相对现有方法平均绝对精度提升超过20%。

Conclusion: UniDFKD证明了用架构无关的语义先验替代架构相关的统计先验是可行的，能够统一适用于CNN和ViT等异构架构，显著提升数据无关知识蒸馏的性能。

Abstract: Data-Free Knowledge Distillation (DFKD) transfers knowledge from a pretrained teacher model to a compact student model by synthesizing semantically informative data, eliminating the need for access to the original training dataset. Existing DFKD methods rely heavily on architecture-specific statistical priors (e.g., Batch Normalization statistics) to guide data synthesis, however, such architecture-dependent priors are often absent in modern architectures such as Vision Transformers (ViTs), resulting in degraded semantic quality of the synthesized data and consequently catastrophic performance degradation. In this paper, we propose \emph{UniDFKD}, a unified data-free knowledge distillation framework that replaces architecture-specific statistics with explicit, architecture-agnostic semantic priors. \emph{UniDFKD} governs the entire synthesis-distillation pipeline along three dimensions: (1) Categorical Semantic Conditioning (CSC) defines \emph{what} to synthesize by persistently modulating the generator with language-derived embeddings to capture semantic diversity; (2) Spatial Semantic Anchoring (SSA) dictates \emph{where} evidence belongs by anchoring the teacher's spatial attributions to a Gaussian prior; and (3) Spatial Semantic Distillation (SSD) controls \emph{how} knowledge is transferred by explicitly aligning teacher-student spatial evidence alongside predictions. Extensive experiments across CNNs and ViTs demonstrate that UniDFKD establishes a new state-of-the-art, outperforming existing methods by an average absolute margin of over 20\% in both homogeneous and heterogeneous settings.

</details>


### [196] [Bootstrapping Vision-Language Model for Hysteroscopic Surgical Scene Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09302)
*Jun Huang,Meiyi Chen,Zijie Yue,Yuhang Xiao,Fang Li,Hanli Wang,Xiaowen Tong,Yi Guo,Miaojing Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-hyster，首个基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的宫腔镜手术场景分割方法，实现对15个类别的像素级定位。该方法利用预训练图像编码器和Transformer解码器构建分割骨干网络，设计类别特定文本提示并结合掩码蒸馏分支过滤低相关视觉特征，以增强分割性能。作者收集了包含4,020张高分辨率图像的多中心宫腔镜手术数据集，实验表明VLM-hyster显著优于现有SOTA模型，并通过妇科医生评估、多中心及前瞻性验证证明了其鲁棒性和泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宫腔镜手术场景分割对计算机辅助干预至关重要，但面临病灶间形态高度相似以及手术视频中镜面反射、运动模糊和液体遮挡等伪影带来的独特挑战，现有方法难以有效解决这些问题。

Method: 提出基于VLM的宫腔镜手术场景分割框架，包含：1）利用预训练图像编码器进行鲁棒视觉特征提取的分割骨干，搭配Transformer解码器进行密集预测；2）设计类别特定文本提示机制；3）引入掩码蒸馏分支过滤与文本提示相关性低的视觉特征，使模型聚焦于类别特定图像区域。同时收集并标注了包含4,020张高分辨率图像的多中心数据集用于训练和评估。

Result: 实验结果显示VLM-hyster在分割性能上大幅超越现有SOTA AI模型；通过妇科医生专业评估以及多中心和前瞻性验证，证明了该方法具备良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: VLM-hyster展现出了在宫腔镜手术中实现AI辅助定位手术器械和病灶方面的显著潜力，为计算机辅助宫腔镜手术提供了可行方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Hysteroscopic surgical scene segmentation plays a pivotal role in understanding the hysteroscopic intraoperative environment as well as computer-assisted intervention. However, this task presents unique challenges due to the high morphological similarity among different lesions and the presence of artifacts such as specular reflections, motion blur, and fluid occlusions in surgical videos. In this work, we propose the first vision-language model (VLM)-based hysteroscopic surgical scene segmentation method, which performs pixel-wise localization for fifteen representative categories in hysteroscopic surgical scenes. Our VLM-hyster has a segmentation backbone that utilizes the pretrained image encoder for robust visual feature extraction, coupled with a transformer-based decoder for dense prediction. Moreover, we design category-specific text prompts and incorporate a masked distillation branch to filter out visual features with low correlation to the text prompts, enabling the model to focus more effectively on category-specific image regions and thereby enhancing segmentation performance. We collect a large multicentric hysteroscopic surgical scene dataset, containing 4,020 high-resolution images with detailed mask annotations, for model training and evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that VLM-hyster substantially outperforms state-of-the-art AI models. Furthermore, extensive assessments by gynecologists, as well as multicentre and prospective validations, demonstrate VLM-hyster's robustness and generalizability. The results suggest that VLM-hyster earns considerable potential in enabling AI-assisted localization of surgical instruments and lesions in hysteroscopic surgeries. Code is available at https://github.com/viscom-tongji/VLM-hyster.

</details>


### [197] [Degraded Infrared Small Object Detection via Degradation-Adapted Physics-Guided Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09311)
*Xinkai Lu,Wenjun Chen,Yi Li,Yi Chang,Luxin Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAISOD框架，用于在不同退化条件下（如雾和非均匀性）的鲁棒红外小目标检测。该框架先识别退化类型和严重程度，再通过专用分支自适应处理，最终融合结果进行检测，并引入物理引导的复原机制避免过度复原导致小目标丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外小目标检测方法在面对雾、非均匀性等退化时性能下降，而基于图像复原的预处理方法通常只针对特定退化类型设计，泛化能力差，无法应对多样的退化场景。

Method: DAISOD包含三个核心步骤：首先识别退化类型和严重程度；然后通过专用分支自适应处理不同退化；最后融合各分支结果用于后续检测。同时引入物理引导的复原机制，显式估计退化参数并通过物理模型去除退化影响，避免过度复原导致小目标被消除。

Result: 构建了涵盖多种退化类型和程度的退化红外小目标检测数据集，大量实验表明DAISOD在各种退化条件下均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: DAISOD通过退化自适应处理策略和物理引导复原机制，有效提升了红外小目标检测在多种退化条件下的鲁棒性，为该领域提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Infrared small object detection has made significant progress in recent years. However, degradations such as fog and nonuniformity can suppress target-background contrast, substantially increasing detection difficulty. Existing methods mainly rely on image restoration as preprocessing, but they are typically designed for specific degradation types and fail to generalize to varying degradations. To alleviate this, we propose DAISOD, a degradation-adapted infrared small object detection framework for robust detection under different degradations. DAISOD first identifies the type and severity of degradations, then adapts the processing via dedicated branches, and finally fuses the results for subsequent detection. Moreover, a physics-guided restoration mechanism is incorporated to explicitly estimate degradation parameters and remove degradation effects through physical models, avoiding excessive restoration that may erase small targets. Moreover, we construct a degraded infrared small object detection dataset covering diverse degradation types and levels. Extensive experiments show that DAISOD outperforms state-of-the-art methods under various degradation conditions.

</details>


### [198] [MemeMind: Reference-Guided Trace Construction for Offline Context Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09316)
*Run Yang,Weihang Wang,Boheng Sheng,Yuchen He,Jielei Zhang,Pengyu Chen,Zhiyu Wu,Qiang Sun,Huyang Sun,Longwen Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MemeMind框架，通过离线参考答案恢复失败轨迹，构建成功的工具使用经验来优化离线上下文。在动漫、漫画和游戏梗图解读任务上，相比最强基线提升22.0%、21.1%（30B模型）和8.1%、8.0%（235B模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线上下文优化依赖适配集上的轨迹数据，但部分查询只产生失败轨迹，优化器无法从中获得成功使用工具的正确示例，导致学习效果受限。

Method: MemeMind包含两个核心组件：TraceBuilder利用离线参考答案识别所需证据，通过文本搜索、图像检索和视觉定位执行工具调用，并验证工具轨迹后加入适配缓冲区；ToolGuide将收集到的轨迹汇总为共享指南和各工具的独立指令。参考答案仅在适配阶段使用，推理时使用冻结模型配合学习到的指南。

Result: 在MemeX基准（1000个专家标注的梗图）上，两种Qwen3-VL模型和两种语言分区下，MemeMind较最强上下文优化基线提升22.0%/21.1%（Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B）和8.1%/8.0%（Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B）。消融实验显示，为失败组构建成功工具使用提供最大增益。

Conclusion: 为失败轨迹构建成功的工具使用经验是离线上下文优化的关键增益来源，MemeMind通过在适配阶段利用离线参考答案恢复缺失经验，显著提升了推理时的证据获取效率。

Abstract: Offline context optimization improves an agent by revising its instructions and examples while keeping the model frozen. This approach learns from rollouts on an adaptation set, but some queries produce only failed rollouts. In these cases, the optimizer sees no successful example of how the available tools can reach the correct answer. We introduce MemeMind, which uses an offline reference answer to recover this missing experience. TraceBuilder identifies the evidence required by the reference, executes text search, image retrieval, and visual grounding, and verifies the resulting tool trace before adding it to the adaptation buffer. ToolGuide then summarizes the collected traces into a shared guide and separate instructions for each tool. The reference answers and constructed traces are used only during adaptation, while inference uses the learned guides with a frozen model. We study this problem through Anime, Comic, and Game meme interpretation. These memes combine edited and ambiguous visual content, overlaid text, long tail franchise knowledge, and culture specific references. Their interpretation can require coordinated visual grounding, image retrieval, and text search, making them a demanding setting in which native rollout groups may fail together. We evaluate MemeMind on MemeX, a benchmark of 1,000 such memes annotated by experts. Across two Qwen3-VL models, two language partitions, and two independent judges, MemeMind improves over the strongest context optimization baseline by 22.0% and 21.1% on Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B, and by 8.1% and 8.0% on Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B under GPT-5 judging. Ablations and held out traces show that constructing successful tool use for failed groups provides the largest component gain and produces more effective evidence acquisition at inference time.

</details>


### [199] [Warp-free Cross-view Geo-localization via Feature-space Consensus Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09321)
*Zhuo Song,Lian Xu,Runqing Jiang,Yongjian Zhang,Kunhong Li,Ye Zhang,Yulan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合视角共识引导的学习框架，完全绕开显式几何变换，在特征空间内动态挖掘并增强跨视图语义共识，通过辅助联合视角通路和全局模式探针将单视图嵌入拉向联合视角锚点，实现跨视图地理定位。在四个标准基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视图地理定位方法依赖几何变换来暴露共视线索，但这类变换基于限制性的空间假设，在视图依赖的可见性下会引入严重视觉畸变，导致噪声监督和脆弱对应关系。作者旨在绕过几何变换，直接挖掘特征空间中的语义共识。

Method: 提出联合视角共识引导学习框架：(1) 训练期间引入辅助联合视角通路，实现直接的跨视图交互，每视图选择性聚合佐证信息形成统一的共识表示；(2) 设计全局模式探针作为语义字典，将单视图与联合视图的不同模态投影到严格对齐的度量空间；(3) 采用共识介导的对比目标，训练时将单视图嵌入向联合视图锚点拉近，将共识挖掘能力蒸馏到单视图编码器中用于推理阶段。

Result: 在四个标准基准（跨视图地理定位数据集）上达到最先进的性能，验证了发现跨视图语义共识对于可靠地理定位的重要性。

Conclusion: 通过完全绕开显式几何变换、在特征空间内动态挖掘语义共识的方式，可以有效解决视角差异大和外观偏差显著带来的跨视图地理定位挑战，为跨视图匹配提供了一条更鲁棒的新路径。

Abstract: Cross-view geo-localization is challenging due to drastic viewpoint changes and large appearance discrepancies between street-level and satellite imagery. Although existing methods often use geometric warping to expose co-visible cues, such transformations rely on restrictive spatial assumptions and inevitably introduce severe visual distortions under view-dependent visibility, yielding noisy supervision and fragile correspondences. To overcome this, we propose a novel joint-view consensus-guided learning framework that entirely bypasses explicit geometric warping. Instead of forcing rigid spatial alignment, we dynamically mine and adaptively strengthen a semantic consensus directly within the feature space. Specifically, an auxiliary joint-view pathway during training enables direct cross-view interaction, allowing each view to selectively aggregate corroborative evidence into a unified consensus representation. To resolve feature heterogeneity among the single- and joint-view streams, we introduce global pattern probes acting as a semantic dictionary to project divergent modalities into a strictly aligned metric space. Guided by a consensus-mediated contrastive objective, single-view embeddings are explicitly pulled toward the joint-view anchor during training, distilling this consensus-mining capability into the single-view encoders for robust retrieval at inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across four standard benchmarks, underscoring the importance of discovering cross-view semantic consensus for reliable geo-localization.

</details>


### [200] [Diffusion Image Editing via Asynchronous Token Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09322)
*Yang Shi,Liangsi Lu,Minzhe Guo,Yifeng Xie,Yanhui Chen,Jingchao Wang,Xuhang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ATDEdit，一个推理时文本引导扩散图像编辑框架，通过异步token解码实现局部编辑与背景保持，无需外部掩码或模型微调。在PIE-Bench上取得了最强的保持指标（PSNR 27.44 dB，LPIPS 0.055），同时保持有竞争力的语义对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导扩散图像编辑中，简单切换文本条件会导致全局漂移问题：去噪动态会将token之间的变化传播开来，破坏未编辑区域的原始内容。现有的方法难以在保持图像身份、布局和背景的同时实现语义属性修改，需要一种无需外部掩码且不依赖模型微调的解决方案。

Method: ATDEdit将每个采样步骤视为全局耦合token矩阵的并行更新，实现token索引的条件切换与差异化更新策略。具体包括：1) 利用token级条件惊讶度（conditional surprisal）估计可编辑位置；2) 仅对选中的token集合应用目标条件校正；3) 在保持token位置提供源key/value记忆；4) 将选中的保持token潜在行投影回源值以促进背景保持。整个过程无需外部或用户提供的空间掩码，也无需模型微调。

Result: 在PIE-Bench基准上，ATDEdit取得了最强的保持指标，包括27.44 dB的PSNR和0.055的LPIPS，同时在语义对齐上保持有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: ATDEdit表明，通过同步性token解码与差异化更新策略，可以在无需外部掩码和模型微调的情况下有效兼顾局部编辑精度与背景保持，为文本引导扩散编辑提供了一种新的推理时优化范式。

Abstract: Text-guided diffusion image editing aims to modify semantic attributes of an image while preserving its identity, layout, and background. However, naïvely switching the text condition during sampling often causes global drift, as denoising dynamics propagate changes across tokens and can disrupt unedited regions. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{A}synchronous \textbf{T}oken \textbf{D}ecoding \textbf{Edit} (ATDEdit), an inference-time framework that views each sampler step as a parallel update of a globally coupled token matrix and enables token-indexed condition switching with differentiated update policies. Instead of applying synchronous target-conditioned updates to all tokens, ATDEdit estimates editable locations using token-wise conditional surprisal and applies target-conditioned corrections to the selected token set. It supplies source key/value memory at keep-token positions and projects selected keep-token latent rows back to their source values; these operations promote background preservation but do not constitute a pixel-level invariance guarantee. This approach combines local editing and background preservation without external or user-provided spatial masks and without model fine-tuning. On PIE-Bench, ATDEdit achieves the strongest reported preservation metrics, including 27.44~dB PSNR and 0.055 LPIPS, while retaining competitive semantic alignment.

</details>


### [201] [Revisiting the Current Frame: Physical-Trace-Guided Network Output Correction for Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09342)
*Yifeng Lin,Liuxiang Qiu,Guangming Ren,Tiesong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了ANCHOR框架，一个与模型无关的视频恢复校正方法，利用低质量当前帧作为时间对齐锚点，通过空间信任场自适应平衡恢复结果与原始观测，在HDR重建和视频去雨任务中一致提升多种SOTA模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频恢复方法在时间信息利用上存在参考帧退化不一致、内容差异和重建误差等问题，但已有工作主要聚焦于改进恢复网络本身，忽略了输出结果在不同空间位置上的可靠性评估。

Method: 提出ANCHOR框架，将低质量当前帧视为时间对齐锚点，从异构物理痕迹证据中估计空间信任场，并基于该信任场自适应地在恢复提案与原始观测之间进行平衡校正。

Result: 在HDR视频重建和视频去雨两个任务上的实验表明，ANCHOR能够为多种最先进的恢复模型带来一致的性能提升，验证了可靠性感知输出校正的有效性。

Conclusion: ANCHOR作为模型无关的后处理校正框架，通过可靠性感知的空间信任估计有效提升了视频恢复的质量，为视频恢复领域提供了一个新的研究方向。

Abstract: Video restoration methods exploit temporal information to recover information missing from degraded observations. However, reference frames within the sequence may introduce inconsistent degradation, content discrepancy, or reconstruction errors due to physical image-formation variations, occlusion, and imperfect temporal aggregation. Existing approaches mainly focus on improving restoration networks, while the reliability of the generated outputs at different spatial locations remains largely unexplored. In this work, we propose ANCHOR, a model-agnostic framework that revisits the low-quality current frame as a temporally aligned anchor for video restoration correction. Specifically, ANCHOR estimates a spatial trust field from heterogeneous physical-trace evidence and adaptively balances the restoration proposal with the original observation. Experiments on High Dynamic Range video reconstruction and video deraining demonstrate consistent improvements across various state-of-the-art restoration models, validating the effectiveness of reliability-aware output correction for video restoration.

</details>


### [202] [Beyond Global Editing: Per-Instance Disentangled Subspaces for Training-Free Hallucination Mitigation in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09344)
*Ali Cheraghian,Hamidreza Dastmalchi,Hamed Barzamini,Morteza Saberi,Mojtaba Golzan,Shafin Rahman,Hossein Rahmani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的幻觉缓解框架，通过构建解纠缠幻觉子空间，在测试时动态计算各子空间权重，进行按实例的自适应投影，以抑制大视觉语言模型（LVLM）生成中的幻觉，同时保留图像相关语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型（LVLM）在推理时经常产生幻觉，导致生成文本与视觉输入不符。现有缓解方法中，微调计算成本高且需要大规模标注数据；解码类方法虽无需训练但计算开销大、不稳定；已有模型编辑方法依赖单一全局子空间，无法捕捉不同输入之间的多样化幻觉模式。

Method: 该方法包含两个阶段：首先构建一组解纠缠幻觉子空间（Disentangled Hallucination Subspaces），每个子空间对应一种独立的幻觉模式；在推理阶段，模型根据每个输入与这些子空间的关系自适应计算权重，生成动态组合投影，选择性抑制最可能的幻觉方向，同时保留与图像相关的语义信息。

Result: 在多个视觉语言基准测试和多个LVLM家族上进行了广泛实验，结果表明该方法在幻觉抑制方面取得了一致性的性能提升，验证了其鲁棒性、泛化性和效率优势。

Conclusion: 本文提出的训练免费、按实例动态抑制幻觉的框架，相较于依赖单一全局子空间的现有模型编辑方法，能更精细地匹配不同输入的幻觉模式，在保持效率的同时显著提升幻觉缓解效果，具有较强的普适性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Recent advances in large vision-language models (LVLMs) have enabled powerful multimodal reasoning by integrating visual encoders with large language models (LLMs). However, their reliability is frequently undermined by hallucinations, where generated text inaccurately describes the visual input. Although fine-tuning can mitigate this problem, it is computationally expensive and requires large, curated datasets, making training-free alternatives attractive. Among these, model editing is more promising than decoding-based approaches: decoding methods adapt outputs per input but introduce computational overhead and instability, whereas model editing modifies internal representations offline, providing a more efficient and stable solution. However, existing model-editing techniques typically rely on a single global subspace to correct hallucinations, treating all test samples identically and failing to capture diverse hallucination modes across inputs. To address this limitation, we propose a training-free hallucination mitigation framework for dynamic, per-instance suppression at test time. Our method first constructs a set of Disentangled Hallucination Subspaces, each isolating a distinct hallucination mode. During inference, the model adaptively calculates weights reflecting each input's relationship to these subspaces, guiding a dynamically combined projection that selectively suppresses the most probable hallucination directions while preserving image-grounded semantics. Extensive experiments across multiple vision-language benchmarks and LVLM families demonstrate consistent improvements, highlighting the robustness, generalizability, and efficiency of our approach.

</details>


### [203] [One-Time Training for All Grains: Open-Set Grain Recognition and Quantitative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09345)
*Qihe Su,Mengyu Sun,Yuxi Ke,Zhuoyan Jiang,Wanneng Yang,Chenglong Huang,Ziyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GROW框架，用于无需重新训练的开放集谷物识别和定量分析。该方法通过类不可知的谷物定位将混合谷物图像转换为个体实例，结合视觉嵌入和形态特征构建可扩展的GrainBank，通过排名相似度加权top-k检索实现识别。与传统联合重训练相比，分类注册时间从4153秒降至39秒，同时保持有竞争力的识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谷物品种识别方法通常基于固定品种集训练，引入新品种需要额外数据采集和模型重训练，效率低下且维护成本高，无法满足日益增长的品种多样性需求。

Method: GROW框架包含三个关键步骤：(1) 类不可知的谷物定位，将混合图像转为个体实例用于计数和表型测量；(2) 结合视觉嵌入和形态学描述符构建融合谷物描述符，存储于可扩展的GrainBank中；(3) 通过排名相似度加权top-k检索进行识别，新品种通过追加描述符即可纳入，无需更新已部署模型。

Result: 在渐进式品种扩展、不同谷物密度和背景域偏移等场景下进行了大量实验，验证了GROW的可扩展性、鲁棒性和适应性。与联合重训练相比，平均分类注册时间从4153秒降至39秒，同时保持了有竞争力的识别性能。

Conclusion: GROW为可扩展的谷物识别、计数和表型分析提供了一种高效且可维护的解决方案，无需重复模型重训练，能够适应不断引入的新谷物品种。

Abstract: Advances in crop breeding have introduced an increasing number of grain varieties, creating a growing demand for efficient variety recognition and quantitative analysis. However, existing methods are typically trained on a fixed variety set, and incorporating newly introduced varieties requires additional data collection and model retraining. To address this limitation, we propose GROW, a framework for Grain Recognition and quantitative analysis in Open sets Without retraining. GROW first performs class-agnostic grain localization, converting mixed-grain images into individual instances for variety-wise counting and phenotypic measurement. It then combines visual embeddings and morphological descriptors into fused grain descriptors stored in an extensible GrainBank. Query grains are recognized through rank-similarity weighted top-k retrieval, and newly introduced varieties are incorporated by appending their descriptors without updating the deployed models. Extensive experiments under progressive variety expansion, varying grain densities, and background domain shifts demonstrate the scalability, robustness, and adaptability of GROW. Compared with joint retraining, GROW reduced the average category-registration time from 4153 s to only 39 s while maintaining competitive recognition performance. These results demonstrate that GROW provides an efficient and maintainable solution for extensible grain recognition, counting, and phenotypic analysis without repeated model retraining.

</details>


### [204] [Alpha as an Efficiency Signal: Visibility-Routed RGBA Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09355)
*Zhe Li,Honghao Qiao,Zhixin Xu,Qijie Wang,Bo Peng,Dawei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GameAlpha-2.4K数据集（含2400条游戏风格RGBA视频）和参考条件化的RGBA视频生成器，该生成器通过可见性路由器跳过透明token的DiT更新以提升效率，实验表明相比传统两阶段管线取得了更低的FVD，并实现了1.2倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏行业需要高质量RGBA动画，但现有RGBA视频数据集缺乏游戏资产覆盖（多为写实内容），且传统先生成RGB再抠图的两阶段流程会导致半透明区域被背景模糊、抠像不稳定。

Method: 1) 构建GameAlpha-2.4K数据集，采用matte友好的合成、多假设alpha恢复和基于合成的质量门控；2) 提出参考条件化的RGBA视频生成器，单次前向同时生成RGB帧和alpha遮罩；3) 提出可见性路由器，在早期识别透明token并跳过其后续DiT更新，结合x_0-lock使其沿原始flow-matching调度预测终点。

Result: 相比传统两阶段管线获得更低的FVD；可见性路由器在最后两个DiT去噪步骤中跳过35%的token计算，实现1.2倍主干加速，质量损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 联合建模RGB与alpha、配合效率优化策略（可见性路由器）可有效提升RGBA游戏视频生成的质量与效率，为工业应用提供了可行的方案。

Abstract: RGBA videos combine RGB appearance with an alpha channel, enabling animated assets to be applied across arbitrary backgrounds, which are heavily used in gaming industry. However, generating high-quality RGBA animations for games remains challenging for two reasons. First, most existing RGBA video datasets are dominated by photorealistic content, with limited coverage of game assets. Second, the traditional generate-then-matte pipelines estimate alpha only after RGB synthesis, so semi-transparent regions are often blurred by background, resulting in unstable matting outputs. More recently, many methods have begun to model RGB and alpha jointly, but existing approaches are mostly text-conditioned, and still have unresolved issues in efficiency and quality. To address these challenges, we introduce GameAlpha-2.4K, a 2.4K-clip game-style RGBA video dataset built with matte-friendly synthesis, multi-hypothesis alpha recovery, and compositing-based quality gates. Using this dataset, we train a reference-conditioned RGBA video generator that jointly produces RGB frames and alpha mattes in a single pass. To improve efficiency, we propose a visibility router that identifies transparent tokens in an early stage and bypasses their later DiT updates, while x_0-lock guides them along the original flow-matching schedule toward self-predicted endpoints. Our model obtains lower FVD than traditional two-stage pipelines, and the visibility router skips 35% of token evaluations in the final two DiT denoising steps, providing a 1.2x backbone speedup with negligible quality degradation compared to dense inference.

</details>


### [205] [ControlRadio: Prompt-Driven Controllable Diffusion for Cross-Modal Radio Map Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09357)
*Kangjun Liu,Xiying Pan,Shuhang Zhang,Xiang Xiang,Ke Chen,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ControlRadio，一种可控的生成式框架，能够从自然语言描述和环境布局（建筑结构、发射器位置）生成无线电台图，在保持高精度的同时将计算时间相比传统仿真方法降低四个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无线电台图构建需要密集测量或高计算成本的物理仿真，难以扩展和实时部署；现有生成式AI方法缺乏细粒度控制和物理一致性，难以应用于真实无线环境。

Method: 采用联合语义与空间条件控制的可控生成框架，结合可控潜空间先验（controlled latent prior）和布局感知条件（layout-aware conditioning）来增强生成稳定性与结构一致性，输入包括自然语言描述、建筑结构和发射器位置。

Result: 在多种城市场景下达到最先进精度并展现强泛化能力，同时相比传统基于仿真的方法将计算时间降低超过四个数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作为可扩展且可控的无线环境建模提供了新范式，对下一代通信系统和数据驱动的无线感知具有广泛影响。

Abstract: Radio maps describe how wireless signals propagate across space and are essential for wireless communication, sensing, and network planning. However, constructing accurate radio maps traditionally requires either dense measurements or computationally expensive physical simulations, which limits scalability and real-time deployment. Recent advances in generative artificial intelligence offer a promising alternative, but existing approaches lack fine-grained control and physical consistency when applied to real-world wireless environments. Here we present \textbf{ControlRadio}, a controllable generative framework that produces radio maps from natural-language descriptions and environmental layouts, including building structures and transmitter locations. Joint semantic and spatial conditioning enables interpretable, propagation-plausible generation, while a controlled latent prior and layout-aware conditioning improve stability and structural consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ControlRadio achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and strong generalization across diverse urban scenarios, while reducing computation time by more than four orders of magnitude compared with conventional simulation-based methods. Such results suggest a new paradigm for scalable and controllable wireless environment modeling, with broad implications for next-generation communication systems and data-driven radio sensing.

</details>


### [206] [Deep Learning based Detection of Fishing Vessels and Fishing Monitoring using Nightlight Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09360)
*Shantakar Mohanty,Prasun Kumar Gupta,Raian Vargas Maretto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于SDGSAT-1夜间灯光影像与双分支YOLO11深度学习架构的小型渔船检测方法，在印度西海岸实现了0.96的mAP，检测出31525艘次渔船，并识别出77.3%的潜在"暗船"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上监视需求日益增长，特别是针对未开启AIS系统的"暗船"渔船活动监测存在巨大挑战，需要开发新的检测手段。

Method: 利用SDGSAT-1卫星的10米全色和40米RGB夜间灯光影像，构建双重分支YOLO11架构（并行卷积骨干网络分别处理两种模态后拼接），专门针对夜间灯光影像中的小目标检测进行优化。

Result: 双分支YOLO11模型精度0.99、召回率0.93、F1值0.96、mAP@50为0.96，优于YOLOv5s、YOLOv8s和标准YOLO11s；检测出31525艘次渔船，其中77.3%被识别为潜在暗船；捕捞活动高峰期为1-4月，主要活动区域位于距海岸50-100km的大陆架。

Conclusion: 夜间灯光卫星影像结合深度学习可有效提升渔船检测能力，为海洋监视和渔业合规监管提供了有价值的技术手段和实证依据。

Abstract: The demand for maritime surveillance has given rise to the need for monitoring fishing vessel activities, particularly in addressing the challenge of "dark vessels" that operate without Automatic Identification System (AIS) transmission. This study presents a novel approach for detecting small-scale fishing vessels using nighttime light (NTL) imagery from the SDGSAT-1 satellite, combined with deep learning techniques to enhance fishing monitoring awareness along the western coast of India. A dual-branch YOLO11 architecture was developed to exploit both the 10-meter panchromatic and 40-meter RGB imagery from SDGSAT-1. The custom model architecture was specifically optimized for small object detection in NTL imagery, featuring parallel convolutional backbones that process both modalities before concatenation for enhanced feature extraction. The dual-branch YOLO11 model demonstrated optimal performance with a precision of 0.99, recall of 0.93, F1-score of 0.96, and mAP@50 of 0.96, significantly outperforming single-branch implementations of YOLOv5s, YOLOv8s, and standard YOLO11s architectures. When applied to the western coast of India, the model detected 31525 vessel instances across the temporal dataset spanning 2022-23. Cross-matching analysis with AIS data revealed that only 7146 (22.7%) of detected vessels had corresponding AIS transmissions, while 24379 (77.3%) were identified as potential dark vessels. Spatio-temporal analysis showed peak fishing activity during January-April, with a primary activity corridor parallel to the coastline within 50-100 km, corresponding to productive continental shelf areas. This research contributes to maritime surveillance capabilities by highlighting the effectiveness of nighttime lights satellite imagery for fishing vessel detection and provides valuable insights into fishing patterns and potential regulatory compliance issues in Indian waters.

</details>


### [207] [FeedbackTrack: Visual-Cortex-Inspired Cross-Frame Feedback for Transformer Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09369)
*Yueyang Cang,Xiaoteng Zhang,Zhiyuan Ning,Yuchen He,Li Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FeedbackTrack，一种受视觉皮层启发的框架，将稀疏的、组级跨帧反馈引入预训练Transformer跟踪器中，通过Query Feedback和Gate Feedback两条轻量级路径缓存并复用前一帧的中间状态，在SPMTrack和ARTrackV2上一致提升五个骨干配置的性能，仅增加不到1%的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数Transformer跟踪器仍依赖前馈式特征提取，现有时间机制仅更新模板、提示、查询或预测状态，很少复用中间表示来调节对应处理阶段，缺乏有效的跨帧递归信息整合。

Method: 提出FeedbackTrack框架，将前一帧的中间状态detach、缓存后通过两条轻量级路径反馈到当前帧对应的Transformer组：Query Feedback用于令牌级查询调制，Gate Feedback用于上下文相关的特征调制，仅需固定大小的一帧缓存，不改变原有跟踪流程。

Result: 在SPMTrack和ARTrackV2上一致改进五种骨干配置，SPMTrack-G在LaSOT上达到79.1 AUC，GOT-10k上达到83.4 AO，参数增加不到1%；跨帧反馈比同帧调制高出1.8-3.2 AO点；学习到的反馈强度呈现非均匀的深度相关组织。

Conclusion: 跨帧递归反馈是Transformer跟踪性能提升的主要来源，优于同帧调制方案，且反馈强度的深度非均匀分布验证了递归反馈机制对Transformer跟踪的有效性。

Abstract: Visual object tracking requires effective temporal integration, yet most Transformer trackers still rely on predominantly feed-forward feature extraction. Existing temporal mechanisms typically update templates, prompts, queries, or prediction states, while intermediate representations are rarely reused to modulate corresponding processing stages. We propose \textbf{FeedbackTrack}, a visual-cortex-inspired framework that introduces sparse, group-level layer-aligned cross-frame feedback into pretrained Transformer trackers. Previous-frame intermediate states are detached, cached, and returned to corresponding Transformer groups in the current frame through two lightweight pathways: Query Feedback for token-level query modulation and Gate Feedback for context-dependent feature modulation. FeedbackTrack preserves the original tracking pipeline with only a fixed-size one-frame cache. Across SPMTrack and ARTrackV2, FeedbackTrack consistently improves five backbone configurations on LaSOT and GOT-10k, achieving 83.4 AO and 79.1 AUC with SPMTrack-G while adding less than 1\% parameters. Controlled comparisons show that cross-frame feedback outperforms same-frame modulation by 1.8--3.2 AO points, demonstrating that the gains mainly come from recurrent historical information. Further analysis reveals a non-uniform depth-dependent organization of learned feedback strengths, highlighting the effectiveness of recurrent feedback for Transformer tracking.

</details>


### [208] [Preserve More Details: Mitigating Content Drift in Real-World Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09373)
*Chunxiao Liu,Wei Liu,Anbin Xiong,Erli Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FSP-Diff，一种具有双路径架构的单步扩散模型，用于解决真实图像超分中因输入低清晰度导致的内容漂移问题（包括视觉细节退化和文本语义偏移）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Stable Diffusion的真实图像超分方法往往忽视了LQ输入清晰度不足会不可避免地导致生成HQ图像中的内容漂移，主要表现为视觉细节退化和文本语义偏移，严重损害了保真度和感知质量。

Method: 提出FSP-Diff，一种新颖的单步扩散模型，采用双路径架构：Detail-Conditioned Pathway用于注入结构化细节以恢复精细结构；Detail-Modulated Semantic Pathway利用结构化细节来优化语义引导，从而减轻语义偏差。

Result: 在标准Real-ISR基准上的大量实验表明，FSP-Diff在定量和定性指标上均超越了现有的单步扩散方法。

Conclusion: FSP-Diff通过双路径架构有效解决了LQ输入导致的内容漂移问题，在真实图像超分任务中取得了优于现有单步扩散方法的性能，兼顾了视觉保真度和感知质量。

Abstract: Real-world image super-resolution (Real-ISR) aims to reconstruct high-quality (HQ) images from low-quality (LQ) inputs subject to diverse real-world degradations. Recent advances have leveraged the LQ inputs and natural image priors learned by Stable Diffusion models to achieve impressive results. However, existing methods often overlook insufficient clarity of LQ inputs inevitably induce content drift in the generated HQ images. This manifests primarily as visual detail degradation and textual semantic shift, severely compromising both fidelity and perceptual quality. To address this challenge, we propose FSP-Diff, a novel one-step diffusion model featuring a dual-pathway architecture. This architecture comprises a Detail-Conditioned Pathway for injecting structured details to recover fine structures, and a Detail-Modulated Semantic Pathway that refines semantic guidance using structured details to mitigate semantic deviations. Extensive experiments on standard Real-ISR benchmarks demonstrate that FSP-Diff surpasses existing one-step diffusion methods in both quantitative and qualitative metrics.

</details>


### [209] [Efficient Human-Contact Representation for Human-Scene Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09388)
*Nghia Vu,Tuong Do,Binh X. Nguyen,Erman Tjiputra,Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型高效的人-场景接触表示方法（稀疏接触掩码），通过引入稀疏算子替代传统稠密算子，在减少冗余数据的同时提升计算速度，在接触预测和场景合成两个任务上超越现有最先进模型，推理速度提升至少12倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人-场景交互在网络架构和参数优化方面已有显著进展，但接触信息的高效表示仍是一个未解决的开放挑战。现有方法在表示人与环境接触时存在高维输入中的冗余数据问题，影响计算效率和模型精度。

Method: 提出稀疏接触掩码（sparse contact masks），通过策略性地选择关键接触信息来减少高维输入中的冗余数据；在此基础上设计一组稀疏算子（sparse operators），替代深度网络层中的传统稠密算子，以实现更快的计算速度和更精确的接触过滤。

Result: 在三个公开基准数据集上，针对接触预测和场景合成两项关键任务进行实验，结果表明该方法在重建精度上优于最先进的模型，同时计算速度相比近期基线方法提升至少12倍。

Conclusion: 高效的稀疏接触表示方法能够同时提升人-场景交互模型的计算效率和预测精度，为虚拟现实、游戏、机器人等实际应用提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Human-scene interaction is an active research topic with several industrial applications in virtual reality, gaming, robotics, and surveillance. Despite significant progress in network architectures to improve the results or optimize models' parameters for fast inference speed, the efficient representation of contact between humans and their environments remains an open challenge. In this paper, we propose a new efficient human-contact representation for human-scene interaction. Our primary contribution is the introduction of sparse contact masks that strategically select essential contact information, significantly reducing redundant data in high-dimensional inputs. Leveraging this efficient contact representation, we propose a suite of sparse operators to replace traditional dense operators within deep network layers for faster computation. Our approach not only enhances computational speed but also filters out non-essential contact data, thereby improving the precision of human-scene interaction models. To validate the effectiveness of our method, we conduct intensive experiments across three public benchmark datasets, focusing on two critical tasks for human-scene interaction: contact prediction and scene synthesis. The experimental results show that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art models in reconstruction accuracy and achieves a computation speed-up of at least 12 times over recent baselines.

</details>


### [210] [CoInS-Net: A Continuous Position-Aware Network for Joint Medical Image Interpolation and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09391)
*Yujia Sun,Ningfeng Que,Peiting Shi,Rongrong Fu,Yingying Yang,Xinhang Li,Yin Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoInS-Net，一种用于医学图像帧插值和病灶分割联合任务的连续位置感知交互网络，通过双向交互、共享Swin编码器和原型交互机制，在四个公开医学影像数据集上优于传统单任务方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像插值和分割通常独立进行，导致大量冗余计算，且无法充分利用相邻切片间互补的跨切片结构信息。各向异性医学体数据稀疏采样导致结构不连续和边界模糊，影响临床图像分析的可靠性。

Method: 提出连续位置感知交互网络(CoInS-Net)：1)共享Swin编码器与连续空间坐标查询实现双向交互；2)空间连续位置插值模块从相对坐标和物理间距生成各尺度的目标位置特征；3)基于原型的任务互交互模块通过共享原型实现分割与插值分支间的全局结构交换；4)多尺度任务协同解码器将各尺度分离为共享和任务特定组件，使两个任务在边界层面互相增强且无需额外标注。

Result: 在四个涵盖多种模态和解剖区域的公开医学影像数据集上，所提方法优于传统的单任务方案，联合优化框架实现了插值和分割任务之间的相互促进。

Conclusion: CoInS-Net通过联合优化有效实现医学图像插值与分割任务的互促，为智能临床医学图像分析提供了可靠且通用的技术方案。

Abstract: Accurate medical image interpolation and anatomical structure segmentation are fundamental for computer-aided diagnosis and treatment planning. Anisotropic medical volumes with sparse through-plane sampling often suffer from structural discontinuity and boundary blur, hindering reliable clinical image analysis. Most existing methods implement interpolation and segmentation independently, which introduces redundant computation and fails to fully exploit complementary cross-slice structural information between sequential slices. To address these issues, we propose a continuous position-aware interaction network, termed CoInS-Net, for joint frame interpolation and lesion segmentation. Unlike conventional cascaded interpolation-then-segmentation paradigms, the framework enables bidirectional interaction under a shared Swin encoder with continuous spatial coordinate queries. A spatially continuous position interpolation module generates target-position features at every scale from the relative coordinate and physical spacing, and a prototype-based task mutual interaction module lets the segmentation and interpolation branches exchange global structure through a small set of shared prototypes rather than dense feature mixing. A multi-scale task-cooperative decoder further separates each scale into shared and task-specific components, so the two tasks reinforce common anatomy while preserving their distinct requirements down to the boundary level, without extra annotations. Experiments on four public medical imaging datasets with diverse modalities and anatomical regions demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms conventional single-task schemes. The joint optimization framework effectively realizes mutual promotion between interpolation and segmentation tasks, providing a reliable and universal technical scheme for intelligent clinical medical image analysis.

</details>


### [211] [CableDex: Cable Length Estimation on Industrial Reels Using a Handheld Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09392)
*Francisco Guillén,Ricardo Almeida,Bruno Silva,João C. Neves*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CableDex是一个计算机视觉系统，通过手机拍摄的单张照片自动估算工业电缆卷盘上的电缆长度，替代耗时且不准确的人工测量。系统结合相机标定、实例分割、姿态估计和体积计算，在五种卷盘类型上达到4.90%的平均绝对百分比误差（MAPE），满足工业界10%的误差容限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业电缆卷盘上电缆长度的传统人工测量耗时且不准确，需要一种自动化、便捷且精确的测量方案。

Method: 系统结合相机标定、实例分割、姿态估计和体积计算，使用基于1,000张人工标注图像训练的实例分割模型，通过手机应用实现从卷盘标签扫描、图像采集到分割和长度估算的端到端流程。

Result: 实例分割模型达到99.5%的mAP50，推理时间每张图像5.66毫秒；在75个卷盘、五种卷盘类型上评估，MAPE为4.90%，满足10%的工业误差容限。

Conclusion: CableDex提供了一种高效、准确的电缆长度自动测量方案，通过移动端应用实现端到端流程，在工业场景中具有实用价值。

Abstract: CableDex is a computer vision system that addresses the time-consuming and inaccurate manual measurement of cable length on industrial reels from a single photograph captured with a mobile phone. The system combines camera calibration, instance segmentation, pose estimation, and volumetric calculation to estimate the cable length across five different reel types and various cable sizes. This system is based on an instance segmentation model trained on 1,000 manually annotated images, achieving 99.5\% mAP50 with an inference time of 5.66 ms per image. Evaluated on 75 reels across five reel types, the system achieves a MAPE of 4.90\%, within the 10\% error tolerance commonly accepted in industrial cable-reel measurement. The demonstration presents the end-to-end pipeline, from reel label scanning and image capture to segmentation and length estimation, through the mobile application.

</details>


### [212] [Sign Language Recognition Using Original and Synthetic Depth Image Based Point Cloud Data Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09400)
*Rustem Ozakar,Eyup Gedikli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究利用Depth Anything V2网络从RGB图像生成合成深度图像，并转换为点云数据用于手语识别。在三个手语数据集上比较了原始深度图像和合成深度图像生成的点云在多种PointNet架构及不同数据模型下的分类性能。结果显示，两种点云数据在多数模型中都取得了可接受的性能，原始深度图像点云整体优于合成点云，但在部分模型中合成数据表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语识别主要依赖RGB图像，而提供深度图像的手语数据集有限。研究者希望利用深度图像生成的点云进行手语识别，但深度数据稀缺，因此尝试通过神经网络从RGB图像合成深度图像来弥补这一不足。

Method: 使用Depth Anything V2网络从RGB图像生成合成深度图像；在三个同时包含RGB和深度图像的手语数据集（Real-time ASL Fingerspelling、KArSL、AUTSL）上进行实验；将原始深度图像和合成深度图像分别转换为点云数据，采用多种PointNet架构进行分类；使用三种数据模型（基于帧、Point Gesture Map、LSTM）进行对比。

Result: 原始和合成深度图像生成的点云在大多数模型中都取得了可接受的分类性能。总体而言，基于原始深度图像的点云模型性能优于合成深度图像模型，但在部分特定模型中，合成深度图像模型的表现反而优于原始模型。

Conclusion: 合成深度图像在多数手语识别场景下可作为原始深度数据的有效替代方案，尽管整体性能略逊于原始深度数据，但在某些模型中甚至表现更优，这为深度数据有限的手语识别研究提供了新的可行途径。

Abstract: Research regarding the sign language recognition mostly relies on RGB images, whileas sign language datasets that provide depth images are limited. Point clouds obtained from depth images can be used for sign language recognition with neural networks like PointNet. In recent years, various neural networks are used for generating realistic depth images from monocular RGB images. In this work, synthetic depth images were created from RGB images using Depth Anything V2 network. For this purpose, three sign language datasets (Real-time ASL Fingerspelling, KArSL, AUTSL) which contain both RGB and depth images were used. Classification accuracies of the point cloud data created from both original and synthetic depth images using various PointNet architectures were measured for sign language recognition. From the original and synthetic point clouds, frame based, Point Gesture Map and Long Short Term Memory data models were used for classification and their performances were compared. In the results, both original and synthetic based data achieved acceptable performance in most models. In general, original depth based point cloud models performed better than synthetic ones, however in some models synthetic depth based models performed better than the originals.

</details>


### [213] [One Model to Magnify Them All: Efficient Scale-Invariant Histopathology via Conditional Normalization and Continuous Magnification Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09403)
*Agnieszka Florkowska,Henning Müller,Marek Wodzinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出条件层归一化（CLN）机制，通过小型MLP从输入像素尺寸生成仿射归一化参数，集成到标准CNN架构中，实现全切片图像（WSI）的分类和分割。该方法在连续像素尺寸范围内训练，能够泛化到测试时任意未见过的缩放尺度，在PANDA前列腺癌数据集上匹配或超越独立训练的单倍率模型，将五模型集成压缩为单一网络，训练和推理成本降低约4-5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字病理WSI在不同放大倍率下获取，编码从全局组织架构到细粒度细胞形态的互补诊断信息，但深度学习模型对尺度变化敏感。现有放大倍率不变方法依赖预定义离散分辨率的固定多尺度架构，而临床部署中采集倍率连续变化，很少与模型固定训练分辨率对齐，中间尺度常见，稳健覆盖需要昂贵的倍率专用模型集成。

Method: 提出条件层归一化（CLN），通过小型MLP根据输入像素尺寸生成仿射归一化参数，并集成到标准CNN架构中用于WSI分类和分割。模型在连续像素尺寸范围内采样训练，从而将推理与扫描仪相关的倍率解耦，实现对测试时任意未见尺度的泛化。

Result: 在PANDA前列腺癌数据集上，该方法在平均上匹配或超过独立训练的单倍率模型，在包括训练未见过倍率在内的每个评估倍率中均位列前三。将五模型集成压缩为单一网络，训练和推理成本降低约4-5倍，同时保持乘加运算次数不变。代码已开源。

Conclusion: CLN提供了一种轻量且高效的放大倍率不变方案，消除了对多模型集成的依赖，在保持性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，为WSI分析中的尺度泛化问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Whole slide images (WSIs) in digital histopathology are acquired at discrete magnification levels encoding complementary diagnostic information from global tissue architecture to fine-grained cellular morphology. Yet, deep learning models remain sensitive to scale variation. Existing magnification-invariant methods rely on multi-scale architectures at predefined discrete resolutions, while in clinical deployment the acquisition magnification varies continuously, rarely aligns with a model's fixed training resolution, and intermediate scales are common, so robust coverage otherwise demands a costly ensemble of magnification-specific models. We propose Conditional Layer Normalization (CLN), a lightweight mechanism that generates affine normalization parameters from input pixel size via a small MLP, integrated into standard CNN architectures for both WSI classification and segmentation. Trained on patches sampled continuously across a range of pixel sizes, the model decouples inference from scanner-dependent magnification and generalizes to arbitrary, previously unseen scales at test time. On the PANDA prostate cancer dataset, our approach on average matches or exceeds independently trained single-magnification models and ranks among the top three performers at every evaluated magnification, including those unseen during training. This collapses a five-model ensemble into a single network and reduces training, and inference cost roughly 4-5 times, while leaving the multiply-accumulate count unchanged. The code is available at: https://github.com/aflorkowska/OneModelToMagnifyThemAll.

</details>


### [214] [MeanSR: Restoration Trajectory Learning for One-Step Perceptual Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09405)
*Axi Niu,Jiawei Kou,Kang Zhang,Qingsen Yan,Jinqiu Sun,Yanning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MeanSR是一种单步感知超分辨率方法，通过学习LR条件下的平均速度场直接建模从低分辨率到高分辨率的有限时间转换，无需蒸馏预训练教师模型，在多个感知质量指标上优于CTMSR且大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式超分辨率方法虽然感知质量好但推理成本高；已有的单步蒸馏方法依赖昂贵的预训练教师模型；CTMSR虽避免蒸馏但未显式建模LR到HR的恢复动力学。因此需要一种不依赖蒸馏且能显式建模恢复过程的单步感知超分方法。

Method: 提出MeanSR框架：(1) 学习LR条件下的平均速度场，直接捕获从退化/噪声输入到HR输出的有限时间转换；(2) 为平均速度生成重新定义分布轨迹匹配；(3) 引入阶段感知的时间采样策略以改进轨迹学习。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上，MeanSR在CLIPIQA、MUSIQ和MANIQA指标上优于CTMSR，同时大幅减少FLOPs和推理延迟。重建结果具有更清晰的细节结构和更真实的纹理，感知伪影更少。

Conclusion: MeanSR通过显式建模LR到HR的恢复动力学，实现了无需蒸馏且高效的单步感知超分辨率，在感知质量和计算效率上均取得显著改进。

Abstract: Diffusion-based super-resolution (SR) achieves strong perceptual quality but requires costly iterative denoising. Existing one-step distillation methods reduce inference time but depend on expensive pretrained teachers, whereas CTMSR avoids distillation through PF-ODE consistency training yet does not explicitly model the restoration dynamics from low-resolution (LR) inputs to high-resolution (HR) images. We propose MeanSR, a one-step perceptual SR method that learns an LR-conditioned average velocity field to directly capture the finite-time transition from degraded or noisy inputs to plausible HR outputs. We further reformulate distribution trajectory matching for average-velocity generation and introduce a Stage-Aware Temporal Sampling strategy to improve trajectory learning. Experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that MeanSR outperforms CTMSR on CLIPIQA, MUSIQ, and MANIQA while substantially reducing FLOPs and inference latency. MeanSR also reconstructs sharper structures and more realistic textures with fewer perceptual artifacts.

</details>


### [215] [Foundation Models are Implicit Deepfake Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09427)
*Stefan Smeu,Dragos-Alexandru Boldisor,Elisabeta Oneata,Dan Oneata*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现了一个跨模型、跨数据集、跨图像和视频域的一致现象：假媒体样本的预训练自监督表征幅度系统性低于真实样本。基于此发现，将深度伪造检测建模为异常检测问题，仅用特征幅度的简单统计即可达到与复杂方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练自监督表征已成为深度伪造检测的核心组件，但哪些属性使真实与伪造媒体可区分仍不清楚。作者旨在揭示这一区分性的本质来源。

Method: 首先通过实验发现假样本表征幅度系统性更低的现象；将深度伪造检测建模为异常检测问题，使用特征幅度的简单统计进行分类；通过消融实验分析幅值降低主要源于语义偏移而非底层生成指纹；并验证该信号随基础模型规模增大而增强。

Result: 简单的特征幅度统计即可取得与复杂深度伪造检测方法相当的性能；特征幅值降低主要由伪造内容引入的语义偏移驱动；该判别信号随基础模型规模增长而增强。

Conclusion: 表征学习领域的进步能自然转化为更强的零样本深度伪造检测器，特征幅度可作为简单而有效的深度伪造检测信号。

Abstract: Pretrained self-supervised representations have emerged as a core component of current deepfake detection methods, yet it remains unclear which of their properties make real and fake media distinguishable. In this work, we uncover a surprisingly consistent phenomenon: across multiple pretrained models, datasets, and both image and video domains, fake samples systematically produce lower-magnitude representations than their real counterparts. Motivated by this finding, we formulate deepfake detection as an anomaly detection problem and show that simple statistics of feature magnitude achieve competitive performance with far more sophisticated deepfake detection methods. We further investigate the origin of this effect and demonstrate that reduced feature magnitude is primarily associated with semantic shifts introduced by fake content, while low-level generative fingerprints play a comparatively smaller role. Finally, we show that this discriminative signal strengthens as the size of the underlying foundation model grows, suggesting that advances in representation learning naturally translate into stronger zero-shot deepfake detectors.

</details>


### [216] [Unveiling the Secret of AdaLN-Zero in Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09438)
*Jie Zhu,Mingyu Ding,Boqiang Duan,Leye Wang,Jingdong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文深入研究了DiT中关键的条件机制adaLN-Zero，发现零初始化是其性能优势的最关键因素，并基于此提出了分析引导的初始化策略adaLN-Gaussian和改进的条件机制SE-adaLN-Zero，在四个数据集（尤其ImageNet1K）以及文生图任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiT）作为新兴的图像生成架构备受关注，但其理解仍停留在表面。尽管已有改进其性能的努力，但对DiT内部关键机制（尤其是adaLN-Zero）的工作原理缺乏深入认识。

Method: （1）系统研究adaLN-Zero中三个潜在因素：SE-like结构、零初始化、"渐进"更新顺序，发现零初始化影响最大；（2）基于分析提出adaLN-Gaussian初始化策略；（3）受SE-like结构启发，提出改进的条件机制SE-adaLN-Zero。

Result: 在四个数据集上（尤其是ImageNet1K）以及文生图任务上的大量实验证明了adaLN-Gaussian和SE-adaLN-Zero的有效性和泛化能力，两者均能提升优化效率。

Conclusion: 本研究不仅深化了对DiT中adaLN-Zero机制的理解（揭示零初始化的关键作用），还提供了实用的改进方法，为扩散Transformer架构的进一步优化提供了分析指导。

Abstract: Diffusion transformer (DiT), a rapidly emerging architecture for image generation, has gained much attention. However, despite ongoing efforts to improve its performance, the understanding of DiT remains superficial. In this work, we delve into and investigate a critical conditioning mechanism within DiT, adaLN-Zero, which achieves superior performance compared to adaLN. Our work studies three potential elements driving this performance, including an SE-like structure, zero-initialization, and a "gradual" update order, among which zero-initialization is proved to be the most influential. Building on this understanding, we propose an analysis-guided initialization strategy, termed adaLN-Gaussian, which serves both as an empirical validation of our analysis and as a practical initialization method that consistently improves optimization efficiency. On the other hand, inspired by the SE-like structure, we introduce an improved conditioning mechanism called SE-adaLN-Zero. Extensive experiments following DiT on four datasets, especially on ImageNet1K demonstrate the effectiveness and generalization of adaLN-Gaussian and SE-adaLN-Zero. Beyond class-to-image generation, we also evaluate the generalization of the two improved methods on text-to-image generation.

</details>


### [217] [Sekai2: From World Exploration to Interactive World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09449)
*Kang He,Wenshuo Peng,Zihui Gao,Jiaming Tan,Kaipeng Zhang,Yongtao Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Sekai2，一个多源真实世界视频数据集，包含128,892个片段共2,826小时、覆盖113个国家/地区的视频，每条视频均配有相机轨迹和层次化语义标注，特别引入了982个沿非线性轨迹拍摄的全景序列以支持持久场景表示与长期空间记忆学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频语料难以同时满足长时程生成和相机控制的需求：大规模网络视频缺乏轨迹和时间对齐文本，而带位姿标注的数据集通常短程或面向重建。需要一个同时具备长视频、相机轨迹和时序语义标注的大规模真实世界数据集。

Method: 基于Sekai的世界探索视频构建多源数据集Sekai2，包含128,892个片段（共2,826小时），所有片段均提供相机轨迹和层次化标注（区分主体运动、环境动态、静态场景内容和相机行为），共产生649,597个时序语义片段。特别引入982个包含环路和重访的全景序列，提供同一地点跨时间和视角的重复观测。

Result: 语料规模分析显示：完整的位姿-字幕覆盖、广泛的地理和语义多样性、多样的相机轨迹以及高度非冗余的时间描述。在120秒分解下，43,594个片段达到完整两分钟，占全部素材的51.4%。

Conclusion: Sekai2为长时程视频生成、相机可控合成和交互式世界模型预训练提供了可扩展的资源，尤其是重访序列为学习持久场景表示和几何一致的世界模型提供了关键监督信号。

Abstract: Video world models must capture how scenes evolve over time and across viewpoints. Training them for long-horizon generation and camera control therefore benefits from long videos paired with camera trajectories and temporally grounded semantics. Existing corpora rarely offer the three together: large-scale web video provides broad visual diversity but no trajectories or time-aligned text, while pose-annotated datasets are typically short-range or reconstruction-oriented. We introduce Sekai2, a multi-source real-world video dataset that carries the world-exploration footage of Sekai toward interactive world modeling. The release contains 128,892 clips totaling 2,826 hours from 10,428 source videos across 113 countries or regions, and is deliberately weighted toward sustained observation: under a common 120-second decomposition, 43,594 segments reach the full two minutes and account for 51.4% of all footage. Every clip includes a released camera trajectory and hierarchical annotations disentangling subject motion, environment dynamics, static scene content, and camera behavior, resulting in 649,597 temporally grounded segments. Crucially, we further introduce 982 panoramic sequences captured along non-linear trajectories with loops and revisits. These revisits provide repeated observations of the same locations across time and viewpoints, offering essential supervision for learning persistent scene representations, long-term spatial memory, and geometrically consistent world models. Corpus-scale analyses demonstrate complete pose-and-caption coverage, broad geographic and semantic diversity, varied camera trajectories, and highly non-redundant temporal descriptions. Together, these properties make Sekai2 a scalable resource for long-horizon video generation, camera-controllable synthesis, and interactive world-model pre-training.

</details>


### [218] [A Content-Aware Pure Permutation with Intrinsic Avalanche Effect: Breaking the Diffusion-Permutation Dichotomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09452)
*Zahra Ghoraeian,Mohammad-Reza Sadeghi,Samaneh Mashhadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出三角内容感知置换（TCA）算法，挑战"纯置换无法产生差分敏感性"的传统假设。该方法利用Canny边缘检测和Delaunay三角剖分，使单个像素的变化引发全局置换模式的完全改变，将NPCR从接近零提升至97.10%，证明纯置换也能产生显著的雪崩效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文献认为像素置换仅重新排列像素位置而不改变像素值，无法产生差分敏感性和雪崩效应。作者旨在打破这一假设，证明通过内容感知的图像几何敏感置换，纯置换也能实现强差分敏感性。

Method: TCA算法首先使用Canny算子提取边缘点，然后对边缘点和角点应用Delaunay三角剖分，生成独特的图像分区。由于三角剖分高度依赖图像几何结构，单个像素的改变会改变边缘图，导致完全不同的三角剖分和全局置换模式，从而实现纯置换下的差分敏感性。

Result: 在50张图像上实验，TCA平均14.81次迭代达到NPCR=97.10%和UACI=20.06%，而传统方法保持接近零的NPCR。迭代阈值因内容复杂度在6.4到30.7之间变化。低PSNR（11.93 dB）和接近零的相关性（~10^-3）验证了优越的统计性能，但代价是比经典方法更慢的计算速度。

Conclusion: 纯置换可以产生显著的差分敏感性，TCA的不可解析、内容依赖特性使其非常适合基于参考的加密、脆弱水印和非盲隐写应用。较慢的速度是换取更强安全性的有意权衡。

Abstract: Pixel permutation is a fundamental tool in image processing, image encryption, and data hiding (including watermarking and steganography) that rearranges pixels without changing their values. A common assumption in the literature is that permutation alone cannot create differential sensitivity; changing one pixel merely relocates that pixel in the output, producing no avalanche effect. This paper challenges this by introducing the Triangular Content-Aware Permutation (TCA) algorithm.
  The method extracts edge points using Canny and applies Delaunay Triangulation to edges and corners, creating a unique partition. Since triangulation is highly sensitive to image geometry, changing a single pixel alters the edge map, resulting in a completely different triangulation and global permutation pattern. Unlike classical dimension-based permutations and advanced content-aware methods (2025-2026), which lack differential sensitivity, TCA increases NPCR from near-zero to 97.10% solely through pixel relocation.
  Experiments on 50 images show that TCA, with an average of 14.81 iterations, achieves NPCR = 97.10% and UACI = 20.06%, proving pure permutation can create significant differential sensitivity. Conventional methods maintain near-zero NPCR. The iteration threshold varies from 6.4 to 30.7 based on content complexity. Low PSNR (11.93 dB) and near-zero correlation (~10^-3) confirm superior statistical performance. Although slower than classical methods due to triangulation, this is a deliberate trade-off for stronger security. Given the non-analytic, content-dependent nature of the pattern, TCA is ideal for reference-based encryption, fragile watermarking, and non-blind steganography.

</details>


### [219] [RecoverFly: A Failure-Aware Reinforcement Learning Post-Training Framework for Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09467)
*Boxiong Wang,Hui Kang,Geng Sun,Jiahui Li,Chao Yu,Daxin Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RecoverFly，一个针对端到端无人机视觉-语言-动作（UAV-VLA）策略的失败感知强化学习后训练框架。RecoverFly通过token级RL稳定优化语法约束的自回归无人机动作，重访未解决的失败案例以强化纠正学习，并结合两阶段长尾场景课程与参考策略正则化提升场景适应性。在TravelUAV基准上，RecoverFly在seen、unseen-map和unseen-object三个划分上均取得最优性能，相比AerialVLA基线在约30%训练集规模的rollout预算下成功率提升了3.12至8.37个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端UAV-VLA策略采用行为克隆目标进行训练，缺乏交互式闭环执行所需的纠正性监督。虽然强化学习提供解决方案，但其有效性受限于样本利用效率低、长尾场景分布以及优化中的策略分布偏移问题。

Method: RecoverFly包含三个核心组件：（1）token级RL，用于语法约束自回归无人机动作的稳定优化；（2）重访未解决失败案例以加强纠正学习和提升样本利用率；（3）两阶段长尾场景课程结合参考策略正则化，改善场景适应能力同时保留已习得能力。

Result: 在TravelUAV基准的seen、unseen-map和unseen-object三个划分上均取得最佳性能。相比AerialVLA初始化，在总rollout预算约为训练集大小30%的条件下，成功率提升3.12至8.37个百分点。

Conclusion: RecoverFly验证了失败感知的RL后训练框架对端到端UAV-VLA策略的有效性、鲁棒性和泛化能力，为解决复杂环境下无人机视觉语言导航任务提供了新思路。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle vision-language navigation (UAV-VLN) requires agents to translate visual observations and language instructions into reliable flight actions in complex environments. Although recent end-to-end UAV vision-language-action (UAV-VLA) policies reduce reliance on separately designed perception, planning, and control modules, their behavior-cloning objectives provide limited corrective supervision for interactive closed-loop execution. Reinforcement learning (RL) offers a promising solution, while its effectiveness is constrained by inefficient use of samples, long-tailed scene distributions, and policy distribution shift during optimization. To this end, we propose RecoverFly, a failure-aware RL post-training framework for end-to-end UAV-VLA policies. Specifically, RecoverFly adapts token-level RL for stable optimization of grammar-constrained autoregressive UAV actions, revisits unresolved failure cases to strengthen corrective learning and sample utilization, and combines a two-stage long-tail scene curriculum with reference-policy regularization to improve scene adaptation while preserving acquired capabilities. Experiments on the TravelUAV benchmark demonstrate that RecoverFly achieves the best performance on the seen, unseen-map, and unseen-object splits. Moreover, compared to the AerialVLA initialization, RecoverFly improves success rate by 3.12 to 8.37 percentage points under a total rollout budget of about 30\% of the training-set size, validating its effectiveness, robustness, and generalization capabilities.

</details>


### [220] [FaLCon: Facet-Anchored Retrieval with Late Consensus for Sim2Real Text-Based Person Anomaly Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09474)
*Hieu Dinh Trung Pham,Phuong Huu Vu Tran,Thuan Duc Mai,Son Nguyen Minh Le,Khang Le Minh,Hoang Vo,Minh-Chi Phung,Huy Minh Nhat Nguyen,Cuong Tuan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个锚点约束的由粗到精检索框架，用于文本驱动的行人异常搜索（Sim2Real场景）。框架结合全局语义匹配与细粒度验证，通过多视角查询表示、软声明感知融合、判别性重排序和不确定性门控共识模块，在PAB基准上达到95.41% mAP@10的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本行人异常搜索面临两个核心挑战：一是Sim2Real场景下相似候选仅在细微动作、物体交互或外观属性上存在差异，模型主要基于合成数据训练；二是直接对整个图库应用多模态大语言模型进行细粒度验证的计算成本过高。

Method: 提出锚点约束的由粗到精检索框架：(1) 每个查询表示为原始标题、结构化拼接和多个语义分面（外观/动作/物体）；(2) 多异构视觉语言检索器通过鲁棒的逐查询分数校准和软声明感知融合进行集成；(3) 全标题和拼接标题作为锚点保持候选召回，分面提供纠偏证据；(4) 候选池经判别性Qwen3重排序器及两个互补的语义验证模块（基于异常感知完形填空和多智能体证据推理）精化；(5) 不确定性门控共识模块对模糊查询自适应重加权三个专家模型。

Result: 在PAB基准上，软声明感知检索达到86.44% mAP@10，显著优于单一检索骨干；完整框架进一步提升至95.41% mAP@10、94.44% R@1和99.09% R@5。

Conclusion: 保留强全局检索能力同时将昂贵的语义推理限制在小候选池内，对细粒度Sim2Real行人异常搜索是有效的，验证了由粗到精策略在平衡计算效率与检索精度方面的优越性。

Abstract: Text-based person anomaly search requires retrieving real-world pedestrian images from detailed natural-language descriptions using models trained primarily on synthetic data. This Sim2Real setting is particularly challenging because visually similar candidates may differ only in subtle actions, object interactions, or appearance attributes, while applying multimodal large language models to the entire gallery is computationally expensive. We propose an anchor-constrained coarse-to-fine retrieval framework that combines global semantic matching with fine-grained verification. First, each query is represented by its original caption, a structured concatenation, and several semantic facets. Heterogeneous vision-language retrievers are then integrated through robust per-query score calibration and soft claim-aware fusion. Full and concatenated captions serve as anchors to preserve candidate recall, whereas appearance, action, and object facets provide bounded corrective evidence. The resulting candidate pool is further refined by a discriminative Qwen3 reranker and two complementary semantic verification modules based on anomaly-aware cloze completion and multi-agent evidence reasoning. Finally, an uncertainty-gated consensus module adaptively reweights the three experts on ambiguous queries. Experiments on the PAB benchmark show that the proposed soft claim-aware retrieval achieves 86.44% mAP@10, substantially outperforming individual retrieval backbones. The complete framework further improves performance to 95.41% mAP@10, 94.44% R@1, and 99.09% R@5. These results demonstrate that preserving strong global retrieval while restricting expensive semantic reasoning to a small candidate pool is effective for fine-grained Sim2Real person anomaly search. Our code will be available on Github.

</details>


### [221] [Agreement-Based Audio-Visual Segmentation:Champion Report for the MeViS-Audio Track in the 8th LSVOS Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09475)
*Yiwen Ren,Jianing Liu,Yingxin Wang,Kexin Zhang,Licheng Jiao,Lingling Li,Xu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个针对MeViS-Audio赛道（视频物体分割）的解决方案，通过多阶段策略实现：先用ASR将语音转换为文本，再用多个互补的定位和分割模型生成候选掩码轨道，通过掩码一致性投票选取最佳轨道，辅以方向/计数/复数规则修正，最后由视频级分类器判断目标是否存在。系统获得了0.5952的J&F分数，并排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MeViS-Audio任务要求系统根据语音描述在视频中分割目标物体，并在目标缺席时返回空掩码。该任务具有挑战性，单一模型预测不可靠，需要综合利用多个互补模型并做出鲁棒的决策。

Method: 系统采用分阶段流程：(1) 使用Qwen3-ASR将语音转为文本；(2) 用多个互补的定位与分割模型生成多条视频掩码轨道；(3) 选取与其他候选掩码平均一致性最高的轨道；(4) 应用方向、计数和复数规则处理需要多目标跟踪的查询；(5) 视频级分类器结合视觉、音视频及视频内查询分数判断目标是否存在。

Result: 系统在MeViS-Audio赛道获得0.5952的J&F分数，无目标准确率0.7931，目标准确率0.9205，最终得分0.769589，排名第一。

Conclusion: 多模型互补融合加上显式规则修正和视频级分类决策的策略，能够有效提升语音驱动视频物体分割的性能，证明了该分阶段方案在该赛道上的有效性。

Abstract: The MeViS-Audio track asks a system to segment the objects described by a spoken motion expression throughout a video and to return empty masks when the described target is absent. We present a simple staged solution. Qwen3-ASR first converts speech into text. Several video mask tracks are then produced with complementary grounding and segmentation models. Instead of trusting a single prediction, we select the track that has the highest average mask agreement with the other candidates. A small set of explicit direction, count, and plural rules corrects queries that require more than ordinary single-object tracking. Finally, a video-level classifier combines visual, audio-visual, and within-video query scores to decide whether any target is present. The submitted system obtains 0.5952 J &F, 0.7931 no-target accuracy, 0.9205 target accuracy, and a final score of 0.769589. The challenge organizers notified our team that this result ranked first in the track.

</details>


### [222] [Beyond Uniform Restoration: Empowering All-in-One Restoration with Pixel-Level Multimodal Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09482)
*Chunxiao Liu,Wei Liu,Anbin Xiong,Erli Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MGN-AIR，一种像素级图像复原框架，通过估计像素级视觉提示并融合文本与视觉提示，为每个像素提供全局和局部的退化线索，实现细粒度的修复控制，在去噪、去雨、去模糊、去雾、去雪和低光增强等多个任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全合一图像复原方法通常对整个图像采用统一的复原策略，忽略了不同区域可能遭受不同类型和不同程度退化的问题，缺乏像素级别的精细控制能力。

Method: 提出MGN-AIR框架，首先学习估计像素级视觉提示，随后结合文本提示和视觉提示分别提供全局和局部的退化线索，指导模型确定每个像素位置的关注重点和对应的复原方式。

Result: 在去噪、去雨、去模糊、去雾、去雪和低光增强等多个全合一图像复原基准上进行广泛实验，结果表明所提方法一致且显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 像素级复原策略比统一复原策略更具优势，能够实现更精细、更准确的图像复原效果，为全合一图像复原任务提供了新的有效思路。

Abstract: All-in-one image restoration is a unified low-level vision task that aims to effectively recover high-quality images from inputs degraded by various types and levels of corruption using a single model. Recent works have achieved remarkable progress by learning degradation-adaptive prompts or network architectures. However, these methods typically apply a uniform restoration strategy across the entire image, neglecting the fact that different regions may suffer from distinct degradation types and varying degrees of severity. In contrast, we propose to perform restoration at the pixel level, thereby enabling more fine-grained and precise control over the restoration process. Specifically, we present MGN-AIR, a novel pixel-level restoration framework for all-in-one image restoration. Our approach first learns to estimate a pixel-level visual prompt. Then, it leverages both textual and visual prompts to provide global and local degradation cues, guiding the model on where to look and how to restore at each pixel. We conduct extensive experiments on multiple all-in-one image restoration benchmarks, covering a wide range of tasks including denoising, deraining, deblurring, dehazing, desnowing, and low-light enhancement. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed method consistently and significantly outperforms existing approaches.

</details>


### [223] [GeoRoute: Geometry-Aware Hybrid Inference for Traffic Future-Frame Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09493)
*Khang Minh Le,Hieu Dinh Trung Pham,Luu Thanh Danh,Nam-Tien Le,Hieu Anh Ngo,Phuong Huu Vu Tran,Son Nguyen Minh Le,Nguyen Trong Nghia,Tu Tran Thi Cam,Huy Minh Nhat Nguyen,Cuong Tuan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个无需训练的推理框架，通过多帧时间上下文和视角条件路由来稳定预训练视频扩散模型在长时程交通场景预测中的静态几何结构。前端摄像头视频使用多帧深度分层渲染器投影历史帧的静态几何，异质视角则通过冻结的视觉-语言模型推断相机组别并选择专门的运动预测器。在AI City Challenge Track 5基准上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程未来帧预测对自动驾驶和交通系统至关重要，但现有潜在视频扩散模型直接应用于结构化交通场景时存在几何不稳定和时间连贯性退化问题，如时间重影、几何漂移和物体运动不一致。

Method: 提出训练免费的推理阶段优化框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 多帧深度分层渲染器，从观测历史帧投影静态几何并保留生成模型产生的动态区域；(2) 基于冻结视觉-语言模型的视角条件路由机制，推断相机组别并选择特定运动预测器。

Result: 在AI City Challenge Track 5基准上取得前十名的竞争性性能，证明该方法能提升静态几何稳定性和低层结构保真度。

Conclusion: 几何感知的推理时优化和视角条件混合推理可以在不改变原始模型架构的情况下改善交通场景长时程视频预测的静态几何稳定性，为预训练生成模型的推理阶段增强提供了可行思路。

Abstract: Long-horizon future-frame prediction is important for autonomous driving, traffic surveillance, and intelligent transportation systems, yet remains challenging due to temporal ghosting, geometry drift, and inconsistent object motion. Recent latent video diffusion models have achieved impressive visual quality, but directly applying them to structured traffic scenes often leads to unstable geometry and degraded temporal coherence over extended horizons. We present a training-free inference framework that stabilizes reliable static structure in pretrained video predictions through multi-frame temporal context and view-conditioned routing. For front-camera videos, our method refines generated futures with a multi-frame depth-layered renderer that projects static geometry from observed history frames while preserving dynamic regions from the generative base model. For heterogeneous traffic views, a frozen vision-language model infers a coarse camera group from the observed clip and selects a specialized motion-based predictor. The framework requires neither retraining nor fine-tuning of the underlying video model and can be applied directly to pretrained generators. We validate the proposed framework on the AI City Challenge Track 5 benchmark, where our final system achieves competitive performance among the top-ranked teams. These results demonstrate that geometry-aware inference-time refinement and view-conditioned hybrid inference can improve static-geometry stability and low-level structural fidelity without changing the original model architecture.

</details>


### [224] [SwissCrop25: A National Multi-Year Benchmark for Operational Crop Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09497)
*Thomas Lauber,Mehmet Ozgur Turkoglu,Sélène Ledain,Helge Aasen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SwissCrop25，一个覆盖瑞士全国、跨越2019-2025七个生长季的作物制图基准数据集。该数据集包含73类细粒度作物分类（含草地管理类型）、5类非作物土地覆盖类别以及Sentinel-2时序和每日温度观测数据。作者定义了留一年验证协议，在真实部署条件下比较U-TAE、TSViT和Galileo三种架构，发现领域特定模型优于基础模型，TSViT整体最优，比U-TAE的macro-mIoU高出12个百分点，并揭示了季节内不同模型的性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作物制图数据集只能孤立地评估模型的跨年份泛化能力、细粒度作物分类能力和农田与周边景观区分能力，缺乏一个能同时检验这三项需求的综合性基准数据集。

Method: 构建覆盖瑞士全国七个生长季的基准数据集，融合Sentinel-2时间序列、每日温度观测、73类细粒度作物分类和5类非作物土地覆盖；定义留一年验证协议，采用联合农田边界划定和作物分类任务，评估U-TAE（卷积时序注意力）、TSViT（Transformer时空模型）和Galileo（地球观测基础模型）三种代表性架构。

Result: 领域特定模型在整体性能上优于基础模型Galileo；TSViT取得最佳整体性能，较U-TAE在macro-mIoU上领先12个百分点；数据集揭示了显著的跨年分布偏移，且温度衍生的物候信息能提升模型鲁棒性；季节内评估显示U-TAE在生长季早期表现更好，而TSViT在后期凭借更好的罕见类区分能力占据优势。

Conclusion: SwissCrop25提供了一个符合真实操作条件的挑战性测试平台，能够揭示传统基准无法体现的模型架构差异，为操作级作物制图系统的评估与开发提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Operational crop mapping requires models that generalise across years, resolve fine-grained crop taxonomies, and distinguish cropland from surrounding landscapes. However, existing crop mapping datasets enable evaluation of these requirements only in isolation. We therefore introduce SwissCrop25, a national-scale crop mapping benchmark dataset spanning seven growing seasons (2019-2025). SwissCrop25 combines Sentinel-2 time series, daily temperature observations, a fine-grained 73 crop taxonomy including grassland management types, and 5 explicit non-crop land cover classes. To evaluate realistic deployment conditions, we define a leave-one-year-out protocol with joint cropland delineation and crop classification for benchmarking representative crop mapping architectures. Evaluating U-TAE (convolutional temporal-attention model), TSViT (transformer-based spatio-temporal model), and Galileo (EO foundation model) reveals differences between architectures hidden by conventional benchmarks. In this setting, domain-specific models outperform Galileo, with TSViT achieving the best overall performance and a 12 pp macro-mIoU advantage over U-TAE. SwissCrop25 also exposes substantial interannual distribution shifts and shows that incorporating temperature-derived phenological information improves robustness. Finally, in-season evaluation reveals a trade-off between models, with U-TAE performing better early in the season and TSViT gaining an advantage later through improved rare-class discrimination. SwissCrop25 provides a challenging testbed for evaluating crop mapping systems under realistic operational conditions and is publicly released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/EOA-team/SwissCrop25 .

</details>


### [225] [XFeat Revisited: Reproducibility and Evaluation of a Lightweight Image Matcher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09519)
*Lazar Đoković,Aimee Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对轻量级局部特征提取与匹配器XFeat进行可复现性研究，重新实现其架构并评估，发现原论文、补充材料与公开代码存在多处不一致；复现模型在标准基准上与原模型表现接近甚至更好，但架构消融显示并行关键点分支的重要性被高估，跳跃连接位置证据不足；下游评估中单应估计结果一致，而Aachen视觉定位低于原报告值；零样本跨模态匹配在严重模态偏移下性能显著退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
