Therefore, deploying open-sourced large language models on local servers can be considered as an efficient approach to design and implement creative embodied AI algorithms with lower cost and more stable free usage. Inspired by this ordinary motivation, we originally propose and implement the "Socratic Models-ChatGLM", which is a well-performed algorithm for multi-modal interactive control of robotic arm based on offline large language models via the facile PyBullet platform, even presents extraordinary potential to address complicated text-image integrated multi-step long-horizon robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [293] [Spatiotemporal Context-dependent Personalized Movement Compensation in Delayed Telemanipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08200)
*Sai Jiang,Zonghe Chua*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以人为本的个性化运动缩放方法，针对每位参与者拟合延迟、方向、距离特异的缩放参数，以缓解遥操作中的通信延迟问题。在虚拟模拟器和远程手术机器人上进行了评估，结果显示与传统无辅助条件相比，关键指标上实现了最高20-25%的性能提升，且个性化方案在特定条件下（内向短距离、中等延迟）带来了额外精度优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作中的通信延迟会破坏视觉运动协调并降低任务精度。现有的运动缩放方法通常采用统一增益，忽视了用户个体差异和任务情境（延迟、方向、距离）的变化，亟需一种能够适配个体和上下文差异的自适应方案。

Method: 为每位参与者分别拟合延迟、方向、距离特异的缩放参数；在虚拟模拟器中执行延迟到达任务；通过最小化模拟中的平均过冲量来计算各条件组合下的缩放增益；在虚拟模拟器和远程手术机器人上分别进行评估，采用过冲量、端点误差、轨迹平滑度、运动经济性和复合误差-时间指标等多项评价指标。

Result: 运动缩放相对于无辅助条件持续改善了性能，关键指标上获得最高20-25%的收益；在较长延迟下效果最为显著；个性化在中等延迟下内向短距离到达任务中提供了额外的精度增益。

Conclusion: 个性化运动缩放有望成为更具自适应性遥操作框架的基础，该框架可整合情境信息以提升远程操作过程的安全性和精度。

Abstract: Communication delay remains a central challenge in telerobotics, where it disrupts visuomotor coordination and reduces task precision. Motion scaling is an effective countermeasure to delay-induced overshoot, yet typical deployments rely on uniform gains that neglect individual and contextual variability. We propose a human-centered method that fits personalized delay-, direction-, and distance-specific scaling parameters for each participant. We conducted experiments with twenty participants who performed delayed reaching tasks in a virtual simulator. Scaling gains were computed to minimize mean overshoot in simulation in each combination of experimental conditions. Evaluation was done in simulation and on a telesurgical robot to evaluate assistance benefits. Performance was assessed across multiple delays, distances, and movement directions using overshoot, endpoint error, trajectory smoothness, economy of motion, and a composite error-time metric. Motion scaling consistently improved performance relative to unassisted trials, yielding up to 20-25% performance gains in key metrics. Effects were most pronounced at longer delays. Personalization demonstrated additional accuracy benefits for inward reaching at a short distance under moderate delay. The results highlight the potential of personalized scaling as a foundation for more adaptive frameworks that integrate contextual information to improve the safety and precision of teleoperated procedures.

</details>


### [294] [Action- and Language-Conditioned Video Assessment for Embodied Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08273)
*Hwanhee Kim,Jaehyun Jang,Seungmin Cha,Hyeonseo Yun,Donghoon Lee,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ALVA（动作与语言条件视频评估器），一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的轨迹级评估方法，用于具身智能体在多步自然语言指令任务中的进度评估。ALVA采用两阶段流程：先基于执行动作对帧间视觉转换进行摘要，再结合指令评估摘要生成离散的轨迹级进度分数。在模拟3D家庭环境中，ALVA表现出保守的评估模式，近零假阳性率，作为闭环策略优化的终端反馈时优于静态图像和嵌入相似度基线，缩小了与真实标签oracle的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法基于最终帧匹配或连续嵌入相似度来评估指令完成度，容易忽略执行过程中中间转换（intermediate transitions）所包含的关键信息，而这些中间转换对于判断指令是否真正完成至关重要。因此需要一个能够同时考虑视觉观察、动作序列和自然语言指令的轨迹级评估机制。

Method: ALVA使用预训练视觉语言模型（VLM），采用两阶段评估框架：第一阶段，根据执行的动作序列对帧与帧之间的视觉转换进行总结（action-conditioned video summarization）；第二阶段，将生成的视频摘要与自然语言指令进行比较评估，输出离散的轨迹级进度分数（trajectory-level progress score）。该评估器同时以视觉观察、动作序列和语言指令为条件。

Result: 在模拟3D家庭环境中，ALVA表现出保守评估特征，假阳性率接近零。用作闭环策略优化的终端反馈时，它比静态图像和基于嵌入相似度的视觉基线提供更有效的反馈，并缩小了与真实标签oracle之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 动作和语言条件的视频评估（ALVA）被证实是所评估模拟具身控制任务中一种可解释、有效的反馈机制，为具身智能体的指令完成度评估提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-based embodied agents executing multi-step natural language instructions require feedback mechanisms that assess task progress over complete trajectories. Conventional approaches based on final-frame matching or continuous embedding similarity may overlook intermediate transitions that are necessary for determining whether an instruction has been completed. We propose ALVA (Action- and Language-Conditioned Video Assessment), a trajectory evaluator that conditions its assessment on visual observations, the executed action sequence, and the natural language instruction. The method uses a pre-trained vision-language model (VLM) in two stages: it first summarizes frame-to-frame visual transitions conditioned on the executed actions and then assesses the generated summary with respect to the instruction to produce a discrete trajectory-level progress score. In simulated 3D household environments, ALVA exhibits a conservative assessment pattern with near-zero false-positive rates. When used as terminal feedback for closed-loop policy optimization, it provides more effective feedback than the evaluated static image and embedding-based visual baselines and reduces the performance gap to a ground-truth oracle. These results support action- and language-conditioned video assessment as an interpretable feedback mechanism for the evaluated simulated embodied-control tasks.

</details>


### [295] [Scalable High-Speed Lateral Control for Single-Body and Articulated Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08293)
*Aashish Shaju,Steve Southward,Mehdi Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的横向控制框架，用于单体和铰接式自动驾驶车辆在高速下的鲁棒路径跟踪。该框架扩展了基于回旋曲线的控制器，引入三个关键改进：优化前瞻与振荡抑制的切向检查和Frechet距离方法、实时轨迹段分类动态调整前瞻搜索范围、以及双自适应速率控制的前瞻机制。通过引入灵活的跟踪点选择和曲率到转向查表，支持拖拉机后轴、铰接点和拖车相关位置的控制，无需改变核心控制架构。基于TruckSim和Simulink的高保真仿真验证表明，该框架在不同车型上均实现了稳定高速跟踪、低横向误差和可接受的侧向加速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径跟踪控制器在处理高速、复杂道路条件下的单体和铰接式车辆时存在前瞻距离难以自适应、振荡明显等问题，且缺乏能够统一支持不同车辆构型的横向控制架构。

Method: 在回旋曲线（clothoid）控制器基础上提出三项关键扩展：(1)集成切向检查与Frechet距离方法优化前瞻距离并抑制振荡；(2)实时轨迹段分类以动态调整前瞻搜索范围；(3)双自适应速率控制的前瞻机制，响应横向偏差和前方路径几何。同时引入灵活的跟踪点选择与曲率到转向查表机制，支持不同车辆构型而无需修改核心控制架构。

Result: 在TruckSim和Simulink高保真仿真中，针对单体和铰接式车辆在双车道变换和蜿蜒道路场景下进行验证。结果表明：高速下路径跟踪稳定、振荡减少、前瞻自适应有效、横向偏差低、侧向加速度可接受，且适用于不同车辆构型。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一横向控制架构具有从单体到铰接式自动驾驶车辆的可扩展性，为多车型平台的横向控制提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a scalable lateral control framework for robust path tracking of single-body and articulated autonomous vehicles at high speeds. A clothoid-based controller is extended with three key adaptations: 1) an integrated tangential check and Frechet distance method for optimized lookahead and oscillation mitigation; 2) real-time trajectory segment classification for dynamic adjustment of the lookahead search range; and 3) a dual-adaptive, rate-controlled lookahead mechanism responsive to cross-track error and upcoming path geometry. To support different vehicle configurations, the framework also incorporates flexible tracking point selection and curvature-to-steering lookup tables, enabling control of points such as the tractor rear axle, hitch point, or trailer-related locations without changing the core control architecture. The controller is evaluated using high-fidelity TruckSim and Simulink simulations for both standalone and articulated vehicles across dual lane changes and winding-road scenarios. Results demonstrate stable high-speed path tracking, reduced oscillations, effective lookahead adaptation, low cross-track errors, and acceptable lateral acceleration across different vehicle configurations. The results support the use of a unified lateral control architecture that can scale from single-body to articulated autonomous vehicles.

</details>


### [296] [Diminishing Returns of Intelligence: The Non-Linear Relationship Between LLM Scale and User Perception in Short-Duration Open-Ended Social Human-Robot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08320)
*Amanda Rasille Røn Volf,Morten Roed Frederiksen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过19名参与者的被试内实验，比较了驱动社交机器人的LLM（4B、8B、30B参数）在短暂开放式人机互动中的表现，发现更大模型并未带来显著的用户感知优势，但频繁使用AI的用户更敏感于模型能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用于驱动具身社交智能体，但更大的模型是否能在短暂的人机交互中提升用户感知仍不明确，需要实证研究来验证模型规模对用户体验的实际影响。

Method: 采用被试内实验设计，19名参与者与由Qwen3-VL（4B、8B、30B参数）驱动的机器人面部进行开放式社交互动，从感知智能、自然度、愉悦感和幽默感四个维度进行评估，并分析AI交互频率与评分的关系。

Result: 30B模型未在整体上显著优于小模型，感知自然度和智能方面与4B模型无显著差异；但AI交互频率与30B模型的智能评分呈显著相关，表明经验丰富的用户对模型能力差异更敏感。

Conclusion: 在短暂开放式社交人机交互中，模型规模存在边际收益递减效应，对话流畅性、响应性和社交得体行为的重要性不亚于原始参数数量。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to drive embodied social agents, yet it remains unclear whether larger models improve user perception during brief human-robot encounters. This paper examines the effect of LLM parameter size on short-duration, open-ended social interactions with a robot interface. In a within-subjects study, 19 participants interacted with robot faces driven by Qwen3-VL models at 4B, 8B, and 30B parameters. Participants evaluated the interactions in terms of perceived intelligence, naturalness, enjoyment, and humor. Results showed no significant overall preference for the 30B model over the smaller variants, including no significant advantage over the 4B model in perceived naturalness or intelligence. A significant relationship between AI interaction frequency and intelligence rankings for the 30B model suggests that more experienced users may be more sensitive to differences in model capability. Overall, the findings indicate diminishing returns from model scaling in brief open-ended social HRI, where conversational flow, responsiveness, and socially appropriate behavior potentially matter as much as raw parameter count.

</details>


### [297] [MPPI Planning with Gaussian Based Human Cost Function for Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08323)
*Chinmay Mundane*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出预测性高斯交互场（PGIF），一种时空成本函数，将行人预测传播到整个规划时域，并将其编码为各向异性高斯排斥场，使MPPI控制器在拥挤动态环境中实现0%碰撞率，且无额外计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全机器人导航需要预测行人未来位置，但现有MPPI实现通常将行人编码为静态点障碍物，低估了动态场景中的风险，导致碰撞率过高。

Method: 提出Predictive Gaussian Interaction Fields (PGIF)，一种闭式时空成本公式：将行人运动预测前向传播至整个规划时域，编码为沿行人运动方向对准的各向异性高斯排斥场；场的前向扩散随行人速度增长，形成运动锥危险区，对进入行人路径的机器人轨迹施加更强惩罚，同时对rollout完全可并行化。

Result: 在300个随机人群场景、三个密度层级下评估：PGIF-MPPI在每个密度层级均达到0%碰撞率，而vanilla MPPI的碰撞率最高达82%；同时保持实时规划性能，无测量到的计算开销增加。

Conclusion: PGIF为MPPI提供了一种高效且有效的动态行人感知成本建模方案，在不牺牲实时性的前提下显著提升了拥挤场景中的导航安全性。

Abstract: Safe robot navigation in crowded spaces requires planning that accounts for where people will be, not only where they are now. Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is an effective sampling-based planner, but many implementations encode humans as static point obstacles at their current positions, underestimating risk in dynamic scenes. We propose Predictive Gaussian Interaction Fields (PGIF), a spatiotemporal cost formulation that propagates pedestrian predictions forward over the full planning horizon and encodes them as anisotropic Gaussian repulsive fields aligned with each pedestrian's direction of motion. The forward spread of each field grows with the pedestrian's speed, creating a motion cone danger zone that penalises robot trajectories entering the pedestrian's path of travel more strongly than those approaching from behind. The formulation is closed-form and fully parallelisable across rollouts, adding no measurable computational overhead. Evaluated over 300 randomised crowd scenarios at three density levels, PGIF-MPPI achieves a 0\% collision rate at every density level, compared with up to 82\% for vanilla MPPI, while maintaining real-time planning performance.

</details>


### [298] [Stochastic Physics-Informed Neural Networks on Lie Groups for Learning Underwater Vehicle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08356)
*Evan F. Palmer,Ross L. Hatton,Geoffrey A. Hollinger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机物理信息神经网络的数据驱动框架，用于学习水下航行器的随机动力学模型。该方法结合Euler-Poincaré动力学和李群几何，并利用结构保持的随机积分和矩匹配技术，确保训练过程的几何一致性，在仿真和真实港口环境中均取得了准确且鲁棒的建模效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下航行器在基础设施检测和科学采样等自主任务中需要精确的运动模型，但传统基于物理的建模方法难以准确刻画其复杂运动特性。

Method: 利用Euler-Poincaré动力学和李群几何构建随机物理信息神经网络架构，通过结构保持的随机积分方法以及矩匹配和有限维匹配技术实现几何一致的训练。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实港口环境中（航行器在码头桩间导航），该方法能够学习到准确且鲁棒的动力学模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够学习准确鲁棒的动力学模型，从而在复杂的海洋环境中实现安全基于模型的自主控制。

Abstract: Accurate models of underwater vehicle motion are needed for autonomous execution of marine tasks like infrastructure inspection and scientific sampling. However, such motion is challenging to characterize using traditional physics-based methods. This paper presents a novel data-driven framework for learning stochastic underwater vehicle dynamics. Using Euler-Poincaré dynamics and the geometry of Lie groups, we develop a stochastic physics-informed neural network architecture that respects the physical and geometric constraints of underwater vehicles. Our approach leverages structure-preserving stochastic integration and builds upon moment matching and finite dimensional matching to ensure geometrically-consistent training. We evaluate our approach in simulation and on an underwater vehicle navigating dock pylons in a harbor environment. The results demonstrate that our method learns accurate and robust dynamics models, enabling safe model-based control in challenging marine environments.

</details>


### [299] [EsaacSim: A Multimodal Event Camera Add-on for NVIDIA Isaac Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08522)
*Ignacio Bugueno-Cordova,Malte Kuhlmann,Nicolás Navarro-Guerrero,Miguel Campusano,Rodrigo Verschae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了EsaacSim，一个基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim的多模态事件相机仿真插件，支持灰度与Bayer RGGB事件生成，并通过运动引导的帧间合成策略提升有效时间分辨率，实验证明其在不同分辨率下能以240-960Hz的有效事件率在线同步生成RGB、APS、事件、深度和IMU数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机在机器人领域日益重要，但传感器获取困难且缺乏与现代机器人平台集成的仿真工具，限制了其推广应用。

Method: 在NVIDIA Isaac Sim中实现可配置事件相机插件，支持多分辨率、灰度与Bayer RGGB事件生成，通过运动引导的帧间隙合成策略提高有效时间分辨率，并通过原生ROS2接口同步输出RGB、APS、事件、深度和IMU数据。

Result: 实验在RTX 4060 GPU上实现6.98-27.28ms（灰度）和7.58-29.16ms（Bayer RGGB）的事件流生成耗时，额外GPU内存占用低于400MB，支持五种分辨率和240-960Hz的有效事件率。

Conclusion: EsaacSim能够有效支持机器人研究和合成数据生成中的在线多模态事件相机仿真，已发布早期版本供社区使用。

Abstract: Event-based vision is becoming an increasingly important sensing paradigm for robotics, yet its adoption remains limited by sensor availability and the lack of integrated simulation tools for modern robotics platforms. This paper presents EsaacSim, a multimodal event camera add-on for NVIDIA Isaac Sim that enables online simulation of configurable event cameras with grayscale and Bayer RGGB event generation. The framework supports multiple event camera resolutions and provides synchronized RGB, APS, event, depth, and IMU outputs through native ROS2 interfaces. A motion-guided frame-gap synthesis strategy further increases the effective temporal resolution while preserving compatibility with the Isaac Sim rendering pipeline. Experimental evaluation demonstrates synchronized multimodal simulation across representative robotic scenes and efficient online performance over five event camera resolutions at effective event rates from 240 to 960Hz. Event stream generation requires 6.98--27.28ms for grayscale events and 7.58--29.16ms for Bayer RGGB events while using less than 400MB of additional GPU memory on an NVIDIA RTX~4060 GPU. These results show that EsaacSim enables supports online multimodal event-camera simulation for robotics research and synthetic data generation. We release an early version of the simulator and report its current architecture and performance.

</details>


### [300] [Curriculum Generation under Structured Parametric Environments for Robust Navigation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08545)
*Prishita Ray*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于重参数化的课程生成框架，通过单向梯度优化自动生成结构化连续环境参数课程，并引入分布偏移正则化目标以增强多模态观测空间下的鲁棒性。在Car Racing和Bipedal Walker两个连续控制环境中，该方法在五个随机种子下一致优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体的鲁棒导航策略需要适应连续变化的环境条件（如转弯率、障碍物、摩擦力、坑洞和坡度）。课程生成是提升泛化能力的有效机制，但如何以样本高效且自动化的方式设计课程仍具挑战性。

Method: 提出重参数化课程生成框架，针对结构化连续环境参数使用单向梯度优化；同时在由图像和标量输入构成的多模态观测空间中引入分布偏移正则化目标，以促进更细粒度的隐表示学习。

Result: 在2D障碍物Car Racing变体和Bipedal Walker变体两个连续控制环境中，该方法在五个随机种子下一致优于普通策略训练、随机参数采样、手动课程、前沿方法、SPRL、ALP-GMM和反向课程学习等基线。消融实验验证了重参数化课程机制的有效性，同时表明辅助正则化目标的效果依赖于具体环境。

Conclusion: 重参数化的课程生成框架能够有效提升智能体在连续环境参数变化下的泛化性能，分布偏移正则化对多模态观测空间的鲁棒性提升具有环境依赖性的作用。

Abstract: Robust navigation policies for autonomous agents must generalize across continuously varying environmental conditions such as turn rates, obstacles, friction, pits, and slopes. Curriculum generation provides a principled mechanism for improving generalization by progressively adapting training environments, but designing such curricula in a sample-efficient and automated manner remains challenging. This paper proposes a reparameterized curriculum generation framework for structured continuous environment parameters using unidirectional gradient-based optimization. To improve robustness in multimodal observation spaces consisting of image-based and scalar inputs, a distribution-shift regularization objective is incorporated to encourage the learning of finer-grained latent representations. The proposed method is evaluated across two continuous-control OpenAI Gym environments: a 2D obstacle-based Car Racing variant and Bipedal Walker variant, where coupled environment parameters jointly influence policy performance. Across five random seeds, our method consistently outperforms vanilla policy training, random parameter sampling, manual curricula, frontier-based methods, Self-Paced Reinforcement Learning (SPRL), Absolute Learning Progress with Gaussian Mixture Models (ALP-GMM), and reverse curriculum learning baselines. Ablation studies further demonstrate the effectiveness of the reparameterized curriculum mechanism across both environments, while highlighting environment-dependent benefits of the auxiliary regularization objective.

</details>


### [301] [Vid2WAM: Distilling Video Diffusion Priors into World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08558)
*Chenhao Qiu,Ruixiang Wang,Runyi Zhao,Sixu Lin,Songen Gu,Shufeng Nan,Guiliang Liu,Kui Jia,Yanwei Fu,Simo Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Vid2WAM框架，利用离线蒸馏将大型视频基础模型的视觉扩散先验转移到紧凑的世界动作模型（WAM）学生中，不再依赖目标任务的专家轨迹作为未来监督来源，通过任务条件未来滚动和逆向动力学模型生成伪动作进行训练，显著提升样本效率和新任务泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）通过联合建模未来视觉动态和动作来提升机器人策略学习，但其可扩展性和泛化能力受限于对昂贵专家示范数据的依赖。作者质疑未来监督是否必须来自目标任务专家轨迹，探索利用无需专家示范的替代监督来源来降低WAM训练成本。

Method: Vid2WAM采用两个互补通道进行蒸馏：（1）任务条件未来滚动直接监督学生网络的未来预测分支；（2）逆向动力学模型从合成未来数据中恢复具身特异性伪动作用于动作学习。同时引入源感知残差动作自适应机制，在共享动作骨干上学习源特定修正，以缓解合成与真实监督混合时的干扰问题。推理阶段丢弃视频教师和逆向动力学模型，仅保留轻量WAM学生。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明，Vid2WAM在有限专家示范下显著提升了新任务泛化能力和数据效率，同时保持了低延迟推理性能。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了WAM的训练可以脱离目标任务专家轨迹的限制，通过视频基础模型的视觉先验蒸馏实现高效、可扩展的机器人策略学习，为降低具身智能数据成本提供了新思路。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot policy learning by jointly modeling future visual dynamics and actions. However, their scalability and generalization remain constrained by their reliance on costly expert demonstrations. We challenge this by asking whether future supervision for WAMs must originate from target-task expert trajectories. In this paper, we propose Vid2WAM, an offline distillation framework that transfers visual diffusion priors from a large video foundation model into a compact WAM student. Given an observation and language instruction, Vid2WAM distills supervision through two complementary channels: task-conditioned future rollouts directly supervise the student's future prediction branch, while an inverse dynamics model recovers embodiment-specific pseudo-actions for action learning. To robustly integrate synthetic and real supervision, we introduce source-aware residual action adaptation that learns source-specific corrections around a shared action backbone and mitigates interference from noisy pseudo-actions. During inference, both the video teacher and inverse dynamics model are discarded, leaving only the WAM student for efficient deployment. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that Vid2WAM improves novel-task generalization and data efficiency under limited expert demonstrations while preserving low-latency inference.

</details>


### [302] [Knowledge-Distilled End-to-End Reinforcement Learning for Smooth 6-DOF Thrust Control and Rapid Adaptation to Ocean Currents in Remotely Operated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08598)
*Tiankuang Wen,Huiping Li,Gang Liu,Yong Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了TSRCA-PPO方法，一种面向遥控潜水器（ROV）控制的端到端强化学习方法，通过两阶段蒸馏学习框架来同时实现低稳态跟踪误差、快速瞬态响应、节能运行和平滑推力输出，在仿真环境下各项指标均优于传统级联P-PID控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端强化学习控制方法在洋流扰动下难以实现最优控制，缺乏一个能同时实现低稳态跟踪误差、快速瞬态响应、高能效和平滑控制力输出的统一控制框架。

Method: 提出TSRCA-PPO方法，核心创新包括：1）两阶段蒸馏学习框架，通过学习近最优策略；2）奖励函数设计创新；3）特权多编码器架构。

Result: 消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。仿真结果表明TSRCA-PPO在所有评估指标上均优于传统级联P-PID控制器，其中稳态位置误差、稳态姿态误差、整定时间、能量指标和推力平滑度指标分别降至P-PID对应值的42.7%、76.5%、10.6%、93.5%和15.9%。

Conclusion: TSRCA-PPO方法能够有效应对洋流扰动下的ROV控制问题，在性能上全面超越传统P-PID控制方法，为实现鲁棒、节能且平滑的ROV自主控制提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: With the continuous improvement of computational capabilities, end-to-end reinforcement learning has been rapidly developed for remotely operated vehicles control. Nevertheless, existing end-to-end reinforcement-learningbased methods still face challenges in achieving optimal control under oceancurrent disturbances. In particular, there remains a lack of a unified control framework that can simultaneously achieve low steady-state tracking error, rapid transient response, energy-efficient operation, and smooth controlforce outputs under disturbances. To address the issue, this paper proposes the thrust smoothness rapid current adaptation proximal policy optimization (TSRCA-PPO) method which learns a near-optimal strategy by a twostage distillation learning framework. The core innovations of this work lie in the reward-function design and the privileged multi-encoder architecture. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of each module. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed TSRCA-PPO method consistently outperforms the conventional cascaded P-PID controller across all evaluation metrics. Specifically, TSRCA-PPO reduces the steady-state position error, steady-state attitude error, settling time, energy index, and thrustsmoothness index to 42.7%, 76.5%, 10.6%, 93.5%, and 15.9% of the corresponding P-PID values, respectively.

</details>


### [303] [RAG-Based Auto-Configuration for Industrial Fieldbus Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08618)
*Aadil Gani Ganie,Saad Ezzini,Naveed Farooz Marazi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SysName是一个面向工业设备调试的自动化配置管线，能够从异构PDF手册中端到端提取协议参数并生成配置。通过混合稠密-稀疏检索索引和本体图谱（基于ECLASS、AAS、SOSA/SSN），结合BGE-M3编码器和交叉编码器重排器定位相关手册段落，使用本地LLM（T=0.1）生成对齐本体的JSON-LD配置，并配备两阶段弃权门和SHACL验证保障安全。在28个金标查询上HitRate@10达0.96，MRR@10从0.56提升至0.63，字段级F1=0.87，单设备端到端运行时间2.6-6.6秒，零不安全写入和零静默失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业设备调试目前依赖工程师从异构PDF手册中手动提取数百个协议特定参数并转录到监控系统中，这一流程耗时且容易出错，亟需自动化解决方案来提高效率和可靠性。

Method: 构建混合稠密-稀疏检索索引，结合基于ECLASS、AAS和SOSA/SSN的本体图谱；使用BGE-M3编码器和交叉编码器重排器；本地LLM（T=0.1）通过协议特定提示和四步修复管线生成JSON-LD配置；采用两阶段弃权门（重排器分数阈值+IRI解析率）阻止不安全调用，最后通过SHACL验证。

Result: 混合检索器HitRate@10达0.96；重排器将MRR@10从0.56提升至0.63且弃权判定分数分离完美；生成器字段级F1=0.87，12次运行中9次精确匹配；H100 GPU上单设备端到端2.6-6.6秒完成，五设备基准上零不安全写入、零静默失败；案例研究（UR5e机器人，254页手册）字段级F1=1.0。

Conclusion: SysName在生产环境中展现了高精度和高安全性的自动化设备配置能力；组件级评测揭示了OPC-UA生成是唯一系统性失败来源（端到端指标无法发现该问题）；两阶段弃权门有效保障了安全，所有不成功运行均被明确标记而非静默失败。

Abstract: Industrial device commissioning requires engineers to manually extract hundreds of protocol-specific parameters from heterogeneous PDF manuals and transcribe them into supervisory control systems, a time-intensive, error-prone workflow. This paper presents SysName, a production-oriented pipeline that automates device configuration end-to-end for Modbus RTU, OPC-UA, Profibus DP, and CANopen. It builds a hybrid dense-sparse retrieval index augmented by an ontology graph derived from ECLASS, AAS, and SOSA/SSN, using a BGE-M3 encoder with a cross-encoder reranker to surface relevant manual passages. A local LLM (T=0.1) generates ontology-aligned JSON-LD configurations via protocol-specific prompts and a four-step repair pipeline. A two-stage abstention gate, combining a reranker-score threshold and an IRI resolution ratio, blocks unsafe LLM invocations and filters low-coverage configurations before SHACL validation. On a gold set of 28 field-level queries, the hybrid retriever reaches 0.96 HitRate@10, and the reranker raises MRR@10 from 0.56 to 0.63 with perfect score separation for abstention. The generator attains field-level F1=0.87 with exact match on 9 of 12 runs. End-to-end runs on an H100 GPU complete in 2.6-6.6s per device with zero unsafe writes and zero silent failures on a five-device benchmark; every unsuccessful run is flagged by abstention or deployment verification. Component-wise evaluation localises the single systematic failure to OPC-UA generation, invisible to end-to-end metrics alone. A case study commissions a physics-simulated Universal Robots UR5e robot from unmodified vendor documentation (254-page manual, 8-page register list, 496 chunks), reaching field-level F1=1.0 over three runs with read-back and joint-consistency verification. An ablation study and comparison with five industrial-LLM systems complete the analysis.

</details>


### [304] [Anchor-Based AI Approach for Pre-Crash Object Detection Utilizing Micro-Doppler Signatures in Automotive Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08701)
*Patrick Zaumseil,Rainer Engert,Dagmar Steinhauser,Jonathan Wache,Soumya Dewangan,Thomas Brandmeier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于锚点的AI模型，利用高分辨率汽车雷达的微多普勒特征进行碰撞前目标检测和运动参数估计。通过创新的雷达图像膨胀技术放大局部雷达模式，该模型在动态场景下的目标参数估计性能优于传统跟踪方法，显著降低了近场关键区域的漏检率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进的自动驾驶系统依赖于约束系统（如智能安全气囊、自适应安全带）的可靠激活，而这需要前置雷达传感器进行即时精确的目标检测。传统雷达跟踪方法在处理雷达点云稀疏、波动以及多径反射和鬼影目标等问题时存在局限，特别是在碰撞前的关键近场场景中，需要一种更可靠的目标检测和参数估计方法。

Method: 提出了一种锚点（anchor-based）AI模型，用于处理高分辨率雷达数据，特别关注微多普勒特征。开发了一种创新的雷达图像膨胀（dilation）技术，应用于特征输入通道，以放大微多普勒等局部雷达模式，解决雷达点云稀疏和波动的问题。使用量产雷达传感器在碰撞前相关场景中采集数据集，用于模型评估和与先进雷达跟踪方法的对比。

Result: 实验结果表明，该锚点AI模型在动态场景中的目标参数估计方面优于传统跟踪方法，表现出较高的精度和可靠性，能够有效处理不同数据集，在碰撞前场景中显著降低了误报和漏检。

Conclusion: 该锚点AI模型结合雷达图像膨胀技术，能够有效利用微多普勒特征提升碰撞前目标检测和运动参数估计的准确性，为智能安全约束系统的早期可靠激活提供了有力支撑，展示了优于传统跟踪方法的性能优势。

Abstract: Advanced automated driving presents significant potential to improve modern automotive safety systems, but it depends highly on the reliable activation of restraint systems. Forward-looking sensors are crucial for immediate and precise object detection. Recent developments in automotive radar technology enable detailed environment detection and the recognition of high-resolution features, such as micro-Doppler signatures. Combined with advanced AI techniques, these features significantly enhance object detection and improve the accuracy of kinematic parameter estimation. This is essential for the early and reliable activation of irreversible safety systems, such as smart airbags and adaptive seat belts. Therefore, an anchor-based AI model is presented, designed to process high-resolution radar data with an explicit focus on micro-Doppler signatures to improve pre-crash object detection. Furthermore, these signatures can improve the accuracy of kinematic object parameter estimation and reduce false negatives, especially in the critical near-field. To address the challenges of sparse and fluctuating radar point clouds, an innovative radar-image dilation technique on the feature input channels was developed to amplify local radar patterns, like micro-Doppler features. Therefore, this approach increases the system's reliability and increases its ability to detect objects in pre-crash scenarios despite radar multipath reflections and ghost objects. In order to investigate the applicability and compare the model's performance with advanced automotive radar tracking methods, a radar data set using series sensors and pre-crash relevant scenarios was recorded. The results demonstrate the advantages of the anchor-based AI model over established tracking approaches. It excels at estimating object parameters in dynamic scenarios and underscores its ability to process different data sets effectively.

</details>


### [305] [WA-SpecDec: World-Aware Speculative Decoding for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08725)
*Zikang Wen,Yuning Zhang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WA-SpecDec，一种世界感知的投机解码框架，通过在VLA预填充阶段注入世界模型派生的物理场景感知，无需改变松弛接受规则即可在保持更高任务成功率的同时实现1.5倍加速，并将近接触失败率平均降低18.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA投机解码方法在放宽动作令牌接受准则时采用固定的令牌距离容忍度，但该放宽是场景无关的——相同的动作令牌偏差在自由空间可能是无害的，而在接触区域附近可能导致碰撞或抓取失败。这种场景无关的松弛限制了其在真实物理世界中的安全性和可靠性。

Method: WA-SpecDec在VLA预填充阶段注入由世界模型派生的物理场景感知，生成共享的世界感知预填充状态，同时服务于草稿提案和目标验证阶段，不修改现有的松弛接受规则。该框架可适配三种不同的松弛接受方案。

Result: 在三种最先进的松弛接受方案上，WA-SpecDec在更宽松的松弛条件下保持更高的任务成功率并支持更长的接受前缀；在成功率相当的运行点上，相较纯VLA投机解码实现1.5倍匹配成功率加速，平均减少18.6%的近接触失败。

Conclusion: 通过引入世界感知能力，可以在不牺牲任务成功率的前提下实现更激进的投机解码加速，显著改善VLA策略在接触密集型任务中的安全性，为VLA策略的高效部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies generate robot controls autoregressively, making closed-loop latency dominated by repeated target-model forward passes. Speculative decoding reduces this cost by verifying blocks of draft action tokens in parallel, and recent VLA methods further relax token-level acceptance because small differences in action-token space often map to similar continuous controls. However, this relaxation remains scene-agnostic. A fixed token-distance tolerance treats the same action-token deviation as equally safe across states, although deviations that are harmless in free space can cause collisions or grasp failures near contact. We propose WA-SpecDec, a world-aware speculative decoding framework that injects world-model-derived physical scene awareness during the VLA prefill stage, producing shared world-aware prefill states for draft proposal and target verification without changing the relaxed acceptance rule. Across three state-of-the-art relaxed acceptance schemes, WA-SpecDec preserves higher task success under looser relaxation and enables longer accepted prefixes. At comparable-success operating points, WA-SpecDec achieves a 1.5x matched-success speedup over VLA speculative decoding alone and reduces near-contact failure (NCF) by 18.6% on average relative to the corresponding speculative baselines.

</details>


### [306] [OnEvoMemory: Evolving Memory through Online Robot Rollouts for Pretrained Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08749)
*Zhongxi Chen,Shenqi Zong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OnEvoMemory，一种面向预训练机器人策略的价值引导记忆模块，通过维护近期上下文、高价值经验和显著状态转换，并利用离线演示与在线滚动的成败结果自动学习记忆保留策略，从而提升长时程操作任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作需要策略跟踪已完成子任务和关键交互事件，但现有记忆机制过度依赖外部模型或人为预设的更新规则，缺乏灵活性。

Method: OnEvoMemory通过三个核心设计实现：一是同时维护近期上下文、高价值经验和显著状态转换三类信息；二是在线学习哪些经验应被保留，由轨迹结果驱动；三是以离线演示初始化记忆先验，在线滚动中根据成败结果逐步优化记忆选择。

Result: 在长时程操作基准上的实验表明，OnEvoMemory通过离线初始化和在线记忆演化两种方式均显著提升了基础VLA策略的性能。

Conclusion: 价值引导的记忆模块能够使策略自动识别任务阶段转换，避免重复已完成子任务，是一种有效替代外部模型和预设规则的记忆机制。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation requires policies to track completed subtasks and critical interaction events. However, existing memory mechanisms heavily rely on external models or predefined update rules. To address this, we propose OnEvoMemory, a value-guided memory module for pretrained robot policies. It maintains recent context, high-value experiences, and salient transitions, while learning which experiences should be retained from trajectory outcomes. Offline demonstrations initialize the memory prior, whereas successful and unsuccessful online rollouts refine memory selection, helping the policy recognize task-stage transitions and avoid repeating completed subtasks. Experiments on long-horizon manipulation benchmarks show that OnEvoMemory improves the performance of the base VLA policy through both offline initialization and online memory evolution.

</details>


### [307] [PEEL: Parallel Extraction for Long-Horizon Disassembly Planning via Scale-Invariant Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08773)
*Servet B. Bayraktar,Andreas Orthey,Zachary Kingston,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PEEL算法，用于解决长时域多部件物体拆解问题。该算法结合尺度不变的采样方案和多臂老虎机RRT（MAB-RRT）规划器，在并行批量规划器下生成有序的拆解图，指导机器人依次移除部件。实验在76个装配体上实现单部件拆解100%成功率，并用Fetch机械臂成功完成4个包含10-17个部件的长时域拆解任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域多部件物体拆解需要机器人在狭窄、紧致的退出通道中计算无碰撞移除运动序列，这一问题在计算上具有挑战性，现有方法难以高效求解。

Method: PEEL算法包含两个核心组件：(1) 尺度不变采样方案——在预热阶段估计物体尺度，随后利用尺度的方向采样器产生高效的单物体运动；(2) 多臂老虎机RRT（MAB-RRT）规划器——根据奖励信号在不同采样器之间切换，并批量并行运行规划器以生成指明部件移除顺序的有序图。

Result: MAB-RRT在76个装配体上实现了100%的单部件拆解成功率，且对参数具有鲁棒性。将MAB-RRT集成到PEEL后，使用Fetch机械臂成功解决了4个涉及10至17个部件的长时域拆解问题。

Conclusion: PEEL通过并行提取和自适应采样策略，能够高效求解长时域多部件拆解问题，为机器人自动化拆解提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon multi-part object disassembly requires robots to compute feasible sequences of collision-free removal motions, even in the presence of tight, narrow escape corridors. To efficiently solve such disassembly problems, we propose Parallel Extraction for Long-Horizon Disassembly (PEEL), an algorithm which efficiently computes disassembly motions for object assemblies and feeds them to a robot manipulator for execution. PEEL uses sampling-based motion planning to compute single-object motions through the use of a scale-invariant sampling scheme, where the object scale is estimated in a burn-in phase and a subsequent directional sampler exploits the scale. This sampling scheme is integrated into a multi-arm bandit rapidly-exploring random tree (MAB-RRT) planner, which switches between different samplers depending on the reward signal received. Using MAB-RRT, the PEEL algorithm runs a batch of planners in parallel to obtain an ordered graph specifying the sequence in which object parts have to be removed. We show that MAB-RRT can efficiently solve single-part disassemblies with 100 percent success rate on 76 assemblies, and that it is robust to its parameters. By integrating MAB-RRT into PEEL, we solve four long-horizon disassembly problems using the Fetch manipulator robot involving 10 to 17 individual object parts.

</details>


### [308] [SG-WAM: Text-Grounded and Spatial-aware Semantic Guidance for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08839)
*Junjie He,Junfeng Li,Zhide Zhong,Haodong Yan,Ruixin Li,Yangyang Zheng,Jiaguan Zhu,Tianran Zhang,Yuqiao Du,Wen Chen,Shunbo Zhou,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-WAM，一种面向世界-动作模型（WAMs）的语义引导方法，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）作为语义规划器，生成文本锚定和空间感知的语义前瞻，并将其注入世界-动作模型，以提升未来视频生成和动作预测对语言指令的遵循能力，在仿真和真实世界中均验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数世界-动作模型在生成未来视频和预测动作时主要依赖视觉线索而非语言指令，因为现成的文本编码器独立嵌入指令而不结合视觉观察，导致预测视频与语言指令语义不对齐，进而降低动作预测的准确性。

Method: 提出SG-WAM方法，训练一个基于VLM的规划器来预测两种语义前瞻：（1）文本锚定的语义前瞻，用于识别正确的目标对象以锚定指令；（2）空间感知的语义前瞻，提供操作所需的场景几何信息。然后将这些前瞻作为高层语义引导注入世界-动作模型，确保未来视频生成和动作预测都严格遵循语言指令。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的广泛实验表明，该方法在精确操作和指令跟随能力上均展现出优越性。

Conclusion: 通过VLM语义规划器的引导，世界-动作模型能够更好地将语言指令与视觉观察进行对齐，从而提升操作精度和指令跟随能力，为机器人操作中的语义引导提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation. However, most existing WAMs generate future videos and actions by relying mainly on visual cues rather than language instructions, since off-the-shelf text encoders embed instructions independently of visual observations. As a result, the videos predicted by these WAMs are often semantically misaligned with their corresponding language instructions, which degrades the accuracy of the predicted actions. To overcome this limitation, we propose SG-WAM, a semantic guidance method for world-action models that leverages a vision-language model (VLM) as a semantic planner to enhance the instruction-grounding capacity of world-action models. Specifically, we train a VLM-based planner to predict text-grounded and spatial-aware semantic foresight. The text-grounded semantic foresight grounds the instruction by identifying the correct target objects, and the spatial-aware semantic foresight provides the scene geometry for precise manipulation. We then inject this foresight into the world-action model as high-level semantic guidance, ensuring that both future-video generation and action prediction faithfully follow the language instruction. Extensive experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate the superiority of our semantic guidance method, showcasing precise manipulation and strong instruction-following capabilities.

</details>


### [309] [Hierarchical Topology-Aware Planning and Control of Underwater Vehicle-Manipulator Systems in Confined Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08871)
*Mohamed Abdelwahab,Ruggero Carli,Damiano Varagnolo,Alberto Dalla Libera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MANTA，一个用于水下车辆-机械臂系统（UVMS）在受限、杂乱且部分已知环境中自主干预的三层分层规划与控制框架。该框架结合通道可达性、操纵可行性和闭环执行，并支持基于地图更新的恢复与路径修复机制。实验表明其在规划成功率和跟踪精度上均优于传统基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受限、杂乱且部分已知的水下环境中，UVMS面临机动性差、通道狭窄和不确定执行等问题，可能导致机器人进入不可恢复区域。现有方法缺乏同时考虑可达性、操纵可行性与闭环执行的系统性框架，因此需要更结构化且数据高效的安全自主干预方案。

Method: MANTA采用三层分层规划与控制框架：第一层在保守的降维基座空间进行全局连通性推理，提取通向任务区域的可通行走廊候选；第二层对每个候选走廊联合优化连续基座运动和机械臂轨迹，生成无碰撞基座-机械臂联合轨迹；第三层利用基于高斯过程的模型强化学习（MC-PILCO）学习到达-保持基座策略，实现轨迹跟踪和操作状态的保持。执行过程中通过地图更新监测触发恢复与路径修复。

Result: 在120个匹配的规划查询中，MANTA相比全状态采样基线实现了更高的任务成功率，同时具有更大的安全间隙余量和更低的机械臂运动量。MC-PILCO学习策略在训练和未见过的管状参考轨迹上均显著降低了位置和偏航跟踪误差。

Conclusion: MANTA是一个结构化且数据高效的安全自主水下干预框架，适用于洞穴、管道和杂乱的次海结构环境，有效提升了UVMS在受限环境下的任务成功率与执行安全性。

Abstract: This paper addresses autonomous intervention with an underwater vehicle--manipulator system (UVMS) in confined, cluttered, and partially known environments, where poor maneuverability, narrow passages, and uncertain execution may cause the robot to enter unrecoverable regions. We propose MANTA, a three-layer hierarchical planning-and-control framework that couples passage accessibility, manipulation feasibility, and closed-loop execution. The first layer performs global connectivity reasoning in a conservative reduced base space to extract traversable corridor candidates toward the task region. The second layer refines each candidate corridor by jointly optimizing the continuous base motion and arm trajectory, producing a collision-free base--arm trajectory. The third layer learns a reach-and-hold base policy using Gaussian-process model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) through MC-PILCO, enabling trajectory tracking and station keeping at the planned manipulation state. During execution, the framework monitors map updates and can trigger recovery and route repair when the active passage becomes infeasible. MANTA is evaluated in confined UVMS planning and closed-loop tracking experiments. Across 120 matched planning queries, it achieves higher task success than full-state sampling-based baselines while producing larger clearance margins and lower arm motion. The learned MC-PILCO policy further reduces position and yaw tracking errors on both training and unseen tube-like references. These results show MANTA as a structured and data-efficient framework for safe autonomous underwater intervention in caves, tubes, and cluttered subsea structures.

</details>


### [310] [SHRIMP: Iterative Refinement of Robot Task Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08884)
*Mya Schroder,Yuna Hwang,Callie Y. Kim,Leqian Cheng,Jeffrey Li-cheng Liu,Chenchen Zheng,Xinning He,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SHRIMP是一个利用自然语言自动生成机器人层次化原始任务计划（hierarchical robot primitive plan）的系统，用户可以通过重新提示和显式修正来迭代修订计划，并在模拟环境中验证后再在实体机器人上执行。用户研究（n=35）表明SHRIMP提升了用户的感知控制感和机器人透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作机器人进入制造、农业、医疗等领域，但传统编程需要机器人专业知识，普通终端用户缺乏这些技能。虽然大语言模型（LLM）可以实现自然语言到任务计划的转换，但语言指令存在语义歧义，且生成模型的转换过程缺乏透明性，用户难以在执行前验证计划正确性。

Method: 提出SHRIMP系统，通过自然语言自动生成层次化机器人原始任务计划；支持用户通过重新提示（re-prompting）和显式修正（explicit correction）迭代修订计划；每次修订后用户可在仿真环境中验证，满意后再在实体机器人上执行。

Result: 通过包含35名参与者的用户研究（涉及桌面厨房任务规划），验证了SHRIMP能够提升用户的感知控制感和机器人透明度。

Conclusion: SHRIMP通过自然语言生成与迭代修订相结合的机制，加上仿真验证的环节，有效解决了语言指令歧义和生成过程不透明的问题，提高了用户对机器人任务规划的掌控感和系统的可理解性。系统视频和源代码已公开。

Abstract: As collaborative robots have entered domains such as manufacturing, agriculture, and healthcare, programming or adapting robot behavior typically requires robotic expertise that most end users lack. Natural language lowers this barrier. Recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) have made it feasible to translate natural language into robot task plans. However, language-based task specification suffers from semantic ambiguity, and generative models lack transparency for how language instructions become robot actions, making it difficult for users to validate the plan before execution. To address these issues, we introduce SHRIMP, a system that allows users to automatically generate a hierarchical robot primitive plan using natural language and iteratively revise their plan through re-prompting and explicit correction. At each revision, SHRIMP allows users to validate their plan in simulation, and once satisfied, execute it on the physical robot. Through a user study involving participants planning tabletop kitchen tasks (n=35), we validate that SHRIMP improves perceived control and enhances robot transparency. System videos and source code are available at https://wisc-hci.github.io/SHRIMP.

</details>


### [311] [Estimation of Spacecraft Inertia Tensor Using Attitude-Only Data from Torque-Free Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08886)
*Daigo Kobayashi,Vakhtang Putkaradze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅基于姿态数据的航天器归一化惯性张量估计框架，无需陀螺仪或已知控制力矩，支持单段长弧和多段短弧联合估计。通过KKT线性初始化与非线性打靶法精修，在精度和计算效率上均显著优于EKF基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统航天器惯性参数估计通常依赖陀螺仪测量或已知控制力矩，在无陀螺或缺乏控制信息场景下难以适用。本文旨在开发一种仅利用姿态观测的轻量级估计方法，同时兼顾精度与计算效率，并支持多段短弧联合估计以适应实际观测约束。

Method: 提出一种纯姿态框架，采用KKT（Karush-Kuhn-Tucker）条件构造线性初始化方案，快速获得初始估计；随后利用欧拉方程的精确Jacobi椭圆解和Magnus展开四元数映射进行非线性打靶法精修，实现高精度惯性张量估计。

Result: 控制姿态噪声下，单段500秒弧段估计比同初值EKF精度提升约一个数量级，计算量降低近两个数量级；三段100秒弧段联合估计达到相似精度提升且速度快一个数量级以上。光写实近距操作仿真中，2000秒单弧段达到亚千分位中位数惯性张量误差，支持10小时姿态预测（单位数度中位数误差）；三段弧段（30-300秒/段）始终优于EKF精修。

Conclusion: 该方法在无需陀螺仪和控制力矩的情况下，实现了高效高精度的惯性张量估计，并通过数值与仿真充分验证了其在多种观测策略下的鲁棒性和优势，实际精度受旋转激励程度和时间采样条件制约。

Abstract: We present an attitude-only framework for estimating a spacecraft's normalized inertia tensor from torque-free rotational motion. Our method supports both continuous single-arc observations and the joint use of multiple short torque-free arcs, while requiring neither gyroscope measurements nor known control torques. A Karush-Kuhn-Tucker formulation provides a fast linear initialization, which is refined by nonlinear shooting using the exact Jacobi-elliptic solution of Euler's equations and a Magnus-expansion quaternion map. Under controlled attitude noise, tests using a single 500-second arc reduced inertia-tensor error by approximately one order of magnitude relative to an Extended Kalman Filter initialized from the same estimate, while requiring nearly two orders of magnitude less computation. Joint estimation from three 100-second arcs provided a similar improvement in accuracy and remained more than one order of magnitude faster. Photorealistic proximity-operations simulations further evaluated both strategies using monocular image-derived attitudes. The 2000-second single-arc cases achieved sub-thousandth median inertia-tensor error and supported 10-hour attitude predictions with single-digit-degree median error. In three-arc cases using 30-300 seconds per arc, our method consistently outperformed the EKF refinement, with performance governed by rotational excitation and temporal sampling.

</details>


### [312] [From Operational Design Domain to Action: A Systematic Behavioral Taxonomy for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08941)
*Chaitanya Shinde,Hadi Hajieghrary,Miguel Hurtado*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于标准的行为能力分类体系，包含21项行为能力，覆盖高速公路、城市和枢纽三个运营域，并基于PEGASUS六层模型ODD推导，通过四属性框架（安全、合规、舒适、效率）对行为进行分解，用于自动驾驶系统的行为验证和SOTIF覆盖证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ODD规范只描述了自动驾驶系统（ADS）被允许运行的条件范围，但未规定系统在该域内必须展示的行为能力，导致运营条件规范与行为验证之间存在关键空白，这是ADS安全保证中尚未解决的挑战。

Method: 基于PEGASUS六层模型ODD，系统性地构建了21项行为能力的结构化分类体系，覆盖三个运营域（高速公路、城市、枢纽）；每项行为沿纵向和横向控制轴进行分解，并用四属性框架（安全、合规、舒适、效率）进行特征化；将ODD层参数化与行为能力规范交叉，生成可用于系统化行为测试的场景族。

Result: 该分类体系基于AVSC00008202111、SAE J3237和SAE J3016标准，并通过在规则强制轨迹优化系统中的部署验证了其作为操作规范层的有效性；同时识别出枢纽域在结构上具有独特性且规范不足，值得专门研究。

Conclusion: 该行为能力分类体系弥合了ODD规范与行为验证之间的鸿沟，为ADS安全保证提供了可操作的规范层，并指出枢纽域是需要更多研究关注的领域。

Abstract: Operational Design Domain (ODD) specifications describe where an automated driving system (ADS) is permitted to operate, but they do not prescribe what the ADS must demonstrably do once deployed within that domain. This gap between operating condition specification and behavioral validation represents a critical unresolved challenge in ADS safety assurance. This paper presents a structured, standards-grounded taxonomy of 21 behavioral competencies organized across three operational domains-Highway (HWY), Urban (URB), and Hub (HUB)-derived systematically from the PEGASUS six-layer model-based ODD. Each behavior is decomposed along longitudinal and lateral control axes and characterized against a four-property framework: Safety (gap maintenance, conflict avoidance, kinematic stability), Compliance (legal rules and behavioral norms), Comfort (rider dynamics and trust), and Efficiency (mission completion and product-level metrics). We further demonstrate that the crossing of ODD layer parameterizations with behavioral competency specifications yields concrete scenario families suitable for systematic behavioral testing and SOTIF coverage evidence. The taxonomy is grounded in AVSC00008202111, SAE J3237, and SAE J3016, and is validated as an operational specification layer through its deployment in a rule-enforced trajectory optimization system. The Hub domain is identified as a structurally distinct, underspecified domain warranting dedicated research attention.

</details>


### [313] [Personalized Lower-limb Exoskeleton Assistance via Preference-based Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09015)
*Xiao-Yin Liu,Guotao Li,Weiqun Wang,Zeng-Guang Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于偏好的贝叶斯优化（PbBO）框架，用于个性化外骨骼助力的参数优化，通过利用候选集采样分布知识提升样本效率，仅需20次迭代即可收敛到用户偏好参数（验证准确率90.7%）。同时设计了分层控制器实现不同任务的个性化扭矩生成和实时交互扭矩跟踪，实验表明优化参数相比无助行可将代谢率降低14.5%-15.4%、心率降低6.3%-7.6%、肌肉激活降低6.7%-31.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外骨骼参数优化中，基于用户偏好的方法严重依赖大量人机在线交互，优化速度慢，不仅导致用户疲劳，还影响优化效果。因此需要一种高效、少交互的偏好优化框架。

Method: 提出基于偏好的贝叶斯优化（PbBO）方法，通过利用候选集采样分布的知识提升样本效率；同时设计分层控制器，为不同任务生成个性化扭矩并实现实时交互扭矩跟踪。

Result: PbBO在优化六个控制参数时，经20次迭代达到90.7%验证准确率。跑台和户外实验显示，优化参数相比无助行可降低代谢率14.5%-15.4%、心率6.3%-7.6%、肌肉激活6.7%-31.5%。

Conclusion: PbBO框架能够以最少交互高效学习个性化外骨骼助力参数，同时分层控制器保证了不同任务下的实时扭矩跟踪，综合实验验证了该方案在提升能量效率和用户体验方面的有效性。

Abstract: A significant challenge in exoskeleton robotics is the need to dynamically adapt control profiles to individual motion preferences, thereby ensuring both efficient and comfortable assistance. Currently, since user experience can serve as a comprehensive metric for evaluating the effectiveness of assistance, user preference-based optimization methods have been widely studied for parameter tuning. However, the existing methods rely heavily on extensive human-robot online interactions and suffer from slow optimization speed, which not only induces user fatigue but also compromises optimization effectiveness. Therefore, this paper aims to explore an efficient preference-based optimization framework for personalized exoskeleton assistance that can learn optimal parameters with minimal interaction. We propose a preference-based Bayesian optimization (PbBO) approach that can improve sample efficiency by leveraging knowledge about the sampling distribution of candidate sets. For optimizing six control parameters, PbBO can converge to user-preferred parameters with 90.7% validation accuracy via 20 iterations. Moreover, the hierarchical controller is designed to generate personalized torque for different tasks and achieve interaction torque tracking in real time. The results of treadmill and outdoor experiments demonstrate that the optimized parameters can reduce metabolic rate by 14.5%-15.4%, heart rate by 6.3%-7.6%, and muscle activation by 6.7%-31.5% compared to unassisted walking.

</details>


### [314] [Latent World Models with Monotone Planning Costs for Image-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09073)
*Amirhosein Chahe,Siwei Cai,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于冻结DINO编码器的潜世界模型，通过自回归展开损失和单调成本排序损失来改进图像目标导航中的规划成本排序问题，在GNM数据集上达到最先进性能，并成功在实体机器人上零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像目标导航中的潜世界模型不仅需要准确的未来预测，还需要可靠的规划成本来排序候选动作序列。研究发现，简单的余弦距离成本排序不佳时会导致CEM等采样式规划器误入歧途，因此需要改进成本排序的可靠性和规划性能。

Method: 1) 基于冻结的DINO系列编码器构建潜世界模型；2) 提出两种互补的训练目标：自回归展开损失缩小训练与多步规划展开之间的差距，单调成本排序损失直接鼓励扰动程度更大的动作序列获得更高的规划成本；3) 研究了基于InfoNCE的动作对比训练，发现时间排列负样本会扭曲潜几何结构并降低规划性能。

Result: 在GNM导航数据集上超越NWM、DINO-WM、OmniVLA和NoMaD等方法，达到最先进的图像目标导航性能；与相同编码器的DINO-WM基线相比，方向误差降低了2.7倍；在物理机器人上零样本部署成功，在未见过的室内和室外环境中能沿着目标导向路径导航。

Conclusion: 通过设计专门的成本排序训练目标，可以有效解决潜世界模型中规划成本排序不可靠的问题，从而显著提升图像目标导航性能，并且零样本迁移到真实机器人上具备泛化能力。

Abstract: Image-goal navigation with latent world models requires not only accurate future prediction, but also a planning cost that reliably ranks candidate action sequences. We define the cost as the cosine distance between the predicted future embedding and the goal embedding, and show that poor cost ordering can mislead sampling-based planners such as Cross-Entropy Method (CEM). To address this, we propose a latent world model built on a frozen DINO-family encoder and train it with two complementary objectives. An autoregressive rollout loss reduces the gap between training and multi-step planning rollouts, while a Monotone Cost Ranking (MCR) loss directly encourages increasingly perturbed action sequences to receive higher planning costs. We also study InfoNCE-based action-contrastive training and find that temporal permutation negatives distort the latent geometry and degrade planning performance. On the GNM navigation dataset, our method outperforms Navigation World Models (NWM), DINO-WM, OmniVLA, and NoMaD, achieving state-of-the-art image-goal navigation performance while reducing orientation error by $2.7\times$ over the same-encoder DINO WM baseline. We also deploy the model zero-shot on a physical robot, where it follows goal-directed paths in unseen indoor and outdoor environments.

</details>


### [315] [UnsDrive: Towards Robust End-to-End Autonomous Driving in Unstructured Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09098)
*Nanxin Zeng,Ruiqi Song,Xiangyu Guo,Baiyong Ding,Yunfeng Ai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出UnsDrive，一种针对非结构化矿区环境的端到端自动驾驶规划器，通过未知感知占用表示和流匹配规划器来处理弱道路结构、地形遮挡和能见度低等挑战，并配套提出MineLoop矿区仿真器进行闭环评估，实验表明其在轨迹精度、避碰和长时程鲁棒性方面均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端规划方法主要针对结构化城市道路设计，在非结构化矿区环境中泛化能力差。矿区道路结构弱、地形遮挡严重、能见度低、存在大量未观测区域，这些特性使安全规划极具挑战性。

Method: 1) 构建未知感知占用表示，利用多帧可见性线索显式建模占用、自由和未知空间；2) 将流匹配规划器基于该表示生成多模态未来轨迹；3) 提出占用-轨迹一致性损失和不确定性感知轨迹评分器，惩罚进入不可通行或未观测区域的轨迹；4) 提出MineLoop矿区闭环仿真器用于评估。

Result: 在开环和闭环实验中，UnsDrive在轨迹精度、碰撞避免和长时程驾驶鲁棒性方面均一致优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 对未知空间的显式推理对于非结构化矿区环境中的自动驾驶具有重要意义，验证了所提方法在该场景下的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: End-to-end planning has shown strong promise for autonomous driving, but most existing methods are designed for structured urban roads and generalize poorly to unstructured mining environments. In such settings, weak road structure, terrain-induced occlusions, degraded visibility, and large unobserved regions make safe planning particularly challenging. To address these challenges, we propose UnsDrive, an end-to-end planner designed for unstructured mining scenes. UnsDrive builds an unknown-aware occupancy representation that explicitly models occupied, free, and unknown space using multi-frame visibility cues, and conditions a flow-matching planner on this representation to generate multimodal future trajectories. To improve safety under partial observability, we further introduce an occupancy trajectory consistency loss and an uncertainty-aware trajectory scorer that penalize trajectories entering non-traversable or unobserved regions. We also present MineLoop, a mining-oriented closed-loop simulator for evaluating autonomous driving under irregular road geometry, degraded visibility, heavy-vehicle interactions, and mining-specific operational constraints. Experiments in both open-loop and closed-loop settings show that UnsDrive consistently outperforms strong baselines in trajectory accuracy, collision avoidance, and long-horizon driving robustness. These results demonstrate the value of explicit unknown-space reasoning for autonomous driving in unstructured mining environments.

</details>


### [316] [ROEVO: Robust Organized Edge Feature-based Visual Odometry Using RGB-D Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09112)
*Mingrui Liu,Xingxing Zuo,Renlang Huang,Minglei Zhao,Jiming Chen,Liang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图像边缘特征的视觉里程计（VO）系统，通过新颖的"organized edges"特征表示，将离散边缘像素转化为序列化簇，实现了边缘级别的跨帧关联、共视图表构建、边级残差跟踪以及形状保持的边缘拟合与捆绑调整（BA），在室内环境中达到了与先进方法相当或更优的精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘特征在各类环境中普遍存在，蕴含丰富的纹理和结构信息，但现有基于边缘的VO方法未能充分利用这一潜力，需要一种更有效的边缘特征表示和利用方式。

Method: 提出"organized edges"特征表示，将分散边缘像素转化为序列化簇；基于此构建边缘级跨帧关联和共视图形；提出边级残差跟踪方法、形状保持的边缘拟合方法以及基于organized edges的BA方法（将传统BA分解为拟合与配准两步），构建完整VO系统。

Result: 在室内环境中进行了大量实验，精度和鲁棒性优于或达到与最先进方法相当的水平，代码已公开。

Conclusion: 通过organized edges特征表示及其配套的跟踪和优化方法，实现了高效精确的纯边缘特征VO系统，为边缘特征在SLAM领域的应用提供了新的思路。

Abstract: This work presents a visual odometry (VO) system that leverages image edge features. Edges are spatially expressive cues commonly present across diverse environments, offering rich textural and structural information. However, existing edge-based VO methods often fail to fully exploit this potential. To this end, we introduce a novel feature representation termed \textit{organized edges}, which transforms disjoint edge pixels into sequentialized clusters, enabling more effective retention and utilization of the underlying textural and structural information. Another nice property of this formulation is that organized edges can perform edge-level association across multiple frames, enabling the establishment of a co-visibility graph. To achieve precise and efficient pose estimation, we propose a range of particularly designed tracking and joint optimization methods based on the characteristics of organized edges. For tracking, we formulate edge-wise rather than pixel-wise residuals to achieve robust and accurate inter-frame registration. For joint optimization, we introduce a novel shape-preserving edge-fitting method and an organized edge-based Bundle Adjustment (BA) approach, which decomposes the traditional BA problem into fitting and registration to preserve the structural integrity. Based on these novel techniques, we develop a complete VO system that exclusively employs organized edge features, achieving efficient tracking and precise local mapping. Extensive experiments demonstrate its accuracy and robustness in indoor environments, outperforming or achieving comparable performance to state-of-the-art methods. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/liumingrui814/ROEVO

</details>


### [317] [Trajectory Divergence Horizon Decision for Reliable Dual-Arm Surgical Subtask Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09125)
*Mingwu Su,Guankun Wang,Jinsong Lin,Rulin Zhou,Ziyi Hao,Zhiwei Fang,Huxin Gao,Jiewen Lai,Jiazheng Wang,Fan Zhang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Trajectory Divergence Horizon Decision (TDHD)，一种测试时自适应执行决策机制，通过测量流匹配生成轨迹在小噪声扰动下的差异来估计动作可靠性，并采用双阈值规则触发重规划，解决手术VLA策略中固定长度开环动作序列导致的累积误差问题。在真实达芬奇式双臂平台上验证，手术操作成功率从55%提升至60%（针）和80%（组织）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人临床负荷增加，需要自主化解决方案。现有VLA模型虽提供统一感知-语言-动作框架，但依赖固定长度开环动作序列，场景变化时易产生累积误差和手术风险，缺乏自适应执行控制。

Method: 将手术VLA部署建模为自适应执行时域决策问题，提出TDHD机制：通过测量流匹配生成的两条轨迹在微小噪声扰动下的发散程度来估计逐步动作可靠性，并利用双阈值规则截断执行以触发及时重规划。同时构建了带多视角感知和语言指令的真实da Vinci样机双臂基准，收集600次遥操作示范。

Result: 在真实硬件上每任务20次试验中，TDHD优于最新VLA基线：针操作成功率从55%提升至60%，组织操作成功率从55%提升至80%，且最终操作阶段的改进最为显著。

Conclusion: 自适应执行控制对VLA模型在手术机器人操作中的可靠部署至关重要，TDHD通过动态调整执行时域有效提升了手术操作的鲁棒性和成功率。

Abstract: Surgical robotic systems are increasingly being adopted as clinical workload rises, motivating autonomous solutions for repetitive manipulation subtasks. Learning-based controllers improve generalization compared with rule-based and analytic approaches, but most are trained for individual tasks and remain difficult to reuse across procedures. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide a unified framework that integrates visual perception, language grounding, and action generation, offering a promising path toward more composable surgical autonomy. However, existing VLA policies rely on fixed-length open-loop action sequences, where changing scene conditions can lead to accumulated errors and potential risks in surgical manipulation. To mitigate this issue, we formulate surgical VLA deployment as an adaptive execution-horizon decision problem and propose Trajectory Divergence Horizon Decision (TDHD), a test-time mechanism that estimates step-wise action reliability by measuring the divergence between two flow-matching-generated trajectories under small noise perturbations and truncates execution using a dual-threshold rule to trigger timely replanning. We further establish a real-world da Vinci-like dual-arm benchmark with synchronized multi-view perception and language instructions, and collect 600 teleoperated demonstrations across needle (reach, pick, regrasp) and tissue (reach, lift, resection) manipulation suites. On real hardware with 20 trials per task setting, TDHD consistently improves performance over the latest VLA baselines: success increases from 55\% to 60\% for needle manipulation and from 55\% to 80\% for tissue manipulation, with the largest gains observed in the final manipulation stages. These results highlight the importance of adaptive execution control for reliable deployment of VLA models in surgical robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [318] [High Fidelity Capture, Reconstruction, and Transfer of Human Demonstrations for Robot-Assisted Bathing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09127)
*Arjun S. Lakshmipathy,Jonathan P. King,Ethan Zuo,Rohit Satishkumar,Hongyi Chen,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Dan Ding,Zackory Erickson,Nancy S. Pollard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以接触区域为核心处理原语的框架，用于收集、理解并迁移人类洗浴示范数据，并构建了首个提供同步运动、形状、接触和力信息的高质量数据集，成功实现了软体手在假人上执行洗浴任务的开放和闭环控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高价值的临床任务（如洗浴）对机器人的安全性和可靠性要求极高，但人类洗浴示范数据的收集、理解和有效迁移非常困难，即使使用现代运动和触觉传感设备也是如此，这阻碍了相关机器人系统的发展。

Method: 提出一个以接触区域为核心处理原语的框架，利用该框架构建由训练有素的临床医生对人类受试者进行洗浴示范的数据集，并利用该数据集设计并控制安装在机械臂上的灵巧软体手，在假人上执行洗浴任务，采用开放和闭环两种控制策略。

Result: 构建了首个在持续性、接触丰富的人-人交互中提供高质量同步运动、形状、接触和力信息的数据集；迁移策略在机器人堆栈的多个层面展示了这些数据的有效利用，并在假人上成功实现了洗浴任务。

Conclusion: 该框架和数据集为物理人机交互（pHRI）研究提供了重要的基础资源，所有相关材料将公开发布，以推动该领域的进一步进展。

Abstract: Despite the demand for robots in high-value clinical tasks like bathing, contemporary systems still lack the safety and reliability required for complex, sustained physical interaction with humans. A key challenge hindering the development of such systems is that collecting, understanding, and effectively transferring highly dynamic, contact-rich human bathing demonstrations is difficult, even with modern motion and tactile sensing equipment. We present a straightforward, but effective framework for doing so with high fidelity by utilizing contact regions as a key processing primitive. We use our framework to build a dataset of bathing demonstrations performed by trained clinicians on human subjects. We then use this dataset to design and control an arm-mounted dexterous soft hand to perform bathing tasks on a mannequin using open- and closed-loop strategies. Our dataset is the first to provide high quality synchronized motion, shape, contact, and force during sustained, contact-rich human-human interaction, and our transfer strategies demonstrate effective use of these data across multiple levels of the robotics stack. All relevant materials will be publicly released to enable further advancements in physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) research.

</details>


### [319] [SpeedTuning: Speeding Up Policy Execution with Lightweight Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09138)
*David D. Yuan,Tony Z. Zhao,Kaylee Burns,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SpeedTuning，一种强化学习框架，用于提升机器人操作策略的自主速度，无需额外数据采集，实验展示超过2.4倍速度提升且保持良好成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型机器人操作策略在实际部署中常受限于执行速度慢的问题。模仿学习策略受到硬件限制和数据采集时操作员速度的制约，且缺乏加速模仿学习策略的成熟方法，执行速度与任务成功之间的经验关系也未被充分探讨。

Method: SpeedTuning框架利用强化学习学习预测动作的最优执行速度，与基础策略互补，无需额外数据采集。相比简单的固定速度线性插值方法，该方法能够自适应地调整执行速度。

Result: SpeedTuning实现了超过2.4倍的执行速度提升，同时保持了与原始任务策略相当的成功率。在倒水、抛掷、拾取等多种动态和精细任务中验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SpeedTuning为加速机器人操作策略提供了一种有效的强化学习方案，在显著提升执行速度的同时维持任务成功率，展示了在真实机器人操作中增强部署性能的潜力。

Abstract: While learned robotic policies hold promise for advancing generalizable manipulation, their practical deployment is often hindered by suboptimal execution speeds. Imitation learning policies are inherently limited by hardware constraints and the speed of the operator during data collection. In addition, there are no established methods for accelerating policies learned via imitation, and the empirical relationship between execution speed and task success remains underexplored. To address these issues, we introduce SpeedTuning, a reinforcement learning framework specifically designed to enhance the speed of manipulation policies. SpeedTuning learns to predict the optimal execution speed for actions, thereby complementing a base policy without necessitating additional data collection. We provide empirical evidence that SpeedTuning achieves substantial improvements in execution speed, exceeding 2.4x speed-up, while preserving an adequate success rate compared to both the original task policy and straightforward speed-up methods such as linear interpolation at a fixed speed. We evaluate our approach across a diverse set of dynamic and precise tasks, including pouring, throwing, and picking, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness in enhancing real-world robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [320] [Particle-Based Conformal Prediction for Contact-Aware Uncertainty Calibration in Stratified Configuration Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09166)
*Luís Marques,Kristian Popov,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CaPTURe，一种基于几何感知的共形预测算法，利用任意保真度的粒子模型为未知未来系统状态生成概率有效的不确定区域，在接触丰富和接触无关的运动场景中都能满足用户设定的覆盖概率要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统在复杂环境中部署时需要可靠的不确定性表示，特别是当机器人与障碍物接触时，其未来配置分布可能变得多模态、不连续或位于低维流形上。分析性或学习型运动模型常因数据不足、简化假设等原因不准确，导致执行不安全或任务失败。

Method: CaPTURe使用校准数据集对运动不确定性估计进行局部校准，构建保证以用户设定概率包含未来机器人配置的预测区域。该方法具有几何感知特性，采用共形预测框架，并利用任意保真度的粒子模型来表示系统状态分布，同时有效处理接触丰富与接触无关运动中不确定性变化的差异。

Result: 在两个模拟规划任务（迷宫弹珠控制和机械臂紧公差插销操作）中，CaPTURe在接触和非接触场景下均满足用户指定的覆盖要求，相比最优基线实现了高达30个百分点的任务成功率绝对提升。

Conclusion: CaPTURe通过局部校准的方法有效解决了接触场景下不确定性表示不准确的问题，在保证安全性和任务效率方面优于现有基线方法，为自主系统在接触丰富环境中的可靠部署提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty representation is essential for deploying autonomous systems that interact with their environment, as robots must reason about how uncertainty arising from both stochasticity and model mismatch is impacted by contacts with obstacles (e.g., when navigating through a cluttered environment or inserting a part into an assembly). We propose Calibrated Particle-sets for Trans-dimensional Uncertainty Representation (CaPTURe), a geometry-aware, conformal prediction-based algorithm that generates probabilistically valid prediction regions of the unknown future system configuration using particle-based models of arbitrary fidelity. While calibrated uncertainty predictions are essential for safe and efficient planning, analytical or learned motion models are often inaccurate - due to limited data, simplifying assumptions, unmodeled effects, etc. - which can lead to unsafe executions or task failure. Additionally, when a robot contacts an obstacle, the distribution of its future configurations can become multimodal or disjoint, or lie along manifolds of lower intrinsic dimension than the space of possible robot configurations. Our method uses a calibration dataset of system transitions to locally calibrate motion uncertainty estimates, constructing regions guaranteed to contain the future robot configuration at a user-set probability. Our calibration procedure captures how motion uncertainty varies between contact-rich and contactless motions, leading to sufficient coverage in both cases. We evaluate our method on two simulated planning tasks: controlling a marble around a labyrinth and performing tight-tolerance peg-in-hole insertion with a manipulator. Compared to relevant baselines, CaPTURe achieves the user-specified coverage requirement both in and out of contact and achieves up to a 30% absolute improvement in task success rate over the best baseline.

</details>


### [321] [SAIN: Structure-Aware Interactive Navigation with Active Dialogue Grounding for Mobile Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09196)
*Yuhao Cao,Xiao Liu,Yang Xie,Lu Liu,Haoyao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAIN是一个零样本框架，将主动对话转化为持久的导航状态（目标证据、走廊记忆、物体候选标签），存储在结构化记忆中，并通过统一策略实现前沿排序和目标接近。在VL-LN IIGN基准上，SR从20.2提升至25.4，SPL从13.07提升至14.17，无需任务特定的策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的机器人常遇到模糊、不完整或欠明确的人类指令，现有交互式实例目标导航方法将oracle答案仅作为瞬时文本上下文，未形成持久的结构化空间或目标中心状态，限制了长时程导航的性能。

Method: SAIN将对话答案编译为目标证据、路径级走廊记忆和物体候选标签，存储到值、房间、图和物体四类结构化记忆中，再由统一策略进行前沿排序和最终目标接近。

Result: 在VL-LN IIGN基准上，SAIN的SR从20.2提升至25.4，SPL从13.07提升至14.17，超越了最强的已报告对话式基线，且无需任何任务特定策略训练。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对话到状态的转换是长时程交互式实例导航中有效的零样本机制，为处理模糊指令的具身智能体提供了新思路。

Abstract: Most existing vision-language navigation tasks assume that instructions are complete and unambiguous. However, real-world robots often encounter natural human instructions that are ambiguous, underspecified, or incomplete, requiring them to resolve such uncertainties through active questioning. Interactive Instance Goal Navigation (IIGN) requires an embodied agent to find the specific instance under an ambiguous category-level instruction through active dialogue. However, existing dialogue-enabled methods often consume oracle answers as transient textual context for immediate decisions, rather than persistent spatial or object-centric structured state. We present SAIN, a zero-shot framework that turns active dialogue into persistent navigation state. Instead of consuming oracle answers as one-step text hints, SAIN compiles them into target evidence, route-level corridor memory, and object-candidate labels. These states are stored in structured value, room, graph, and object memories, then consumed by a unified policy for frontier ranking and final target approach. On the VL-LN IIGN benchmark, SAIN improves SR from 20.2 to 25.4 and SPL from 13.07 to 14.17 over the strongest reported dialogue-enabled baseline, while requiring no task-specific policy training. The results support dialogue-to-state conversion as an effective zero-shot mechanism for long-horizon interactive instance navigation. Project website: https://zorattc.github.io/SAIN/

</details>


### [322] [Ultra-Low-Impedance Robotic Gripper for High-Bandwidth and Transparent Physical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09198)
*Joon Lee,Ari Choi,Seokhwan Jeong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种9自由度三指差动直驱（DDD）夹爪，通过直驱电机与低传动比（1:2）差动机构相结合，实现了约18N的标称抓取力和4.7N的指尖力，同时显著降低了系统惯性和被动机械阻抗，为高带宽交互和无传感器力估计提供了硬件基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人夹爪依赖高传动比减速器产生抓取力矩和外部力传感器测量物理交互，但高传动比会引入摩擦、反射惯量和机械阻抗，外部传感器又增加硬件复杂度，这两者之间存在固有矛盾，需要新的设计方案来平衡扭矩、透明度和灵巧性。

Method: 设计了一种结合直驱（DD）电机与低传动比（1:2）差动传动机构的三指9自由度夹爪。该机构将执行器质量集中在基座以最小化移动连杆惯量，差动架构将两个电机并联耦合以在屈曲时放大扭矩。

Result: 原型实验结果表明：标称抓取力约为18N，指尖力为4.7N，电机对系统惯量的贡献率仅为0.236%，无动力状态下最大被动机械阻抗为50.1N/m。

Conclusion: 该硬件方案有效解决了扭矩、物理透明度和运动灵巧性之间的权衡问题，为高带宽交互和无传感器本体感应式力估计提供了基础。

Abstract: Conventional robotic grippers often use high-ratio transmissions to generate grasping torque and external force sensors to measure physical interaction. High-ratio transmissions increase friction, reflected inertia, and mechanical impedance, while external sensors add hardware complexity. To address these trade-offs, this study proposes a novel 9-DOF, three-fingered Differential Direct-Drive (DDD) gripper that combines DD motors with a low-ratio (1:2) differential transmission. The mechanism centralizes actuator mass at the base to minimize moving-link inertia, while the differential architecture couples two motors in parallel to amplify torque during flexion. Experiments show that the prototype delivers a nominal grasping force of approximately 18 N and a fingertip force of 4.7 N, while maintaining a low motor contribution to system inertia (0.236%) and low passive mechanical impedance, with a maximum measured value of 50.1 N/m when the motors are unpowered. The proposed hardware addresses the trade-offs among torque, physical transparency, and kinematic dexterity, providing a foundation for high-bandwidth interaction and sensorless proprioceptive force estimation.

</details>


### [323] [Task-Oriented Formation Decision via Reinforcement Learning: Herding an Attacking Swarm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09258)
*Zhaozong Wang,Guibin Sun,Jinyong Chen,Rui Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究多机器人系统中的任务导向编队决策问题，聚焦放牧任务。通过低维参数向量表示编队形状，将编队决策转化为参数优化问题，并利用强化学习训练策略以在线调节编队参数，应对攻击者机动性和策略不确定性。仿真和物理实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注多机器人形状编队本身，而缺乏针对具体任务（如放牧）的编队决策研究。该任务面临攻击者机动性更强且策略未知的挑战，需要一种灵活且能自适应任务需求的编队决策方法。

Method: 首先，用低维参数向量隐式编码编队形状，将编队决策转化为参数优化问题，提升预定义形状的灵活性。其次，设计基于强化学习的策略来在线调节编队参数；该策略在涵盖多种攻击策略的仿真环境中离线训练，以应对在线部署中的对抗不确定性。

Result: 与三个基线方法的对比仿真表明该方法能成功完成具有挑战性的放牧任务；可扩展性仿真验证了其对数十个机器人场景的适用性；物理平台上（3个攻击者、7个防御者）的实验进一步证明了方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过参数化编队形状与强化学习相结合，有效缓解了防御者的机动性劣势，并能在在线部署中应对攻击者的未知策略，在仿真与物理实验中均验证了其有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems can accomplish tasks that are difficult for a single robot by organizing into task-specific formations. Different from existing studies on multi-robot shape formation, we here study the task-oriented formation decision problem, with a focus on the herding task. This task is challenging due to the attackers' superior maneuverability and their unknown strategies. To address these challenges, we propose the following novel results. First, we encode the formation shape using a low-dimensional parameter vector. This parametric representation reformulates the formation decision as a parameter optimization problem, thereby resolving the limited flexibility of predefined shapes. By optimizing these formation parameters, the defenders' maneuverability disadvantage is mitigated through a formation shape that continuously adapts to task requirements. Second, we develop a reinforcement learning-based policy to regulate the formation parameters. Trained offline in simulations covering diverse attacking strategies, the learned policy can effectively handle adversarial unpredictability during online deployment. Comparative simulations against three baselines demonstrate that our method can successfully accomplish challenging herding tasks. Additional scalability simulations further verify its applicability to simulated scenarios involving dozens of robots. We also validate the practical feasibility of our method on a physical robotic platform with 3 attackers and 7 defenders.

</details>


### [324] [WorldSimProbe: Diagnosing Simulator Faithfulness in Action-Conditioned World Models for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09298)
*Peterson Co,Sicheng Hu,Chunxuan Jiao,Hongyang Cheng,Yulin Luo,Yijie Xu,Sixiang Chen,Zhongxia Zhao,Zihao Wang,DaFeng Chi,Peidong Liu,YuTong Chen,Henghua Liu,Zhihao Yuan,Huizhu Jia,Yuzheng Zhuang,Tianle Zhang,Liang Lin,Huajie Tan,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于能力评估的框架（Observable Simulator Contract 和 WorldSimProbe），用于系统化地诊断动作条件世界模型（ACWM）作为物理模拟器的保真度，超越了传统的视觉质量或任务结果评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ACWM评估主要关注视觉质量、任务结果或粗粒度的rollout响应性，未能直接测试模拟器的保真度，即动作条件转换是否精确，而非仅仅生成看似合理的输出。这种评估缺口阻碍了ACWM作为可扩展预测模拟器在规划、策略评估和数据生成中的实际应用。

Method: 作者正式定义了Observable Simulator Contract（可观测模拟器契约），要求：给定动作必须引发对应的智能体运动，且环境响应必须基于该已实现的运动。为操作化该契约，引入WorldSimProbe，包含五个受控测试套件：局部控制灵敏度、全局轨迹变化、来源多样动作、交互接地和动力学。每个套件配备专门的评估器，测量模拟器相对校准、稠密动作-运动对应、虚假交互接地和原始级动力学。

Result: 在RoboTwin、ManiSkill和LIBERO三个基准上，对六个开源ACWM进行了超过18,000个实例的评估。WorldSimProbe揭示了控制变化下系统性的动作实现退化、交互接地和动力学方面的结构化失败，且基准信号与人类判断和下游结果一致。

Conclusion: 该基于能力的框架提供了一种透明、标准化的范式，用于诊断ACWM的模拟器保真度，超越了粗糙的、面向任务的评估方式，为ACWM的实际部署提供了更可靠的验证手段。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models (ACWMs) promise to provide embodied AI with scalable predictive simulators for planning, policy evaluation, and data generation. Realizing this promise requires precise action-conditioned transitions rather than merely plausible outputs. Yet their applicability remains difficult to establish because prevailing evaluations emphasize visual quality, task outcomes, or coarse rollout-level responsiveness without directly testing simulator fidelity. To address this gap, we evaluate ACWMs through the observable capabilities expected of physical simulators. Accordingly, we formalize Observable Simulator Contract, a minimal contract that any action-conditioned physical simulator should satisfy: supplied actions must induce corresponding agent motion, and environment responses must be grounded in that realized motion. To operationalize this contract, we introduce WorldSimProbe, comprising five controlled suites spanning local control sensitivity, global trajectory variation, source-diverse actions, interaction grounding, and dynamics. Suite-specific evaluators assess simulator-relative calibration, dense action-to-motion correspondence, false-interaction grounding, and primitive-level dynamics. We evaluate six open-source ACWMs on more than 18,000 instances across RoboTwin, ManiSkill, and LIBERO. World-SimProbe reveals systematic action-realization degradation across control variation, structured failures in interaction grounding and dynamics, and benchmark signals consistent with human judgments and downstream outcomes. Together, this capability-based framework provides a transparent, and standardized paradigm for diagnosing ACWM simulator fidelity beyond coarse, task-directed evaluation.

</details>


### [325] [SAFE-CHEM: Uncertainty-Aware Policy Switching for Robust Robotic Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09303)
*Laura Jones,Shazil Shahzad,Ayesha Sana,Gabriella Pizzuto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAFE-CHEM框架，一种面向化学实验室机器人操作的不确定性感知安全控制框架，通过集成循环神经网络模仿学习策略在线量化认知不确定性，并在不确定性超过校准阈值时自动切换到规则备份控制器，从而保障高风险化学实验中的操作安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数据驱动的学习方法在灵巧操作任务中取得了成功，但在高风险化学实验等场景中，基于学习的策略常难以区分安全与不安全动作，导致过度自信外推并可能引发灾难性失败，安全问题是其部署的主要障碍。

Method: SAFE-CHEM采用集成式循环神经网络模仿学习策略，通过动作预测方差在线量化认知不确定性；利用核密度估计刻画成功条件下的方差分布，建立校准的安全阈值；构建混合控制架构，在不确定性超阈值时从学习策略自动切换到确定性规则备份控制器。

Result: 在三个基础实验室操作任务上的实验表明，该混合策略相比传统单策略基线，提升了整体任务成功率并减少了关键安全违规；同时通过零样本sim-to-real迁移在物理Franka Production 3机器人上验证了框架的实用性。

Conclusion: SAFE-CHEM通过不确定性量化与混合控制相结合，有效弥合了学习策略在安全关键场景中部署的鸿沟，为自动化化学实验中的AI驱动机器人提供了可靠的安全保障方案。

Abstract: The deployment of autonomous robotic systems in chemistry laboratories is accelerating experimental workflows and providing the foundational data for AI-driven scientific discovery. However, despite the success of data-driven methods in acquiring dexterous skills, safety remains a primary barrier to their deployment in high-risk domains, such as early-stage materials chemistry experiments. Specifically, learning-based policies frequently struggle to distinguish between safe and unsafe actions, leading to overconfident extrapolation and potentially catastrophic failures. To mitigate these safety risks, we propose SAFE-CHEM, an uncertainty-aware framework designed for robust, learning-based robotic chemists. Our approach leverages an ensemble of recurrent neural network-based imitation learning policies to quantify epistemic uncertainty online through the variance of action predictions. By characterising the success-conditioned density of this variance using kernel density estimation, we introduce a hybrid control architecture that autonomously switches from the learned policy to a deterministic, rule-based backup controller when uncertainty exceeds a calibrated safety threshold. We evaluate SAFE-CHEM across three fundamental laboratory manipulation tasks, where our empirical results demonstrate that this hybrid strategy improves overall task success rates and reduces critical safety violations compared to traditional single-policy baselines. Finally, we demonstrate the practical viability of the framework through zero-shot sim-to-real transfer onto a physical Franka Production 3 robot manipulator.

</details>


### [326] [DH-VLM: Dual-Horizon Cooperative Latent Reasoning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09333)
*Ziyi Song,Chen Xia,Hang Yu,Sheng Zhou,Zhisheng Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DH-VLM，一种面向自动驾驶的双视野协同潜在推理框架。通过基础设施聚合多层隐藏状态形成全局推理视野的潜在引导，利用"基础设施驱动的潜在演化"机制将其融入自车模型，使自车在本地规划视野内利用长距离上下文理解来做出决策，并构建了面向协同的问-答数据集支持反事实和安全感知推理。实验表明在L2误差和碰撞率上分别超越SOTA 14.6%和26.9%，同时通信成本降低57.3%，GPU内存使用降低25.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型用于自动驾驶虽能增强全局理解和长时规划，但在单车部署时受限于感知范围和遮挡，且计算和延迟开销过大难以在车上部署。协同驾驶虽可通过外部代理交换信息，但现有方法在实际约束下的语义推理能力仍有限。

Method: 提出DH-VLM双视野协同潜在推理框架：(1)基础设施聚合多层隐藏状态构建全局推理视野的潜在引导；(2)通过基础设施驱动的潜在演化机制将全局引导融入自车模型，实现条件性潜在细化；(3)构建面向协同的问-答数据集，覆盖基础场景理解和自车个性化理解，支持反事实和安全感知推理。

Result: DH-VLM在规划性能上达到SOTA，L2误差降低14.6%，碰撞率降低26.9%；与基于查询的端到端协同驾驶方法相比，通信成本降低57.3%，GPU内存使用降低25.5%，且对基础设施引导错误具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DH-VLM为协同自动驾驶提供了实用且鲁棒的范式，通过不对称语义协同高效利用基础设施的全局理解能力，在显著降低通信和计算开销的同时保持强大的规划性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large-scale language models for autonomous driving enable enhanced global understanding and long-horizon planning. However, when deployed in isolated vehicles, limited sensing range and occlusions restrict reliable decision-making, and the substantial computational and latency overhead makes on-board deployment impractical. Cooperative driving provides a potential solution by leveraging external agents for information exchange, but existing methods remain limited in semantic reasoning capability under practical constraints. To address these challenges, we propose DH-VLM, a dual-horizon cooperative latent reasoning framework that enables asymmetric semantic cooperation between the infrastructure and ego vehicle. The infrastructure aggregates multi-layer hidden states to form a global-reasoning horizon latent guidance, which is integrated into the ego model through an Infrastructure-Driven Latent Evolution mechanism for conditional latent refinement. This enables the ego vehicle to leverage long-range contextual understanding while preserving autonomous decision-making within its local planning horizon. Furthermore, we construct a cooperation-oriented question-answer (QA) dataset covering fundamental scene understanding and ego-personalized comprehension to support counterfactual and safety-aware reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DH-VLM achieves state-of-the-art planning performance, outperforming the previous state of the art by 14.6% in L2 error and 26.9% in collision rate. Compared with query-based end-to-end cooperative driving methods, our approach reduces the communication cost by 57.3% and GPU memory usage by 25.5%, while maintaining strong robustness against infrastructure guidance errors, providing a practical and robust paradigm for cooperative autonomous driving.

</details>


### [327] [JEPA-WAM: Learning Vision-Language-Action Policies with Joint-Embedding World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09381)
*Yihan Lin,Jiawei He,Shifeng Bao,Chen Zhao,Yang Li,Xiaobo Wang,Yan Wang,Cheng Chi,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO
