TL;DR: JEPA-WAM是一种基于预训练V-JEPA空间的潜在世界动作模型，通过共享预测器将潜在状态转移预测与连续动作生成耦合，在LIBERO-Plus上达到79.2%（无大规模预训练最佳）和86.3%（π0.5实例化最佳），并在RoboTwin 2.0和真实双臂操作中展现强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式视频生成的世界动作模型（WAM）部署成本高，而现有潜在WAM要么过度压缩预测表征，要么将预测建模与动作生成所用的表征分离，缺乏高效的统一解决方案。

Method: 在预训练V-JEPA空间中构建潜在WAM，预测空间结构化的联合当前-未来目标以捕捉视觉时间结构并保持稠密patch级对应；通过共享预测器使转移监督直接塑造主干；该设计也可实例化到预训练VLA策略中而不破坏其原有感知和动作通路。

Result: LIBERO-Plus上达到79.2%（无大规模机器人策略预训练的最佳结果），预训练π0.5实例化达到86.3%（整体最佳）；在RoboTwin 2.0和真实双臂操作中验证了视觉和空间偏移下的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: JEPA-WAM证明了将潜在转移预测与动作生成通过共享预测器耦合的有效性，在不依赖大规模机器人策略预训练的情况下也能取得领先性能，并能无缝集成到现有VLA策略中。

Abstract: Robust robot control benefits from explicitly modeling state transitions, but video-generation world action models (WAMs) introduce substantial deployment cost. Existing latent WAMs avoid explicit future generation, but often compress predictive representations or separate predictive modeling from the representations used for action generation. We introduce JEPA-WAM, a latent WAM built in a pretrained V-JEPA space, which couples latent transition prediction with continuous action generation through a shared predictor. JEPA-WAM predicts a spatially structured joint current-future target that captures task-shared visual temporal structure between current and future observations, while preserving dense patch-level correspondence. Through the shared predictor, transition supervision directly shapes the backbone, from which dedicated representations are extracted for action prediction. The same design can also be instantiated in pretrained VLA policies while preserving their original perception and action pathways. On LIBERO-Plus, JEPA-WAM achieves 79.2%, the best result without large-scale robot-policy pretraining, while its pretrained $π_{0.5}$ instantiation reaches 86.3%, achieving the best overall performance. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 and real-world bimanual manipulation further demonstrate strong generalization under visual and spatial shifts.

</details>


### [328] [Beyond the Plane: Coupling Planar Vehicle Dynamics with Three-Dimensional Road Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09402)
*Simon Sagmeister,Phillip Pitschi,Nico Haja,Markus Lienkamp*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将平面车辆动力学模型与真实三维道路几何耦合的新方法，通过将二维车辆状态转换到三维空间，并计算道路几何引起的力与力矩施加到平面模型中，在拉斯维加斯赛车场高速数据上验证了该方法能有效缩小平面模拟与真实三维道路之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数车辆动力学模型仅考虑二维平面，而真实道路是三维的。三维道路几何对轮胎法向力的影响可达66%以上，导致高精度的平面模型难以准确复现真实车辆行为。虽然存在三维车辆动力学解决方案，但计算昂贵、复杂且很少被采用。

Method: 提出一种将平面车辆动力学模型与三维道路几何耦合的新方法：将平面模型的二维车辆状态转换到三维空间的对应表示，同时计算由道路几何引起的力与力矩，并将其施加回平面车辆模型中，从而在保持平面模型简单性的同时引入三维道路几何的影响。

Result: 在带坡度的拉斯维加斯赛车场使用全尺寸赛车的高速数据进行验证，并在合成赛道上验证了方法的准确性（包括边缘情况）。结果表明，该方法能够在不放弃更简单的平面模型的情况下，缩小平面模拟与真实三维道路之间的差距。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该耦合方法有效解决了平面动力学模型在三维道路上的局限性，兼顾了计算效率与模拟精度，为自动驾驶系统的开发与测试提供了实用的仿真工具。

Abstract: Simulation is crucial for developing and testing autonomous driving systems. In particular, the development of localization and control algorithms relies on an accurate vehicle dynamics simulation. However, most vehicle dynamics models are two-dimensional while real-world roads are three-dimensional. For example, effects from the three-dimensional road geometry on the Las Vegas Motor Speedway can increase the normal forces on the tires by more than 66% compared to the nominal load at standstill. As a result, even highly detailed planar vehicle dynamics models struggle to accurately reproduce the real vehicle's behavior. While solutions for three-dimensional vehicle dynamics exist, they are rarely adopted, computationally expensive, and complex. To address this issue, we present a novel method to couple planar vehicle dynamics models with real-world three-dimensional road geometry. We transform the planar vehicle state from the vehicle model's two-dimensional plane to its corresponding representation in three-dimensional space. Additionally, we calculate road-geometry-induced forces and moments and apply them to the planar vehicle model. We validate our approach using high-speed data recorded with a full-scale race car on the banked Las Vegas Motor Speedway. Furthermore, on synthetic tracks, we show that our method yields accurate results even in edge cases. Together, our results demonstrate that the gap between planar simulation and real-world three-dimensional roads can be closed without abandoning simpler planar models. To simplify adoption of our method, we provide the implementation as open-source software on github.com/TUMFTM/3d-road-geometry-coupling.

</details>


### [329] [Skills in Weights, Memory in Code: Hybrid Learning for Memory-Dependent Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09410)
*Yunhao Zhao,Zhenyang Ni,Haoyang Chen,Ruohan Zhang,Qi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HyMeS，一种混合学习框架，利用编码代理的推理和记忆管理能力来引导马尔可夫视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型处理记忆依赖的机器人操作任务。HyMeS通过梯度模仿学习低级运动技能，通过启发式学习高级记忆管理策略，并用多模态阶段完成验证闭环更新记忆。在RoboMemArena上，HyMeS将平均累积成功率从52.5%提升至66.2%，平均任务成功率从41.3%提升至60.1%，优于pi0.5和PrediMem基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略通常从当前观测或短固定长度历史生成动作块，但真实世界操作往往是非马尔可夫的，需要机器人保留和推理长跨度交互历史中的任务相关信息来确定下一步动作。

Method: HyMeS采用混合学习框架：1）通过梯度基模仿学习低级运动技能；2）编码代理通过启发式学习，从回滚反馈中迭代更新可执行启发式系统，获取高级记忆管理策略；3）通过多模态阶段完成验证（结合本体感觉信号和多帧VLM判断）闭环更新记忆，实现导向与执行之间的连接。

Result: 在RoboMemArena基准上，HyMeS将平均累积成功率从52.5%提升到66.2%，平均任务成功率从41.3%提升到60.1%（相比pi0.5）；且在累积成功率上超越PrediMem 4.5个百分点，任务成功率超越14.5个百分点。

Conclusion: HyMeS通过将记忆管理从VLA策略中解耦，仅需为可复用运动技能提供示范而非每个历史依赖的任务配置，实现了数据高效的组合泛化，显著提升了记忆依赖操作任务的性能。

Abstract: Modern vision-language-action (VLA) policies have acquired broad manipulation skills, but typically generate each action chunk from the current observation or a short fixed-length history. However, real-world manipulation is often non-Markovian, requiring robots to retain and reason over task-relevant information from long-horizon interaction histories to determine the next action. To address this challenge, we propose HyMeS, a hybrid learning framework that leverages the reasoning and memory-management capabilities of coding agents to steer a Markovian VLA for memory-dependent manipulation. Specifically, HyMeS learns low-level motor skills through gradient-based imitation learning, while a coding agent acquires high-level memory-management strategies through heuristic learning by iteratively updating an executable heuristic system from rollout feedback. Furthermore, we close the loop between steering and execution through multimodal stage-completion verification, which updates memory using proprioceptive signals and multi-frame VLM judgments. Compared with end-to-end memory-augmented VLAs, HyMeS requires demonstrations only for reusable motor skills rather than for every history-dependent task configuration, enabling data-efficient compositional generalization. On RoboMemArena, HyMeS improves mean cumulative success from 52.5% to 66.2% and mean task success from 41.3% to 60.1% over pi0.5, while outperforming PrediMem by 4.5 points in cumulative success and 14.5 points in task success.

</details>


### [330] [VANE: Reliable Test-Time Training for Vision-Language-Action Models via Future Visual Representation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09448)
*Hongjin Ji,Guoyang Xia,Luoyang Sun,Fangxiang Feng,Lei Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VANE框架，一种可靠的在测试时训练（TTT）方法，用于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的闭环操纵任务。VANE将提示适应条件化在当前视觉-语言上下文中，并从执行动作的未来视觉结果中学习。候选更新与活跃策略隔离，在后续观测中评估，只有在获得未来证据支持时才提交，使适应具有选择性和可逆性。在SimplerEnv WidowX上比TTT基线平均成功率提高3.2个百分点，但在Google Robot上显示部署时收益仍依赖于任务和具体实体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时训练（TTT）为从无标注部署流中适配VLA策略提供了一种轻量级方法，但在闭环操纵中难以可靠使用。现有方法存在两个问题：共享适应空间可能混合不兼容的任务修正，在线更新可能在后果未知前就改变后续动作。需要一种更加可靠、选择性且可逆的适应机制。

Method: VANE框架将提示适应条件化在当前视觉-语言上下文上，从执行动作的未来视觉后果中学习。具体而言，候选更新与活跃策略隔离，在后续观测上进行评估，只有当未来证据支持时才提交更新，从而实现了选择性和可逆的适应。

Result: 在SimplerEnv WidowX上，VANE比对应的TTT基线平均成功率提高3.2个百分点。Google Robot的结果进一步表明，部署时间的收益仍然依赖于任务和具体实体。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了一种受限的、基于证据的方法可以在交互过程中适配VLA策略，但需要注意到部署时的收益并不是普遍适用的，而是与具体任务和部署环境相关。

Abstract: Test-time training (TTT) offers a lightweight way to adapt vision--language--action (VLA) policies from unlabeled deployment streams, but it remains difficult to use reliably in closed-loop manipulation. A shared adaptation space can mix incompatible task corrections, while an online update can alter subsequent actions before its consequences are known. We introduce a reliable TTT framework for VLA policies (VANE). VANE conditions prompt adaptation on the current vision--language context and learns from the future visual consequences of executed actions. Candidate updates are isolated from the live policy, evaluated on subsequent observations, and committed only when supported by future evidence, making adaptation selective and reversible. On SimplerEnv WidowX, VANE improves average success by $3.2$ percentage points over the corresponding TTT baseline. Results on Google Robot further show that deployment-time gains remain task- and embodiment-dependent. Together, these results demonstrate a constrained, evidence-based approach to adapting VLA policies during interaction.

</details>


### [331] [Graph-Guided Safe Diffuser: Topological Graph Guidance for Safe Diffusion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09484)
*Nakgyu Yang,KwangBin Lee,SooJean Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: G2SD是一个分层框架，通过高层拓扑图规划器引导低层扩散模型，从结构层面保证安全性，避免推理时引导导致的流形破裂和运动学不可行问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的规划器依赖推理时引导（inference-time guidance）来保证安全，但这种轨迹变形方式容易导致流形破裂（manifold rupture），从而降低运动学可行性。作者希望从结构层面而非推理层面解决安全约束问题。

Method: G2SD采用分层架构：高层使用拓扑图规划器在学习的潜在图上进行规划，将数据流形抽象为潜在图以从结构层面保证安全；低层由扩散规划器生成连续轨迹，并以高层规划器选择的图节点表示作为条件输入。同时提供理论分析，给出流形破裂的发生条件，并证明G2SD通过增加分段数可降低约束违反概率。

Result: 在Maze2D导航任务中，G2SD将无碰撞的目标到达率从基线方法的40-50%提升到98%；在运动（locomotion）任务中也取得了更优的任务分数。

Conclusion: 通过将安全约束提升到图结构层面进行规划，G2SD有效避免了扩散规划器推理时引导所带来的流形破裂问题，显著提升了安全性与任务性能。

Abstract: Many diffusion-based planners enforce safety through inference-time guidance, but such interleaved trajectory deformations often degrade kinematic feasibility due to manifold rupture. We propose Graph-Guided Safe Diffuser (G2SD), a hierarchical framework that leverages a high-level topological graph planner to guide a low-level diffusion model. G2SD enforces safety at a structural level by abstracting the data manifold into a learned latent graph, on which high-level planning is performed. Continuous trajectories are generated by diffusion planners, which are conditioned on the graph node representations selected by the high-level planner. Theoretical analyses demonstrate conditions under which manifold rupture occurs in diffusion planners, and show that G2SD improves safety by reducing the constraint violation probability as the number of segments increases. Experiments demonstrate that G2SD substantially outperforms baselines, increasing goal-reaching rate without any collision from 40-50% to 98% in Maze2D navigation and also achieving superior task scores in locomotion.

</details>


### [332] [Rethink Before You Execute: Adaptive Execution for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09492)
*Feng Ye,Yiming Zhao,Yong Yu,Hongxu Zhou,Yong Pan,Yuan Xue,Peng Jia,Chuanmin Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TempoWAM，一种轻量级即插即用的执行方案，通过递归进度监控器实时估计任务进度，并采用自适应执行协议动态决定何时重新规划，从而改善World Action Models（WAMs）固定执行视界与执行动态不匹配的问题，在LIBERO、RoboTwin和真实世界任务上均取得效率与成功率上的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAMs在每次推理时生成一段动作块，机器人执行固定前缀后再重新规划。然而，固定的执行时域与执行动态不匹配：块可靠性在不同任务阶段存在差异，何时重新规划应取决于累积执行的结果，而非步数计数。

Method: TempoWAM包含两个核心组件：1）递归进度监控器（Recurrent Progress Monitor），从当前观测、任务指令、剩余动作和执行历史中估计任务进度；2）自适应执行协议（Adaptive Execution Protocol），评估当前动作块是否在推进任务以决定是否需要重新规划。为弥合训练与部署之间的差距，该协议通过任务依赖的校准因子进行在线自适应校准。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin和真实世界任务上，TempoWAM持续改善了WAM执行的效率-成功率权衡。在真实机器人上，简单任务中WAM推理减少26.9%且保持成功率，困难任务中成功率提升13.3个百分点。

Conclusion: TempoWAM验证了基于执行进度动态调整重规划时机的有效性，表明监控任务进度而非依赖固定步数能更好地平衡执行效率与任务成功率，同时作为一种即插即用方案，可方便地集成到现有WAM框架中。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly predict future actions and the evolution of the environment. At each inference, a WAM generates a chunk of actions and the robot executes a fixed prefix before replanning. We argue that this fixed execution horizon is poorly matched to execution dynamics: the chunk reliability varies across task stages, so when to replan depends on the result of accumulated execution, not on the step counts. We propose TempoWAM (Timing Execution by Monitoring Progress Online), a lightweight plug-and-play execution scheme for WAMs. A Recurrent Progress Monitor first estimates task progress from the current observation, task instruction, remaining actions, and execution history; and an Adaptive Execution Protocol then evaluates whether the chunk is advancing the task to decide if replanning is needed. To bridge the training-deployment gap, the protocol is calibrated by a task-dependent calibration factor with online adaptation. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and real-world tasks show that TempoWAM consistently improves the efficiency-success trade-off of WAM execution. On real robots, it reduces WAM inferences by 26.9% on easy tasks while maintaining success, and improves success by 13.3 points on difficult tasks.

</details>


### [333] [Tether-Inertial Localization for Planetary Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09515)
*Dielof van Loon,Anton Bredenbeck,Lennart Puck,Martin Azkarate,Salua Hamaza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于缆绳的惯性定位方法（Tether-Inertial Localization），利用缆绳长度和角度测量来估计无人机相对基站的位姿，结合解析悬链线模型与高斯过程残差补偿，实验表明平均RMSE可达5.2cm，比现有技术优一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星探测中UAV（如Ingenuity直升机）受限于载荷能力，导致飞行时间和机载计算资源受限。系缆连接可将电池和计算负担转移至基站，同时利用缆绳实现无漂移定位，但现有定位方法精度有限。

Method: 提出新颖的Tether-Inertial Localization方法：利用缆绳长度和角度测量估计UAV相对基站的位置；将计算高效的解析悬链线模型与高斯过程（GP）残差误差补偿相结合，以应对系统传感器误差和模型局限性。

Result: 在圆形、三角形和八字形轨迹上进行实验验证，缆绳长度达4.5米，总飞行时间37分钟。仅使用缆绳位置估计进行反馈时，解析悬链线模型平均RMSE为7.4cm，经GP残差补偿后降至5.2cm，比现有技术水平优一个数量级。

Conclusion: Tether-Inertial Localization可作为系缆无人机（TUAV）中基于视觉和GNSS定位方法的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Recent developments in planetary exploration have shown the potential of Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs), such as the Ingenuity helicopter that provided valuable mapping data. However, limited payload capabilities constrain the flight times and compute available for localization, which restrict their applicability. By providing a tethered connection, issues such as battery and computational constraints are offloaded to the base rover. At the same time, the cable can be exploited for non-drifting localization. This work presents a novel Tether-Inertial Localization approach that uses tether length and angle measurements to estimate the UAV position relative to its base. The method combines a computationally efficient analytical catenary model with a Gaussian Process (GP) residual error compensation. This accounts for systematic sensor inaccuracies and model limitations. Experimental validation across circular, triangular, and figure-eight trajectories with tether lengths up to 4.5 m and a total flight time of 37 minutes demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed approach. Using only tether-based position estimates for feedback, the analytical catenary model achieves an average RMSE of 7.4 cm, which is further reduced to 5.2 cm through GP-based residual compensation, one order of magnitude better than the state-of-the-art. These results establish Tether-Inertial Localization as a practical alternative to vision- and GNSS-based localization for Tethered Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (TUAVs).

</details>


### [334] [HarnessWAM: Bridging Prediction and Deliberation in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09516)
*Zhaopeng Gu,Bingke Zhu,Tianxi Lin,Guibo Zhu,Yingying Chen,Kai Wang,Tingyu Yuan,Chaoyang Zhao,Zhaowen Li,Peng Su,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessWAM，一个基于智能体框架的WAMs（世界动作模型）增强框架，通过视觉语言模型驱动的任务管理器、能力条件化的可执行空间投影，以及事件驱动的双时间尺度反馈循环，弥合WAMs在有限时域预测与复杂具身任务全局规划之间的"预测-深思鸿沟"，在RoboMemArena和RoboCerebra基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界动作模型（WAMs）虽然能够联合学习环境动态和机器人动作，但其有限时域的预测和动作生成能力不足以应对复杂的具身任务——这些任务需要全局规划、跨阶段状态维护、执行验证和失败恢复。作者将这一缺陷称为"预测-深思鸿沟"（prediction-deliberation gap），并旨在解决该问题。

Method: HarnessWAM框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的任务管理器，维护基于证据的场景信念和结构化任务图；(2) 能力条件化的可执行空间投影，将开放性语义计划约束为满足任务依赖、具身状态约束和底层WAM能力边界的原子技能序列；(3) 事件驱动的双时间尺度反馈循环——轻量级进度估计器提供高频执行证据，任务管理器在关键里程碑处结合当前观测、任务状态和交互历史进行深思决策（推进任务、获取额外观测、修订计划或局部恢复）。

Result: HarnessWAM在RoboMemArena上实现了59.6%的全任务成功率和69.9%的子任务成功率，在RoboCerebra Ideal上实现了23.7%的成功率，均达到当前最优（SOTA）水平。

Conclusion: 模型外部的结构化状态维护和闭环智能体决策能够有效将WAMs的局部控制能力扩展为可规划、可验证、可恢复的具身任务执行能力，从而弥合了预测与深思之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly learn environmental dynamics and robot actions, introducing priors over physical evolution into embodied control. However, finite-horizon prediction and action generation are insufficient for complex embodied tasks that require global planning, cross-stage state maintenance, execution verification, and failure recovery. We refer to this mismatch as the prediction-deliberation gap of WAMs. To address this gap, we propose HarnessWAM, an agentic framework for WAMs. HarnessWAM employs a vision-language-model-based Task Manager to maintain an evidence-grounded scene belief and a structured task graph. A capability-conditioned executable-space projection further constrains open-ended semantic plans into sequences of atomic skills that satisfy task dependencies, embodiment-state constraints, and the capability boundary of the underlying WAM. During execution, HarnessWAM operates through an event-driven, dual-timescale feedback loop: a lightweight progress estimator continuously provides high-frequency execution evidence, while the Task Manager deliberates at salient milestones by jointly considering the current observation, task state, and interaction history to determine whether to advance the task, acquire additional observations, revise the plan, or initiate local recovery. This mechanism enables the robot to recover its state after a subtask failure and resume execution without discarding previously acquired scene knowledge. HarnessWAM achieves state-of-the-art full-task and subtask success rates of 59.6% and 69.9% on RoboMemArena, and an SR of 23.7% on RoboCerebra Ideal. These results demonstrate that model-external structured state maintenance and closed-loop agentic decision making can effectively extend the local control capabilities of WAMs into embodied task execution that is plannable, verifiable, and recoverable.

</details>


### [335] [Robotic Fabric Alignment System for Sewing Using Global Local Weighted ICP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09528)
*Wenbo Dong,Dipankar Bhattacharya Member,Kai Tang,Akinari Kobayashi,Fuyuki Tokuda,Akira Seino,Norman C. Tien,Kazuhiro Kosuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的自动化织物对齐系统，该系统采用全局局部加权迭代最近点（GLW-ICP）方法，在缝纫前自动估计上下织物面板的位姿并操作顶部面板实现精确对齐，在遮挡和非遮挡条件下均能达到毫米级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缝纫前的织物准确对齐是关键的工艺步骤，传统方法无法同时兼顾全局边缘和局部缝线的对齐，特别是在存在遮挡的情况下，难以实现高精度自动化对齐。

Method: 提出全局局部加权迭代最近点（GLW-ICP）方法，通过全局对齐织物边缘点与局部对齐缝线点至对应CAD模型点，同时去除遮挡区域中未匹配的点，实现边缘和缝线的双重精确对齐。

Result: 在多种织物形状的真实实验中，系统在遮挡和非遮挡条件下均稳定实现毫米级对齐精度，验证了方法的有效性和实际适用性。

Conclusion: 该自动化织物对齐系统通过GLW-ICP方法有效解决了实际缝纫场景中织物对齐的精度和鲁棒性问题，适用于自动化缝纫生产线。

Abstract: Accurate fabric alignment is a critical step that must be performed before sewing. This paper presents a novel automated fabric alignment system. The system estimates the poses of top and bottom fabric panels, lying flat and wrinkle-free in arbitrary positions, using a new Global Local Weighted Iterative Closest Point (GLW-ICP) method. The system then manipulates the top panel to achieve precise alignment at both edges and sewing lines. Unlike conventional approaches, GLW-ICP robustly aligns both global edges and local sewing lines by globally aligning fabric edge points and locally aligning sewing line points to their corresponding CAD model points, while removing unmatched points in occluded regions. Real-world experiments with various fabric shapes show that the system consistently achieves millimeter-level alignment accuracy under both occlusion and non-occlusion conditions, demonstrating its effectiveness and suitability for automated fabric alignment in practical scenarios.

</details>


### [336] [Model-Based Systems Engineering Framework for SysML-Driven Design of Autonomous UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09547)
*Deekshitha Angadi,Naveena Budda,Vikas Agarwal,Mohamed Samshad,Bharath Kumar Suryadevara,Narsimlu Kemsaram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于SysML的模型驱动系统工程（MBSE）设计框架，用于自主无人机的系统化开发。该框架通过四个连接层次（利益相关者需求、功能分解、逻辑架构、物理/软件分配）组织开发流程，并将逻辑架构系统地映射到ROS 2软件架构，支持需求分配、接口定义和验证规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主无人机是具有安全、时序和可靠性约束的复杂信息物理系统，但许多开发流程仍依赖文档中心的需求和分离的架构描述，导致早期设计阶段出现歧义、接口不一致和弱可追溯性问题。

Method: 采用SysML作为正式设计骨架，通过四个连接层组织无人机开发。使用多种SysML图表（需求图、活动图、块定义图、内部块图、状态机图和参数图）捕获系统的功能、结构、行为和接口方面，并将逻辑架构映射到ROS 2软件架构（块到节点、流端口到话题、请求响应到服务、目标导向行为到动作）。

Result: 通过代表性自主无人机任务场景（自动起飞、航点导航、悬停稳定、避障、返航和应急处理）在设计层面展示了框架的有效性，模型支持需求分配、接口定义、子系统职责分配和验证规划。

Conclusion: 该MBSE框架为自主无人机的SysML驱动开发提供了一种系统化方法，能够在仿真或物理部署前完成完整的设计验证和支持规划工作。

Abstract: Autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are complex cyber-physical systems that require the coordinated integration of flight control, navigation, perception, communication, power management, and mission-level decision-making under safety, timing, and reliability constraints. However, many autonomous UAV development workflows still rely on document-centric requirements, separated architectural descriptions, and software implementation artifacts, which can lead to ambiguity, interface inconsistencies, and weak traceability during early design. This paper presents a Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) design framework for the SysML-driven development of autonomous UAVs. The proposed framework uses the Systems Modeling Language (SysML) as a formal design backbone to structure UAV development across four connected layers: stakeholder requirements, functional decomposition, logical architecture, and physical/software allocation. SysML requirement diagrams, activity diagrams, block definition diagrams, internal block diagrams, state machine diagrams, and parametric diagrams are used to capture the functional, structural, behavioral, interface, and performance aspects of the UAV system. The logical architecture is then systematically mapped to a Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2) software architecture by relating SysML blocks to ROS 2 nodes, flow ports and connectors to topics, request-response interactions to services, and goal-oriented behaviors to actions. The framework is illustrated at the design level using representative autonomous UAV mission scenarios, including autonomous take-off, waypoint navigation, hover stabilization, obstacle avoidance, return-to-home, and emergency handling. The resulting model supports requirement allocation, interface definition, subsystem responsibility assignment, and verification planning before simulation or physical deployment.

</details>


### [337] [TDMA Based Communications Control Co-Design for Cooperative Carrying: Delay Calibration and Sampling-Rate Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09556)
*Zahra Seifaei,Maximilian Luebke,Torsten Reissland,Danial Dehghani,Norman Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了多机器人协作运输中，如何通过自适应采样时间调整和领导者轮换策略，在保持控制性能的同时提升无线通信负载的公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作运输面临维持稳定控制与保持通信效率之间的根本性挑战，现有方法尚未同时考虑动态采样、领导者轮换与无线协议交互的综合影响。

Method: 基于MuJoCo物理仿真与真实无线建模（含TDMA、MAC、抖动、队列和丢包），对比三种控制方案：固定采样+静态领导、动态采样+静态领导、动态采样+轮换领导。

Result: 动态采样在不牺牲控制性能的前提下有效降低通信开销；轮换领导角色能显著提升空口时间分配的公平性，且对团队搬运能力影响极小。

Conclusion: 该工作是首批在物理现实的多机器人协作中联合考察动态采样、领导者轮换和无线协议交互的研究之一，为通信资源受限的真实场景中部署协作机器人团队提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Multi robot teams performing cooperative transportation face a fundamental challenge: maintaining stable control while keeping communications efficient. This paper investigates how adaptive sampling time adjustment informed by measured network delay and strategic leader rotation can distribute wireless load fairly across the team. We use physics based simulation in MuJoCo with realistic wireless modeling, including time division multiple access, medium access control, jitter, queueing, and packet loss, to evaluate three control approaches: fixed sampling with static leadership, dynamic sampling with static leadership, and dynamic sampling with rotating leadership. Our results reveal an important trade off: dynamic sampling effectively reduces communications overhead without compromising control performance, while rotating the leader role meaningfully improves how fairly airtime is distributed all with negligible impact on the team carrying ability. to the best of our knowledge, being among the first to jointly examine dynamic sampling, rotating leadership, and wireless protocol interactions in physicsrealistic multi robot cooperation, this work provides practical guidance for deploying coordinated robotic teams in real world settings where communications resources are limited.

</details>


### [338] [FactorDrive: Adaptive Multi-Step Reasoning Driven by Planning-Critical Factors for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09591)
*Guolei Huang,Tengfei She,Yuxuan Lu,Yao Huang,Yuqi Ye,Yongjun Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了FactorDrive，一个端到端自动驾驶框架，通过规划关键因子（PCFs）驱动的自适应多步推理来提升规划性能。包含大规模驾驶域指令微调、PCF-CoT数据集构建（将规划推理锚定于轨迹相关的空间物理证据），以及QS-GRPO算法（用轨迹级奖励引导MCTS搜索高质量推理路径并通过GRPO优化策略）。在nuScenes和NAVSIM基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM自动驾驶方法存在三个不足：一是未充分将空间物理证据整合进规划推理；二是推理适应性粗粒度，无法匹配场景特定的规划需求；三是推理路径优化在自动驾驶后训练中几乎未被探索。

Method: 1) 大规模驾驶域指令微调建立基础驾驶知识；2) 构建PCF-CoT数据集，围绕场景特定的规划关键因子组织推理，使推理路径的组成和深度自适应不同规划需求；3) 提出QS-GRPO，用轨迹级规划奖励引导MCTS搜索更高规划质量的推理路径，并通过GRPO优化策略。

Result: 在开放环nuScenes和闭环导向NAVSIM基准上均取得最优（SOTA）规划性能。

Conclusion: FactorDrive验证了将空间物理证据融入推理、自适应推理深度以及搜索优化推理路径能显著提升端到端自动驾驶的规划质量。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have advanced scene understanding and enabled explicit reasoning in end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing methods insufficiently integrate spatial-physical evidence into planning reasoning, while reasoning adaptation remains coarse-grained and falls short of scene-specific planning demands. Furthermore, reasoning-path optimization for higher planning quality remains largely unexplored in autonomous-driving post-training. To address these limitations, we propose FactorDrive, an end-to-end autonomous driving framework for adaptive multi-step reasoning driven by planning-critical factors (PCFs). We first perform large-scale driving-domain instruction tuning to establish foundational driving knowledge. Building on this foundation, we construct PCF-CoT, a chain-of-thought (CoT) dataset that grounds planning reasoning in trajectory-relevant spatial-physical evidence and organizes reasoning around scene-specific PCFs, enabling the composition and depth of reasoning paths to adapt to different planning demands. We further introduce Quality Search-Guided Group Relative Policy Optimization (QS-GRPO), which guides Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) with trajectory-level planning rewards to discover reasoning paths with higher planning quality and uses the resulting responses to optimize the policy through GRPO, thereby improving trajectory planning performance. Extensive experiments on both open-loop (nuScenes) and closed-loop-oriented (NAVSIM) benchmarks demonstrate that FactorDrive achieves state-of-the-art planning performance.

</details>


### [339] [Nonlinear Model Predictive Control of a Robotic Soft Esophagus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09602)
*Dipankar Bhattacharya,Ryman Hashem,Leo K. Cheng,Weiliang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了RoSEv2.0，一种嵌入TOF距离传感器和压力传感器的机器人软食管平台，用于实现蠕动波的闭环控制。通过SINDYC模型预测系统未来状态，并采用模型预测控制（MPC）自主调节蠕动波及气压轮廓。实验成功生成了不同振幅的蠕动波，并进行了支架迁移测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前版本的RoSE因缺乏可视性和嵌入式传感能力，只能通过开环方式生成和控制蠕动波，无法实现精确的闭环控制。

Method: 在RoSEv2.0中嵌入TOF位移传感器和气压传感器，采集数据并利用稀疏辨识非线性动力学（SINDYC）方法发现系统动态模型，进而实现基于该模型的模型预测控制（MPC）来自主生成蠕动波和控制气压轮廓。

Result: MPC成功生成了速度为20 mm/s、波长为75 mm、振幅为5/7.5/10 mm的蠕动波；该平台还被用于不同食团稠度条件下的支架迁移测试。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将SINDYC-based MPC应用于RoSE的闭环控制问题，实现了期望蠕动波的精确生成，解决了软体食管平台缺乏闭环控制的瓶颈。

Abstract: Strictures caused by esophageal cancer can narrow down the esophageal lumen, leading to dysphagia. Palliation of dysphagia has driven the development of a Robotic Soft Esophagus (RoSE), which provides a novel in vitro platform for esophageal stent testing and food viscosity studies. In RoSE, peristaltic wave generation and control were done in an open-loop manner since the conduit lacked visibility and embedded sensing capability. Hence, in this work, RoSE version 2.0 (RoSEv2.0) is designed with embedded Time Of Flight (TOF) and pressure sensors to measure conduit displacement and air pressure, respectively, for modeling and control. Model Predictive Control (MPC) of RoSEv2.0 is implemented to govern the peristalsis and air pressure profile autonomously. The implemented MPC used Sparse Identification Nonlinear Dynamics with Control (SINDYC) models to estimate the future states of ROSEv2.0. The dynamic models are discovered from the TOF and pressure sensor data. Peristalsis waves of speed 20 mm/s, wavelength 75 mm, and amplitudes 5, 7.5, and 10 mm were successfully generated by the MPC. Additionally, RoSEv2.0 with the MPC was employed to perform stent migration testing with various food bolus consistencies. The major contribution claimed in this paper is the application of SINDYC-based MPC to solve the closed-loop control problem of RoSE for achieving desired peristaltic waves.

</details>


### [340] [Predictive safety filter enhanced curriculum learning control for efficient vehicle dynamics controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09653)
*Baocong Zhang,Siliang Lu,Chenyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合课程学习与基于物理的预测安全滤波器的车辆运动与动力学控制方法，在Python-CarSim平台上验证，在多种驾驶工况下表现出更好的性能和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的车辆控制方法需要大量的参数标定工作，而学习控制虽能带来更好的性能和效率，但难以保证安全性。因此需要在保持优良性能的同时提升安全性和鲁棒性。

Method: 采用课程学习（Curriculum Learning）训练控制器，并集成基于物理的预测安全滤波器（Physics-based Predictive Safety Filter），以实现在提升稳定性和敏捷性的同时保障安全。

Result: 在Python-CarSim平台上进行验证，结果表明该方法在多种机动工况下相比先前工作具有更好的改进效果和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了学习控制安全性不足的问题，在保持良好性能的同时增强了安全性，且具有良好的可扩展性，可用于车辆运动与动力学控制任务。

Abstract: Recent advances in learning-based control have enabled impressive achievements in solving complex control problems in various domains. However, since learning-based control may not be able to realize safety-guaranties, it is of great importance to enhance safety and robustness while maintaining good performances. Take vehicle motion \& dynamics control as an example, in order to overcome the pain points of traditional methods such as heavy parameter calibration effort and learning-based control to bring better performance and efficiency in stability \& agility over prior work for state-based vehicle control tasks, in this work, our method aims to develop a curriculum learning controller enhanced with physics-based predictive safety filter. The validation is conducted with the Python-CarSim platform, demonstrating better improvements and scalability under various maneuvers.

</details>


### [341] [Removing Infrastructure Barriers in Human-Robot Collaboration Through Wireless Reconfigurable Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09658)
*Emma Takács,Mátyás Hajós,Ádám Juniki,Ádám Fischer,Zoltán Komáromi,Kristóf Abai,Dániel Horváth,Sándor Máthé,Konstantinos Kousias,Bence Tipary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于5G的无线人机协作（HRC）测试平台，集成了电池供电的多传感器平台和计算机视觉模块，可在不同环境中实现物体检测、位姿估计和手部识别（mAP@50-95达97.74%，推理时间12.5ms），并在匈牙利和挪威的多种5G基础设施上验证了低至12ms的往返响应时间，但同时也揭示了当前5G部署中的互操作性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HRC工作单元受限于电力和数据线缆，难以适应再制造等动态、高混合、低产量的工业场景中频繁的工作单元重构需求，而适合实时感知和安全协作的商用无线设备选择有限。

Method: 构建无线5G测试平台，开发电池供电的多传感器原型平台消除基础设施障碍；集成基于合成与真实数据训练的计算机视觉模块（目标检测、位姿估计、手部识别）；将计算密集型任务通过5G卸载到边缘计算，解决带宽-延迟权衡；在匈牙利和挪威的公共/私有、SA/NSA 5G网络上进行实验验证。

Result: 模型在变化的光照和背景条件下，定向抓取位姿和手部检测的mAP@50-95达97.74±0.10%，平均推理时间12.5ms；在兼容的网络-设备配对下，往返响应时间可低至12ms，适用于安全自适应的HRC；但测量结果也暴露了当前5G部署中互操作性方面的实际限制。

Conclusion: 所提出的无线5G HRC系统在可移植性和实时性能方面展现出良好潜力，适用于再制造、操作员培训和用户研究等场景，但当前5G网络部署的互操作性问题仍需在未来工作中解决。

Abstract: Human-Robot Collaboration (HRC) plays a vital role in dynamic, high mix, low volume industrial scenarios such as remanufacturing, which frequently face workcell rearrangements. Traditional setups are constrained by power and data cabling, restricting modularity and reconfigurations, while the selection of commercial wireless devices suitable for real-time perception and safe collaboration are limited in availability. This paper presents a highly flexible, wireless, 5G-based system that serves as a versatile experimental testbed for applications including remanufacturing, operator training, and user studies. To eliminate infrastructure barriers, the workcell integrates a novel battery-powered, multi-sensor platform prototype. Additionally, to support operator safety and system adaptability across environmental shifts, the system integrates a computer vision module for object detection and pose estimation, further augmented for robust hand recognition. Trained on synthetic and real data, the model reliably detects oriented grasping poses and human hands across varying lighting and background conditions (with an mAP@50-95 of 97.74 +- 0.10% and a mean inference time of 12.5 ms). Offloading these computationally intensive tasks to the edge via 5G, the proposed architecture contributes to resolving the bandwidth-latency trade-off. To demonstrate portability, the system was implemented in both Hungary and Norway, and was evaluated across a combination of public and private, Standalone and Non-Standalone 5G infrastructures. The performed network experiments produced results in round-trip response times down to 12 ms in case of compatible network-device pairings, suitable for safe, adaptive HRC. However, these measurements also revealed practical limitations related to interoperability in current 5G deployments that should be addressed in future works.

</details>


### [342] [TAMS: Task-Aware Multi-View Adaptive Streaming for Wireless Telerobotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09731)
*Zexin Deng,Zhenhui Yuan,Lu Tian,Subhash Lakshminarayana,Longhao Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TAMS系统，一种面向无线遥操作的任务感知多视角自适应流传输方案，根据操纵阶段动态分配视频码率，优先保障操作者最相关视角的带宽。实验表明在受限网络下相比均衡分配方案，TAMS显著提升任务完成时间（68.9s降至43.9s）和成功率（48%提升至71%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线遥操作依赖多视角视频反馈，但上行带宽往往有限且动态变化，现有静态或均衡的码率分配方式无法根据操作任务需求进行自适应调整，导致关键视角画质不足，影响操作效率和成功率。

Method: TAMS通过轻量级机器人侧信号推断当前任务阶段，根据操纵阶段动态分配视频码率，优先为操作者最相关的相机视角分配更多带宽，同时为次要视角保留基础可见性。

Result: 在六自由度遥操作测试平台和三种受限网络条件下，相比均衡分配和静态分配基线，TAMS提升主视角SSIM，缩短任务完成时间并提高成功率。在最受限带宽下，平均完成时间从68.9s降至43.9s，成功率从48%提升至71%。

Conclusion: 任务感知的带宽分配策略能有效应对动态受限上行链路，显著改善无线遥操作体验，代码已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Wireless telerobotic manipulation relies on timely multi-view video feedback, but the available uplink bandwidth is often limited and dynamic. This paper presents Task-Aware Multi-View Adaptive Streaming (TAMS), a system that allocates video bitrate according to the current manipulation phase. TAMS infers task phase from lightweight robot-side signals and prioritizes the camera view most relevant to the operator while preserving baseline visibility for secondary views. Experiments on a six-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) teleoperation testbed under three constrained network conditions show that TAMS improves primary view Structural Similarity Index (SSIM), reduces task completion time, and increases trial success rate compared with equal and static allocation baselines. Under the most constrained bandwidth condition, TAMS reduces mean completion time from 68.9 s to 43.9 s relative to equal allocation and increases trial success rate from 48% to 71%. Code is available at: https://github.com/Dzxx623/TAMS.

</details>


### [343] [Efficient Real-World Online Reinforcement Learning for Robot Manipulation via Centralized Training and Critic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09762)
*Changhao Li,Yifang Zhang,Heng Zhang,Davide Torielli,Damiano Gasperini,Arturo Laurenzi,Luca Muratore,Arash Ajoudani,Nikos Tsagarakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，结合集中训练分散执行（CTDE）与混合奖励架构（HRA），用于真实世界在线强化学习中的机器人操纵策略训练。通过共享集中式多头批判器（分解为任务头和抓取头），解决多智能体并发训练导致的非平稳性及小随机化范围问题，在多个任务上将成功率显著提升（如块搬运从25%到95%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实世界在线RL方法虽通过人类干预实现样本高效学习，但受限于较小的随机化范围，且面临多个智能体并发训练带来的非平稳性挑战。需要一种统一框架来克服这些局限。

Method: 提出统一框架，将集中训练与分散执行（CTDE）和混合奖励架构（HRA）结合。多个执行者共享一个集中式多头批判器，批判器被分解为任务头和抓取头，分别对应稀疏任务奖励和基于势能的抓取奖励。同时重新表述批判器和执行者的目标函数，以利用分解的Q值并显式考虑离散抓取策略的分类动作分布。

Result: 在网球和香蕉拾放、锅复位及模拟块搬运任务上验证，随机化范围比先前工作大5-25倍。相比SOTA基线，成功率从60%提升至80%（网球拾放）、从60%提升至90%（香蕉拾放）、从25%提升至95%（模拟块搬运），且在基线始终失败的任务上取得成功。

Conclusion: 该框架显著提升了真实世界在线RL的样本效率和策略性能，在更大的维度域随机化下表现优异，展示了人类参与式学习在多机器人系统上的潜力。

Abstract: Real-world online reinforcement learning (RL) provides a promising approach for training robotic manipulation policies directly in the physical world, avoiding the sim-to-real gap and enabling continuous policy refinement through human-in-the-loop interaction. Recent methods have demonstrated sample-efficient learning through human intervention but remain limited to small randomization ranges and encounter challenges with the non-stationarity induced by concurrently training multiple agents. To address these limitations, we introduce a unified framework that combines centralized training with decentralized execution (CTDE) and a Hybrid Reward Architecture (HRA). This enables multiple actors to share a centralized multi-head critic. The critic is decomposed into task and grasp heads, corresponding to the sparse task reward and a potential-based grasping reward, respectively. We accordingly reformulate the critic and actor objectives to exploit the decomposed Q-values while explicitly accounting for the categorical action distribution of the discrete gripper policy. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework substantially improves both sample efficiency and policy performance. We validate our approach on two robotic arms and a simulated humanoid robot across tennis ball and banana pick-and-place, pot reset, and simulated block relocation tasks under dimension-wise domain randomization, approximately 5-25x larger than those considered in prior work. Compared with a state-of-the-art baseline, our method improves the success rate from 60% to 80% on tennis ball pick-and-place, from 60% to 90% on banana pick-and-place, and from 25% to 95% on simulated block relocation, while also successfully accomplishing a task where the baseline consistently fails. Videos and more details are available at our project website: https://hil-harc.github.io/.

</details>


### [344] [SLIM-0.5B: Learning Action-Grounded Predictive Latents for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09771)
*Jingkai Wang,Zihan Tang,Gu Zhang,Mingyu Cao,Jiapeng Chen,Jingjiao Zhao,Xiansheng Chen,Pengwei Wang,Lemao Liu,Dejing Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SLIM，一个仅0.5B参数的紧凑型隐式交互策略模型。SLIM通过自监督掩码轨迹预测学习动作导向的预测性隐变量，同时捕获动作条件下的未来状态转换以及解释观测变化的动作。其采用紧凑的Mixture-of-Transformers骨干网络建模观测隐变量与动作token之间的交互，并通过流匹配进行语言条件动作生成。在仿真基准和真实世界评估中，SLIM以更少的参数、更低推理延迟和更少GPU内存，匹配或超越了大规模VLA和世界动作模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略依赖大规模多模态骨干网络，但其大量容量服务于开放域语义理解，而连续机器人操作本质上只需要对观测、动作及动作引起转换的紧凑表示。像素级世界模型虽作为另一条路径，但预测与控制无关的视觉细节代价高昂。因此需要一种紧凑、高效且任务导向的隐式交互策略模型。

Method: SLIM通过自监督掩码轨迹预测学习动作导向的预测性隐变量，联合进行动作重建与未来隐变量预测。模型采用紧凑的Mixture-of-Transformers(MoT)骨干网络来建模观测隐变量与动作token之间的交互关系，最后通过流匹配(flow matching)实现语言条件动作生成。整体模型参数仅0.5B。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实世界评估中，SLIM以显著更少的参数量、无额外具身预训练、更低推理延迟和更低GPU内存占用，匹配或超越了代表性的大规模VLA和世界动作模型基线。

Conclusion: SLIM证明：面向控制任务学习的紧凑隐式交互模型，可以在参数规模和计算成本大幅降低的情况下，达到甚至超越大规模视觉-语言-动作策略的性能，表明构建高效机器人控制策略不必依赖超大参数的多模态基础模型。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies rely on large multimodal backbones to jointly perform perception, language conditioning, and action generation at every control step. Much of this capacity supports open-domain semantics, whereas continuous robot manipulation primarily requires compact representations of observations, actions, and the transitions induced by actions. Pixel-level world models provide another route, but predicting visual details irrelevant to control can be unnecessarily expensive. We propose SLIM (Self-supervised Latent Interaction Model), a compact 0.5B-parameter latent interaction policy. SLIM learns action-grounded predictive latents that capture both action-conditioned future transitions and the actions that explain observed changes. SLIM learns these representations through self-supervised masked trajectory prediction, combining action reconstruction with future-latent prediction. A compact Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) backbone models interactions between observation latents and action tokens. The resulting policy is trained with flow matching for language-conditioned action generation. Across simulation benchmarks and real-world evaluation, SLIM matches or exceeds representative large-scale VLA and world-action-model baselines with fewer parameters, no additional embodied pretraining, lower inference latency, and substantially lower GPU memory usage.

</details>


### [345] [RoboSeg: Online Part-Level Semantic Reconstruction for Robotic Manipulation via a Single Eye-in-Hand Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09778)
*Zhaochen Lan,Mengxiang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboSeg是一个部件级语义重建系统，通过视觉语言模型发现功能部件、异步在线RGB-D语义重建和任务导向抓取生成相结合，无需CAD模型即可实现部件级语义索引，达到83.4%的平均部件IoU，在24次物理实验中实现21次任务成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作需要识别可操作部件（如把手、边缘、触发器、工具尖端），而不仅仅是物体类别或点云信息，现有系统缺乏部件级语义理解能力。

Method: RoboSeg由三个模块组成：1) 在初始RGB观测上查询VLM获取紧凑的功能部件提示词；2) 双异步流扫描——高频几何线程负责RGB-D里程计和TSDF融合，关键帧触发的语义线程负责SAM3部件掩码；3) 通过体素级时间投票将投影掩码融合为持久化的部件标记点云，并用于将AnyGrasp的6-DoF抓取候选分配给语义部件，选择与任务相关部件标签一致的抓取。

Result: 在手动标注物体上达到83.4%的平均部件IoU；在4个物体8个任务的24次物理实验中，所选抓取在所有试验中均接触请求部件，实现21/24任务成功。

Conclusion: RoboSeg可作为任务条件化操作的语义索引层，其中AnyGrasp作为提议生成器被保留，验证了VLM驱动的部件级语义重建在实际机器人操作中的有效性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation requires perception systemsthat identify actionable parts such as handles, rims, triggers,and tool tips, not merely object categories or point clouds. This paper presents RoboSeg, a part-level semantic reconstructionsystem that links vision-language model (VLM) functional-partdiscovery, asynchronous online RGB-D semantic reconstruc-tion, and task-oriented grasp generation without requiring CAD models or pre-scanned meshes. RoboSeg queries a VLM onthe initial RGB observation to obtain compact functional part prompts, then scans with two asynchronous streams: a high-frequency geometry thread for RGB-D odometry and truncated signed distance function (TSDF) fusion, and a keyframe-triggered semantic thread for SAM3 part masks. Projectedmasks are fused by voxel-level temporal voting into a persistentpart-labeled point cloud; RoboSeg uses this map to assign AnyGrasp 6-DoF candidates to semantic parts and select grasps consistent with the task-relevant part label. RoboSeg reaches 83.4% mean part intersection-over-union (mIoU) over manually labeled objects; in a 24-trial physical pilot across fourobjects and eight tasks, the selected grasp contacts the requestedpart in all trials and achieves 21/24 combined task successes.These results characterize RoboSeg as a semantic indexing layerfor task-conditioned manipulation, with AnyGrasp retained asthe proposal generator.

</details>


### [346] [WRAP: Wasserstein-Robust Adaptive Plug-in for Robot Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09807)
*Minhyuk Jang,Astghik Hakobyan,Jungjin Lee,Naira Hovakimyan,Insoon Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WRAP，一个与适配器无关的Wasserstein鲁棒插件，用于非线性EKF和ESKF定位系统。它通过因果模块提供时变有效过程/测量统计量，并使用均值保持的Wasserstein局部更新计算最坏情况协方差和鲁棒增益。在18条UWB-IMU序列上，WRAP相比标称ESKF将三维位置RMSE降低了27.4%，在Jetson Orin Nano上鲁棒求解仅需0.05ms（UWB）和2.92ms（GNSS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人定位在变化的感知条件下会遭受有偏误差和协方差校准不准的问题，传统EKF/ESKF在非高斯、分布偏移的环境中鲁棒性不足。

Method: WRAP作为适配器无关的插件，包含两个核心组件：(1)因果模块提供时变的有效过程与测量统计量；(2)均值保持的Wasserstein局部更新，计算最不利协方差与鲁棒增益。该方法将均值适应与协方差鲁棒化分离，分别对传播和感知使用不同的半径。

Result: 在18条UWB-IMU序列（从适配器训练中留出）上，适配器单独降低RMSE 19.8%，WRAP降低27.4%；各向同性消融达到19.5%，证明增量收益来自方向性过程协方差重分配。GNSS-INS样本内研究表明均值适应贡献了大部分精度提升，DR则改善一致性并缓解过度收紧的经典协方差估计。求解延迟在Jetson Orin Nano上为0.05ms（UWB）和2.92ms（GNSS）。

Conclusion: WRAP通过分离均值适应与协方差鲁棒化，在不改变传播模型、残差或回缩的前提下显著提升EKF/ESKF在变化感知条件下的定位精度与一致性，且计算开销极低，适合嵌入式实时部署。

Abstract: Robotic localization under changing sensing conditions can suffer from biased errors and miscalibrated covariances. We present WRAP, an adapter-agnostic Wasserstein-robust plug-in for nonlinear extended Kalman filter (EKF) and error-state Kalman filter (ESKF) stacks. A causal module supplies time-varying effective process and measurement statistics; a mean-preserving Wasserstein local update then computes least-favorable covariances and a robust gain without changing the propagation model, residual, or retraction. This separates mean adaptation from covariance robustification and uses distinct radii for propagation and sensing. On 18 UWB--IMU sequences held out from adapter training, adapter-only and WRAP reduce mean 3-D position RMSE by $19.8\%$ and $27.4\%$ relative to the nominal ESKF; an isotropic ablation reaches $19.5\%$, linking the incremental gain to directional process-covariance redistribution. An in-sample GNSS--INS study shows that mean adaptation provides most of the accuracy gain, while DR improves consistency and mitigates over-tightened classical covariance estimates. The robust solve takes 0.05 ms for UWB and 2.92 ms for GNSS on a Jetson Orin Nano.

</details>


### [347] [Hierarchical Fast--Slow ReAct Agent for Zero-Shot Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09816)
*Zhaochen Lan,Zhi Yang,Yuxiang Fu,Mengxiang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种层次化的快-慢双系统智能体用于零样本目标导航（ZSON）。系统保留原有的值图反应式控制器，同时构建坐标锚定的语义记忆（房间类型网格、确认物体实例、带位姿关键帧），并引入基于VLM的审慎层，在受约束的推理-检索-行动循环中读取记忆进行决策。在HM3D和MP3D数据集上分别达到68.75%和47.29%的成功率，超越对比的零样本方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ZSON方法存在两个核心问题：一是值图方法每次决策都是对当前地图做argmax，证据被丢弃；二是大视觉语言模型（VLM）按固定调度从未知视角查询，不会重新审视之前经过的房间，失败调用也没有明确的回退机制。论文认为应该将机器人已经看到的内容作为审慎推理的对象。

Method: 提出层次化快-慢双系统智能体：(1) 反应式层：值图控制器每步运行，同时写入坐标锚定记忆——语义网格（房间类型和确认物体实例）和有边界的关键帧存储（带位姿标签）；(2) 审慎层：VLM筛选候选检测，在受约束的推理-检索-行动循环中，先基于文本推理，仅在文本无法区分时才检索第一人称视角；每次调用和每轮均有调用次数上限，第一层免调用解决最常见停滞问题，失败时回退到反应式控制器。

Result: 系统在HM3D val上达到68.75%成功率，在MP3D val上达到47.29%成功率，是所比较零样本方法中最高。配对实验表明，在2000个HM3D场景中选择遥远边界用argmax而非审慎推理会损失3.40个成功率百分点（95%CI [1.70, 5.05]）；而对所有边界都审慎推理也无法恢复这些损失。

Conclusion: 将记忆作为审慎推理的对象而非仅作为地图信号，能够显著提升零样本目标导航的成功率。审慎推理的价值在于"选择性"——只在关键决策点（如远距离边界选择）动用VLM和记忆，而过度审慎（对所有边界都推理）并无增益。有界调用和失败回退机制保证了系统的实用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Zero-shot object-goal navigation (ZSON) requires a robot to find a named object category in a building it has never entered. The prevailing approach scores frontiers with a vision--language \emph{value map}: every decision is another argmax over the map as it currently stands, and the evidence behind that score is discarded the moment it is taken. Systems that place a large vision--language model inside the perception--action loop typically query it on a fixed schedule from the current view alone; a room the robot walked through minutes earlier is never reconsidered, and a failed call has no defined fallback. We turn what the robot has already seen into the object of deliberation. Our hierarchical fast--slow agent leaves the value-map controller running at every step and writes a \emph{coordinate-anchored memory} as it moves: a semantic grid of room types and confirmed object instances, together with a bounded store of pose-tagged keyframes. A VLM screens each candidate detection before it is written. A deliberative layer reads this memory in a bounded reason--retrieve--act loop. It wakes on structural events the reactive layer computes, reasons first over text, and recalls a first-person view only for candidates that text alone cannot separate. Per-invocation and per-run caps bound its calls, a call-free first tier resolves the most frequent stall, and any failure returns control to the reactive controller. Our system reaches 68.75% SR on HM3D v1 \texttt{val} and 47.29% on MP3D \texttt{val}, the highest success rate among the zero-shot methods compared here. Choosing among far frontiers by argmax instead of deliberating costs 3.40 SR points in a paired comparison over all 2000 HM3D episodes (95\% CI [1.70, 5.05]); deliberating over every frontier does not recover them.

</details>


### [348] [RynnValue: Scaling Robotic Value Foundation Models with Temporal Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09853)
*Dongchi Huang,Hongyin Zhang,Bohan Hou,Siteng Huang,Zhian Su,Hang Guo,Tong Lu,Zhaofeng Xu,Jiahao Tang,Jianfei Yang,Donglin Wang,Peixi Peng,Mingxiu Chen,Deli Zhao,Xin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RynnValue，一个基于时间距离（temporal distance）作为监督信号的开源价值基础模型，用于机器人操作任务。该模型仅利用时间戳推导标签，无需偏好或进度标注，可扩展到7000+小时和约300万条指令条件片段，在基准测试和真实世界策略中均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用奖励模型正成为机器人学习扩展的瓶颈。现有方法将监督信号绑定在任务内部锚点（如偏好或归一化进度），这些锚点无法跨本体和数据源迁移，亟需一种可扩展、可跨域泛化的价值学习方案。

Method: RynnValue用时间距离（从当前观测到语言指定目标的定向代价）替代传统锚点，标签可直接从时间戳推导。训练中结合随机时间采样、时间顺序打乱和价值隔离注意力三种策略，抑制预测对失败和回归不敏感的捷径。

Result: 在RBM-EVAL-OOD基准上平均Kendall's tau_a达到0.675，超越完全偏好监督的SOTA（0.655），比仅用进度的方法（0.292）提高一倍以上；零样本泛化到未见任务、本体和视角。转换为密集奖励后，真实世界策略成功率从52.5%提升至72.5%（在线），从63.8%提升至82.5%（离线）。

Conclusion: 时间距离是可行的可扩展监督目标和实用的奖励接口，为通用机器人策略提供了一种无需偏好标注、跨领域泛化的价值学习新范式。

Abstract: General-purpose reward models are increasingly the bottleneck for scaling robot learning, yet the recipe for learning value-related capabilities from large-scale heterogeneous corpora remains underexplored. Existing approaches tie supervision to task-internal anchors such as preferences or normalized progress, none of which transfer cleanly across embodiments and data sources. We introduce RynnValue, an open-source value foundation model for robotic manipulation that replaces these anchors with temporal distance, the directed cost-to-go from an observation to the language-specified goal. Because temporal-distance labels can be derived directly from timestamps, RynnValue scales to over 7,000 hours and roughly 3M instruction-conditioned clips without preference or progress annotations. To make temporal-value learning reliable at scale, we combine random temporal sampling, temporal-order shuffling, and value-isolation attention, suppressing shortcuts that would leave predictions insensitive to failures and regressions. Trained without preference labels, RynnValue attains an average Kendall's tau_a of 0.675 on RBM-EVAL-OOD, surpassing the fully preference-supervised state of the art (0.655) and more than doubling a progress-only counterpart (0.292), while generalizing zero-shot to unseen tasks, embodiments, and viewpoints. Converted into dense rewards via potential-based shaping, it raises real-world policy success from 52.5% to 72.5% online and from 63.8% to 82.5% offline. These results establish temporal distance as a scalable supervision target and practical reward interface for generalist robot policies.

</details>


### [349] [Agentic Harnesses: LLM-Driven Verification Layers for Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09857)
*Rohan Bhagra,Mahantesh Halapannavar,Uddhav Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM驱动验证层的机器人规划安全框架，在规划和执行之间插入一个LLM-as-a-Judge集成层，通过多模型链式推理混合策略对规划动作进行可执行性评估，实现对计划的批准、拒绝或升级人工审查。实验结果表明该系统在计划验证方面达到约85%的精确度，对对抗性攻击的遏制率达到97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人自主系统研究大多聚焦于动作执行而非验证规划模型所提出动作的可行性。如同通用大语言模型，机器人规划模型存在多种风险：可能偏向用户指定目标而提出与科学伦理不符的动作、因无法"记住"先前安全风险而产生不安全行为、或容易受到对抗性攻击的侵害。因此需要在规划与执行之间建立安全验证机制。

Method: 提出一个LLM驱动的验证层，部署在规划与执行之间作为中间件。该层采用LLM-as-a-Judge集成方法：结合多个模型进行链式推理（chain-of-thought），并综合各专家评审输出，融合了混合专家（mixture of experts）与自一致性（self-consistency）策略。验证层作为中间件在规划模块输出的计划到达MCP服务器及机器人底层控制之前进行把关，将计划分类为三种处理方式：批准执行、拒绝重新规划、或升级人工审查。

Result: 该系统在批准/升级/拒绝三类分类任务上达到接近85%的精确度；对对抗性攻击的遏制率达到97%；接受与拒绝任务之间的误差极小，错误主要集中在"升级人工审查"的边界区域。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的验证中间件层能够有效提升机器人规划的伦理安全性和对抗鲁棒性，为机器人在执行前对计划进行可行性验证提供了可行方案，错误模式主要集中在需要升级人工审查的边界情形，为后续改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Advances in advanced artificial intelligence tools have sparked research in robot autonomy, but the development of such systems has largely focused on execution rather than verifying the feasibility actions planning models propose. Like general-purpose LLMs, robotics planning models carry risks: biased toward user-specified goals, they may suggest actions misaligned with scientific ethics, they may be unsafe due to an inability to "remember" prior safety risks, or they may be vulnerable to adversarial attacks on the autonomy ecosystem. We propose a LLM-driven verification layer between planning and execution to evaluate action permissibility. Our LLM-as-a-Judge ensemble combines chain-of-thought reasoning across models and synthesizes those expert judge outputs, mirroring a combination of a mixture of experts and self-consistency approach. This layer serves as middleware, gating plans from the server's planning module before they reach the MCP server and therefore the robot's low-level controls: plans are approved, rejected for reformulation, or escalated for human review. With this system, we achieve near 85% precision across accept/escalate/reject categories 97% containment of adversarial attacks, with negligible errors between accepting and rejecting tasks, and errors mostly manifesting at the escalate boundary.

</details>


### [350] [Entanglement-Free Trajectory Planning for Tethered Mobile Robots with a Slack Tether](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09860)
*Gianpietro Battocletti,Dimitris Boskos,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对松弛系绳移动机器人的运动规划算法，通过考虑系绳缠绕状态，在规划的各个阶段避免缠绕，生成动态可行且无缠绕的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在系绳移动机器人的运动规划中，系绳的缠绕状态是规划阶段的关键因素，尤其是松弛系绳场景下，系绳形状不仅由环境和障碍物决定，还受系绳动力学、机器人轨迹及外力的影响。现有方法未能在整个规划流程中充分考虑缠绕状态，可能导致规划出的轨迹不安全。

Method: 提出一个三步流水线：(i) 构建系绳机器人无缠绕配置空间的拓扑模型；(ii) 利用该模型生成一组候选路径；(iii) 通过求解同伦约束的轨迹生成问题，计算动态可行且无缠绕的轨迹。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该规划算法能够避免缠绕约束的违反，生成更安全、更可靠的轨迹。

Conclusion: 通过在规划流程的所有阶段考虑系绳缠绕状态，可以计算更安全的轨迹，避免机器人运动过程中的系绳缠绕。

Abstract: In motion planning algorithms for tethered mobile robots, the entanglement state of the tether is a critical aspect to consider during the planning phase. This is particularly important in case of a slack tether, where the shape of the tether is not determined solely by the geometry of the environment and the location of the obstacles, but also by the dynamics of the tether, by the trajectory followed by the robot, and possibly by exogenous forces. In this scenario, preventing entanglement requires planning a robot trajectory that accounts for the entanglement definition and for the dynamics of the robot and of the tether. In this work, we propose a motion planning algorithm for tethered mobile robots with a slack tether that computes dynamically feasible entanglement-free trajectories to navigate through an environment with static obstacles. By considering the entanglement state during all the stages of the planning pipeline, we are able to compute safer trajectories that avoid entanglement during the motion of the robot. We achieve this through a three-step pipeline, which includes (i) the construction of a topological model of the entanglement-free configuration space of the tethered robot, (ii) the generation of a set of candidate paths using this model, and (iii) the computation of a dynamically feasible entanglement-free trajectory by solving a homotopy-constrained trajectory generation problem. The resulting trajectory can then be executed to lead the robot to its target location, while maintaining the tether in an entanglement-free configuration. We demonstrate the benefits of this algorithm in simulations, where we show how the planning algorithm avoids violations of the entanglement constraints, resulting in safer and more reliable trajectories.

</details>


### [351] [Energy-Structured Latent World Models with Neural Time Fields for Physically Constistent Open-World Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09876)
*Yapeng Liu,Yuanzhao Zhai,Bo Ding,Huaimin Wang,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出能量结构化潜在世界模型（ELWM），将能量和动量显式嵌入潜状态以确保物理一致的因果转移，并构建物理条件神经时间场（PC-NTF）用于运动规划，在导航成功率、SPL、碰撞率和Eikonal残差等指标上均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型在学习未来表征时缺乏物理约束，物理知识隐式且不可复用，在开放世界导航中可靠性不足。需要一种能够显式嵌入物理结构、保证物理一致性的潜在世界模型。

Method: 提出ELWM，将潜状态显式构造为携带能量和动量，通过耗散端口和控制端口保证严格因果转移；在多模态RGB-D和惯性交互历史上训练，并通过Eikonal方程将ELWM集成到到达时间场，构建PC-NTF物理信息导航策略。

Result: 跨未见场景评估显示：运动预测NRMSE从0.36降至0.29；导航成功率从81.3%提升至89.7%，SPL从0.64提升至0.73；物理碰撞率从12.1%降至5.8%；Eikonal残差从0.083降至0.031。

Conclusion: 将显式物理结构嵌入潜空间能够内在弥合预测世界模型与安全、动态可行的运动规划之间的鸿沟，为可靠导航提供了新范式。

Abstract: Physically consistent motion planning remains a fundamental challenge in embodied AI, as generated trajectories must strictly conform to real-world execution dynamics. While latent world models offer a promising approach by predicting these dynamics, existing methods learn unconstrained future representations where absorbed physics remains implicit. Therefore, they fail to form reusable physical knowledge, which compromises reliability in unpredictable open-world navigation. To address this, we propose a novel Energy-Structured Latent World Model (ELWM). Our key idea is to structure the ELWM latent state to explicitly carry energy and momentum, ensuring strictly causal transitions via dissipation and control ports. Trained on multimodal RGB-D and inertial interaction histories, our model guarantees physically consistent predictions. We further implement this for motion planning by constructing Physics-Conditioned Neural Time Fields (PC-NTF), a key technical cornerstone that integrates ELWM into an arrival time field via the Eikonal equation to yield a physically-informed navigation policy. Across held-out scenes, our evaluation reveals significant improvements. Compared to generic latent models, PC-NTF reduces 0.8-s motion-prediction NRMSE from 0.36 to 0.29. Against Active Neural Time Fields, it improves navigation success from 81.3% to 89.7% and SPL from 0.64 to 0.73, while cutting the physical collision rate from 12.1% to 5.8% and the Eikonal residual from 0.083 to 0.031. Beyond these targeted gains, our results demonstrate that embedding explicit physical structures into latent spaces intrinsically bridges the gap between predictive world models and safe, dynamically feasible motion planning.

</details>


### [352] [RoSE: A Robotic Soft Esophagus for Endoprosthetic Stent Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09891)
*Dipankar Bhattacharya,Sherine Jesna V. A.,Leo K. Cheng,Weiliang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种仿生软体机器人食管（RoSE），作为体外测试平台，通过植入两种不同径向刚度特性的支架（A和B），测量支架径向力（RF）、迁移风险及吞咽效率，发现较硬支架B具有更高的径向力以减少迁移，但若发生屈曲则会显著降低吞咽效率并导致复发性吞咽困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶性食管狭窄导致的吞咽困难可通过内镜支架植入治疗，但支架迁移等晚期并发症会削弱吞咽效果。由于患者临床试验有限，支架设计和置入指南尚不完整，需要体外测试平台来研究支架径向力与迁移之间的关系。

Method: 开发了仿生软体机器人食管RoSE，植入两种不同径向刚度特性的支架（A和B），在不同蠕动速度、波长和食团浓度条件下进行实验，同时采用内镜测压法研究支架置入和功能异常对球内压力信号特征（IBPS）和吞咽效率的影响。

Result: 较硬支架B具有更高的径向力，而支架A因刚度较低维持较低的径向力分布。高径向力有助于在长时间蠕动收缩下减少支架迁移。在测压方面，支架A略微增加IBPS，但较硬支架B显著降低IBPS，尤其是高浓度食团时；如果较硬支架发生屈曲，会降低吞咽效率并导致复发性吞咽困难。

Conclusion: RoSE是一种创新的软体机器人平台，可用于内镜支架的体外测试，为解决支架评估中的临床挑战提供了有效手段。

Abstract: Soft robotic systems are well suited for developing devices for biomedical applications. A bio-mimicking robotic soft esophagus (RoSE) is developed as an in vitro testing device of endoprosthetic stents for dysphagia management. Endoprosthetic stent placement is an immediate and cost-effective therapy for dysphagia caused by malignant esophageal strictures from esophageal cancer. However, later stage complications, like stent migration, could weaken swallow efficacy in the esophagus. The stent radial force (RF) on the esophageal wall is pivotal in avoiding stent migration. Due to limited randomized controlled trials in patients, stent design and stenting guidelines remain incomplete. To address this knowledge deficit, we investigate RoSE by implanting two stents (A and B) of different radial stiffness characteristics, to measure stent RF and its effect on migration. Endoscopic manometry under peristalsis is also performed to study the impact of stenting and stent dysfunction on intra-bolus pressure signatures (IBPSs) and swallowing efficacy. Each implanted stent undergoes experiments with varied peristalsis velocity, wavelength, and bolus concentrations. The results show that stiffer stent B has a higher RF, whereas stent A maintains a lower RF profile due to lesser stiffness. High RF is necessary to minimize migration under prolonged peristaltic contractions in RoSE. For manometry, stent A slightly increases IBPS, but stiffer stent B significantly decreases IBPS, especially for higher-concentration boluses. If a stiffer stent buckles, it can reduce swallow efficacy and cause recurrent dysphagia. RoSE is therefore an innovative soft robotic platform for testing endoprosthetic stents and addressing clinical challenges in stent evaluation.

</details>


### [353] [XPolicyLab: A Unified Standard and Open Ecosystem for Robot Policy Evaluation and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09892)
*XPolicyLab Community,Tianxing Chen,Yue Chen,Tian Nian,Zijian Cai,Guangyu Chen,Wenwei Lin,Qiwei Liang,Peicheng Xiang,Kailun Su,Zixuan Li,Junyuan Tang,Yan Qin,Qiangyu Chen,Shaolong Zhu,Xiang Li,Jiahao Zhang,Weijie Wan,Baijun Chen,Honghao Su,Kehe Ye,Shujia Liu,Kaixuan Wang,Haotian Liang,Yunze Liu,Mingleyang Li,Yuran Wang,Boyu Chen,Hongzhe Bi,Shuhe Huang,Hengkai Tan,Jisong Cai,Yao Mu,Jun Guo,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Weijie Ke,Hengtao Li,Yuhang Tang,Xiaofan Li,Ganlin Yang,Zhangzheng Tu,Shuai Yang,Wenxuan Song,Pengxiang Ding,Kaidong Zhang,Yu Sun,Junliang Guo,Tong Zhang,Yixing Chen,Rongxu Cui,Zongzheng Zhang,Haoxiang Ma,Junhao Cai,Haoyu Zhang,Senqiao Yang,Jinhui Ye,Pengguang Chen,Shu Liu,Xiu Su,Wenhan Fang,Wenhao Li,Yichao Cao,Chengyao Wang,Qiang Chen,Ping Luo,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了XPolicyLab，一个统一的机器人策略评估与部署标准及开放生态系统，通过定义通用数据模式和最小化适配器接口，将N个策略与M个评估环境的集成成本从O(NM)降至O(N+M)，集成42种机器人策略，并通过实验验证了其降低集成成本的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略评估与部署因模型特定的软件依赖、数据表示和运行时接口而碎片化，连接N个策略到M个评估环境需要O(NM)次独立集成，成本高昂且效率低下。

Method: XPolicyLab指定通用的观测、动作和轨迹模式，配合最小化适配器接口（观测更新、动作预测、批量执行、回合重置），采用依赖隔离的客户端/服务器架构分离策略推理与环境执行，使两端各自保留原生软件栈并可本地或远程运行。

Result: 生态集成了42种机器人策略并标准化安装、调试、服务和评估流程。受控实验中，符合标准使代表性策略的集成时间从5小时以上降至2小时，打包技能进一步降至30分钟。同一适配器通过单一接口服务RoboTwin、RoboDojo仿真和标准化真实机器人评估。

Conclusion: XPolicyLab作为共享基础设施发布，用于可复现的策略比较和跨仿真与物理平台的标准化部署，验证了将异构性约束在策略端可大幅降低集成成本。

Abstract: Robot policy evaluation and deployment remain fragmented by model-specific software dependencies, data representations, and runtime interfaces, so that connecting N policies to M evaluation environments requires O(NM) separate integrations. We present XPolicyLab, a unified standard and open ecosystem that reduces this cost to O(N+M). XPolicyLab specifies common observation, action, and trajectory schemas together with a minimal adapter interface for observation updates, action prediction, batched execution, and episode reset, while a dependency-isolated client/server architecture separates policy inference from environment execution, so that each side retains its native software stack and may run locally or remotely. The ecosystem integrates 42 robot policies and standardizes their installation, debugging, serving, and evaluation workflows. Across these adapters, model-specific code varies by an order of magnitude while the environment-facing loop stays within a few lines of a fixed reference, confirming that the contract confines heterogeneity to the policy side. In a controlled study, conforming to the standard reduces the integration effort of a representative policy from over five hours to two hours, and packaged agent skills reduce it further to thirty minutes. The same adapters serve RoboTwin, RoboDojo simulation, and standardized real-robot evaluation through one interface. XPolicyLab is released as shared infrastructure for reproducible policy comparison and standardized deployment across simulation and physical platforms. Project website: https://xpolicylab.github.io/.

</details>
